========== FILE: E:\Code\Vyte\orbyteflow\src\App.tsx ==========

// src/App.tsx
import { useState, useEffect, useCallback } from 'react';
import type {
  ActiveTab, AuditLog, BusinessComplianceProfile, CurrencyCode, DocumentVaultItem,
  ExtractedNlpOrderItem, InvoiceItem, NewProductForm, OrbyteHrOverdueEscalationPayload,
  OrbyteHrStaff, Product, QCAlert, RenewalTask, UserRole, UserSession,
} from './types/vyte.ts';
import { CURRENCY_SYMBOLS } from './types/vyte.ts';
import {
  INITIAL_AUDIT_LOGS, INITIAL_COMPLIANCE_PROFILES, INITIAL_INVOICES,
  INITIAL_PRODUCTS, INITIAL_QC_ALERTS, INITIAL_RENEWAL_TASKS, INITIAL_VAULT_DOCS,
} from './data/mockData';
import { Sidebar } from './components/layout/Sidebar';
import { HeaderBar } from './components/layout/HeaderBar';
import { AuditLogPanel } from './components/common/AuditLogPanel';
import { RestockModule } from './modules/module1-restock/RestockModule';
import { AddProductModal } from './modules/module1-restock/AddProductModal';
import { QualityControlModule } from './modules/module4-quality/QualityControlModule';
import { InvoiceModule } from './modules/module5-invoice/InvoiceModule';
import { ComplianceRenewalModule } from './modules/module6-compliance/ComplianceRenewalModule';
import { SalesAnalyticsModule } from './modules/module3-sales/SalesAnalyticsModule.tsx';
import { OrderStatusModule } from './modules/module2-order/OrderStatusModule';
import { SplashScreen } from './components/SplashScreen';
import { LoadingScreen } from './components/LoadingScreen';
import { LoginPage } from './components/auth/LoginPage';
import { orbyteApi, getDebugMockMode, setDebugMockMode } from './services/api';
import { UniversalSystemMigratorModal } from './components/migration/UniversalSystemMigratorModal';

const SESSION_KEY = 'vyte_active_session_v1';
const FALLBACK_RATES: Record<CurrencyCode, number> = { MYR: 1.0, SGD: 0.313, USD: 0.244 };

const stripModPrefix = (t: string) =>
    t.replace(/\bMod(?:ule)?\s*[1-5]\s*[:\-]?\s*/gi, '').replace(/\bin\s+Mod(?:ule)?\s*[1-5]\b/gi, '').trim();

const formatDisplayDate = () =>
    new Date().toLocaleDateString('en-GB', { day: '2-digit', month: 'short', year: 'numeric' });

function usePersistedState<T>(key: string, fallback: T, transform?: (val: T) => T) {
  const [state, setState] = useState<T>(() => {
    try {
      const raw = localStorage.getItem(key);
      if (!raw) return fallback;
      const parsed = JSON.parse(raw) as T;
      return transform ? transform(parsed) : parsed;
    } catch {
      return fallback;
    }
  });
  useEffect(() => {
    localStorage.setItem(key, JSON.stringify(state));
  }, [key, state]);
  return [state, setState] as const;
}

export default function App() {
  const [userSession, setUserSession] = useState<UserSession | null>(null);
  const [splashDone, setSplashDone] = useState(false);
  const [isValidatingRefreshSession, setIsValidatingRefreshSession] = useState(() => Boolean(localStorage.getItem(SESSION_KEY)));
  const [toasts, setToasts] = useState<{ id: string; type: 'success' | 'error' | 'warning' | 'info'; message: string }[]>([]);
  const [role, setRole] = useState<UserRole>('SME_OWNER');
  const [debugMockMode, setDebugMockModeState] = useState<boolean>(getDebugMockMode);

  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = usePersistedState<ActiveTab>('vyte_state_active_tab_v2', 'mod1');
  const [inventorySubTab, setInventorySubTab] = usePersistedState<'restock' | 'orders'>('vyte_state_subtab_v2', 'restock');
  const [industryFilter, setIndustryFilter] = usePersistedState<string>('vyte_state_industry_filter_v2', 'ALL');
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = usePersistedState<string>('vyte_state_global_search_v2', '');

  const [currency, setCurrency] = useState<CurrencyCode>(() => {
    const s = localStorage.getItem('vyte_currency');
    return s && s in CURRENCY_SYMBOLS ? (s as CurrencyCode) : 'MYR';
  });
  const [exchangeRates, setExchangeRates] = usePersistedState<Record<CurrencyCode, number>>('vyte_live_fx_rates_v2', FALLBACK_RATES);
  const [rateSourceLabel, setRateSourceLabel] = useState('Syncing FX rates...');
  const [loadingOverlayMessage, setLoadingOverlayMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [showAddModal, setShowAddModal] = useState(false);
  const [showMigratorModal, setShowMigratorModal] = useState(false);
  const [pendingCustomerOrderCount, setPendingCustomerOrderCount] = useState(1);
  const [deductedOrderIds, setDeductedOrderIds] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>({});

  const [products, setProducts] = usePersistedState<Product[]>('vyte_state_products_v2', INITIAL_PRODUCTS);
  const [qcAlerts, setQcAlerts] = usePersistedState<QCAlert[]>('vyte_state_qc_v2', INITIAL_QC_ALERTS);
  const [invoices, setInvoices] = usePersistedState<InvoiceItem[]>('vyte_state_invoices_v2', INITIAL_INVOICES, (list) =>
      list.filter((i) => !i.invNo.startsWith('CLM-')).map((i) => ({ ...i, aiCategory: stripModPrefix(i.aiCategory) }))
  );
  const [auditLogs, setAuditLogs] = usePersistedState<AuditLog[]>('vyte_state_logs_v2', INITIAL_AUDIT_LOGS, (list) =>
      list.map((l) => ({ ...l, module: stripModPrefix(l.module) || 'System', detail: stripModPrefix(l.detail) }))
  );
  const [complianceProfiles, setComplianceProfiles] = usePersistedState<BusinessComplianceProfile[]>('vyte_state_compliance_profiles_v1', INITIAL_COMPLIANCE_PROFILES);
  const [vaultDocs, setVaultDocs] = usePersistedState<DocumentVaultItem[]>('vyte_state_vault_docs_v1', INITIAL_VAULT_DOCS);
  const [renewalTasks, setRenewalTasks] = usePersistedState<RenewalTask[]>('vyte_state_renewal_tasks_v1', INITIAL_RENEWAL_TASKS);

  const canApprove = role === 'SME_OWNER' || role === 'MANAGER';
  const activeExchangeRate = exchangeRates[currency] ?? 1.0;

  const pushToast = useCallback((type: 'success' | 'error' | 'warning' | 'info', message: string) => {
    const id = `T-${Date.now()}-${Math.random()}`;
    setToasts((p) => [...p, { id, type, message }]);
    setTimeout(() => setToasts((p) => p.filter((t) => t.id !== id)), 4200);
  }, []);

  const syncWithBackend = useCallback(async (isDebug: boolean, showNotification = false) => {
    if (isDebug) {
      setProducts(INITIAL_PRODUCTS);
      setQcAlerts(INITIAL_QC_ALERTS);
      setInvoices(INITIAL_INVOICES);
      setAuditLogs(INITIAL_AUDIT_LOGS);
      setComplianceProfiles(INITIAL_COMPLIANCE_PROFILES);
      setVaultDocs(INITIAL_VAULT_DOCS);
      setRenewalTasks(INITIAL_RENEWAL_TASKS);
      if (showNotification) pushToast('info', 'Debug Mode enabled: restored local mock dataset.');
      return;
    }
    setProducts([]);
    setQcAlerts([]);
    setInvoices([]);
    setAuditLogs([]);
    setComplianceProfiles([]);
    setVaultDocs([]);
    setRenewalTasks([]);
    try {
      const data = await orbyteApi.fetchWorkspaceData();
      setProducts(data.products);
      setQcAlerts(data.qcAlerts);
      setInvoices(data.invoices);
      setAuditLogs(data.auditLogs);
      if (showNotification) pushToast('success', `Synced ${data.products.length} products from live backend.`);
    } catch {
      if (showNotification) pushToast('error', 'Cannot reach backend server. Workspace cleared.');
    }
  }, [pushToast, setAuditLogs, setComplianceProfiles, setInvoices, setProducts, setQcAlerts, setRenewalTasks, setVaultDocs]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (userSession && !debugMockMode) void syncWithBackend(false, false);
  }, [userSession, debugMockMode, syncWithBackend]);

  const handleToggleDebugMode = (enabled: boolean) => {
    setDebugMockMode(enabled);
    setDebugMockModeState(enabled);
    void syncWithBackend(enabled, true);
  };

  const fetchLiveExchangeRates = useCallback(async (notifyUser = false) => {
    const providers = [
      { url: 'https://open.er-api.com/v6/latest/MYR', name: 'ExchangeRate-API', parse: (d: any) => d?.rates?.SGD && d?.rates?.USD ? { SGD: +d.rates.SGD, USD: +d.rates.USD } : null },
      { url: 'https://api.frankfurter.dev/v1/latest?base=MYR&symbols=SGD,USD', name: 'ECB Frankfurter', parse: (d: any) => d?.rates?.SGD && d?.rates?.USD ? { SGD: +d.rates.SGD, USD: +d.rates.USD } : null },
      { url: 'https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/@fawazahmed0/currency-api@latest/v1/currencies/myr.json', name: 'Forex CDN', parse: (d: any) => d?.myr?.sgd && d?.myr?.usd ? { SGD: +d.myr.sgd, USD: +d.myr.usd } : null },
    ];
    for (const p of providers) {
      try {
        const res = await fetch(p.url);
        if (!res.ok) continue;
        const parsed = p.parse(await res.json());
        if (parsed) {
          const next: Record<CurrencyCode, number> = { MYR: 1.0, SGD: parsed.SGD, USD: parsed.USD };
          setExchangeRates(next);
          setRateSourceLabel(`${p.name} \u2022 1 RM = ${next.SGD.toFixed(3)} SGD | ${next.USD.toFixed(3)} USD`);
          if (notifyUser) pushToast('success', `Updated FX rates: 1 MYR = ${next.SGD.toFixed(3)} SGD / ${next.USD.toFixed(3)} USD`);
          return;
        }
      } catch { /* fallback to next */ }
    }
    setRateSourceLabel('Cached FX Rate');
  }, [pushToast, setExchangeRates]);

  useEffect(() => { void fetchLiveExchangeRates(false); }, [fetchLiveExchangeRates]);

  const handleCurrencyChange = (newCurrency: CurrencyCode) => {
    setCurrency(newCurrency);
    localStorage.setItem('vyte_currency', newCurrency);
    pushToast('success', `Currency set to ${newCurrency} (${CURRENCY_SYMBOLS[newCurrency]}) \u2022 1 MYR = ${(exchangeRates[newCurrency] ?? 1).toFixed(3)} ${newCurrency}`);
  };

  const handleTriggerLoadingScreen = useCallback((message: string, durationMs = 900) => {
    setLoadingOverlayMessage(message);
    setTimeout(() => setLoadingOverlayMessage(null), durationMs);
  }, []);

  const addAuditLog = useCallback((module: string, detail: string, overrideRole?: UserRole) => {
    const time = new Date().toLocaleTimeString('en-GB', { hour12: false });
    setAuditLogs((p) => [{ id: `L-${Date.now()}-${Math.random()}`, time, module: stripModPrefix(module) || 'System', role: overrideRole ?? role, detail: stripModPrefix(detail) }, ...p]);
  }, [role, setAuditLogs]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(SESSION_KEY);
    if (!raw) { setIsValidatingRefreshSession(false); return; }
    let mounted = true;
    (async () => {
      try {
        const parsed = JSON.parse(raw) as { session: UserSession; accessToken: string };
        if (!parsed?.session?.userID || !parsed?.accessToken) throw new Error();
        const [res] = await Promise.all([
          orbyteApi.validateSession({ accessToken: parsed.accessToken, userID: parsed.session.userID, email: parsed.session.email }, parsed.session),
          new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 450)),
        ]);
        if (mounted && res.data.valid) {
          setUserSession(res.data.session);
          setRole(res.data.session.role);
          setSplashDone(true);
        } else localStorage.removeItem(SESSION_KEY);
      } catch {
        localStorage.removeItem(SESSION_KEY);
      } finally {
        if (mounted) setIsValidatingRefreshSession(false);
      }
    })();
    return () => { mounted = false; };
  }, []);

  const handleAuthenticated = (session: UserSession, accessToken = `vyte-jwt-${Date.now()}`) => {
    localStorage.setItem(SESSION_KEY, JSON.stringify({ session, accessToken }));
    setUserSession(session);
    setRole(session.role);
    setSplashDone(false);
    addAuditLog('Auth', `Signed in as ${session.userName} (${session.userID}) \u2022 Role: ${session.userType} \u2022 ${session.companyName}.`, session.role);
  };

  const handleLogout = () => {
    localStorage.removeItem(SESSION_KEY);
    setUserSession(null);
    setSplashDone(false);
  };

  const handleNavigateFromNotification = (targetTab: ActiveTab, subTab?: 'restock' | 'orders') => {
    if (targetTab === 'mod3') { setActiveTab('mod1'); setInventorySubTab('orders'); return; }
    setActiveTab(targetTab);
    if (subTab) setInventorySubTab(subTab);
  };

  const handleApproveAndContactSupplier = async (productId: string, customReorderQty?: number) => {
    if (!canApprove) return pushToast('error', 'Access denied: Only Owner or Manager accounts can approve purchase orders.');
    const target = products.find((p) => p.id === productId);
    if (!target) return;
    const orderQty = customReorderQty && customReorderQty > 0 ? customReorderQty : target.reorderQty;
    const poNumber = `PO-2026-${Math.floor(1000 + Math.random() * 9000)}`;

    await orbyteApi.contactSupplier({
      poNumber, productId: target.id, supplierName: target.supplierName, supplierPhoneNumber: target.supplierContact,
      channel: 'WHATSAPP', messageBody: `Vyte PO ${poNumber}: Please supply ${orderQty} ${target.uom} of ${target.name} (${target.sku}).`,
      userID: userSession?.userID || 'M067', role,
    });

    setProducts((p) => p.map((x) => (x.id === productId ? { ...x, reorderQty: orderQty, restockStatus: 'SUPPLIER_CONTACTED' } : x)));
    const unitCost = target.unitCostRM ?? Number((target.priceRM * 0.62).toFixed(2));
    const poTotalRM = Number((unitCost * orderQty).toFixed(2));
    const poInvoiceNo = `SUP-${poNumber}`;

    if (debugMockMode) {
      setInvoices((p) => [{
        id: `INV-${Date.now()}`, invNo: poInvoiceNo, type: 'SUPPLIER_PAYABLE', party: target.supplierName,
        date: formatDisplayDate(), amountRM: poTotalRM, aiCategory: `Inventory Procurement (${orderQty} ${target.uom} ${target.name})`,
        aiConfidence: 98, paid: false, status: 'NEEDS_REVIEW',
      }, ...p]);
    }
    addAuditLog('Restock', `Approved PO ${poNumber} (+${orderQty} ${target.uom} ${target.name}) to ${target.supplierName}.`);
    pushToast('success', `PO ${poNumber} dispatched to ${target.supplierName}.`);
  };

  const handleConfirmSupplierDelivery = async (productId: string) => {
    const target = products.find((p) => p.id === productId);
    if (!target) return;
    const res = await orbyteApi.confirmRestock({
      poNumber: `PO-${target.sku}`, productId: target.id, SKU: target.sku, quantity: target.reorderQty,
      unitPrice: target.unitCostRM ?? target.priceRM * 0.62, currency: 'MYR', MOQ: target.moq ?? 10,
      unitOfMeasure: target.uom, supplierName: target.supplierName, supplierPhoneNumber: target.supplierContact,
      requestedDeliveryDate: new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10), deliveryLocation: 'Main Warehouse',
      automationStatus: true, userID: userSession?.userID || 'M067',
    });

    if (res.data.backendProduct) {
      setProducts((p) => p.map((x) => (x.id === productId ? res.data.backendProduct! : x)));
      if (res.data.backendInvoice) {
        setInvoices((p) => [res.data.backendInvoice!, ...p.filter((i) => i.id !== res.data.backendInvoice!.id)]);
      }
    } else {
      const newStock = target.stock + target.reorderQty;
      setProducts((p) => p.map((x) => x.id === productId ? { ...x, stock: newStock, restockStatus: newStock <= x.minThreshold ? 'TRIGGERED_PENDING_APPROVAL' : 'CONFIRMED' } : x));
    }
    addAuditLog('Delivery', `Received +${target.reorderQty} ${target.uom} of ${target.name} from ${target.supplierName}.`);
    pushToast('success', `Confirmed delivery of +${target.reorderQty} ${target.uom} ${target.name}.`);
  };

  const handleRecordStockUsage = async (productId: string, usedQty: number) => {
    const target = products.find((p) => p.id === productId);
    if (!target || target.stock <= 0 || usedQty <= 0) return;
    const actual = Math.min(usedQty, target.stock);
    const saleAmountRM = Number((actual * target.priceRM).toFixed(2));
    const posInvoiceNo = `SALE-2026-${Math.floor(1000 + Math.random() * 9000)}`;

    const updatedProd = await orbyteApi.recordStockMovement(productId, -actual, 'SALE', `Counter Sale (${posInvoiceNo})`, userSession?.userID || 'M067').catch(() => null);
    if (updatedProd) {
      setProducts((p) => p.map((x) => (x.id === productId ? updatedProd : x)));
    } else {
      const newStock = Math.max(0, target.stock - actual);
      setProducts((p) => p.map((x) => x.id === productId ? { ...x, stock: newStock, restockStatus: newStock <= x.minThreshold && x.restockStatus !== 'SUPPLIER_CONTACTED' ? 'TRIGGERED_PENDING_APPROVAL' : x.restockStatus } : x));
    }

    const backendInv = await orbyteApi.createBackendInvoice({
      type: 'CUSTOMER_RECEIVABLE', party: `Direct Sale (${target.sku})`, amountRM: saleAmountRM,
      invNo: posInvoiceNo, aiCategory: `Product Sales (${actual} ${target.uom} ${target.name})`, sourceRef: target.id,
    });
    setInvoices((p) => [backendInv ?? {
      id: `INV-${Date.now()}`, invNo: posInvoiceNo, type: 'CUSTOMER_RECEIVABLE', party: `Direct Sale (${target.sku})`,
      date: formatDisplayDate(), amountRM: saleAmountRM, aiCategory: `Product Sales (${actual} ${target.uom} ${target.name})`,
      aiConfidence: 99, paid: true, status: 'NEEDS_REVIEW',
    }, ...p]);

    addAuditLog('Dispatch', `Dispatched ${actual} ${target.uom} of ${target.name} (${posInvoiceNo}).`);
    pushToast('success', `Recorded sale of ${actual} ${target.uom} ${target.name} (${posInvoiceNo}).`);
  };

  const handleSaveProduct = async (newProd: NewProductForm) => {
    const basePriceRM = Number((Number(newProd.priceRM) / activeExchangeRate).toFixed(2));
    const backendCreated = await orbyteApi.createProduct({ ...newProd, priceRM: basePriceRM }).catch(() => null);
    if (backendCreated) {
      setProducts((p) => [backendCreated, ...p]);
      addAuditLog('Catalog', `Added SKU "${backendCreated.name}" (${backendCreated.stock} ${backendCreated.uom}).`);
      setShowAddModal(false);
      pushToast('success', `Added ${backendCreated.name} (${backendCreated.sku}).`);
      return;
    }
    const isLow = Number(newProd.stock) <= Number(newProd.minThreshold);
    const parts = newProd.extraAttr?.split(/[\u2022|]/) || [];
    const created: Product = {
      id: `P-${Date.now()}-${Math.floor(Math.random() * 1000)}`,
      sku: newProd.sku || `SKU-${Math.floor(1000 + Math.random() * 9000)}`,
      name: newProd.name, category: newProd.category, industry: newProd.industry, uom: newProd.uom,
      stock: Number(newProd.stock), minThreshold: Number(newProd.minThreshold), reorderQty: Number(newProd.reorderQty),
      priceRM: basePriceRM, unitCostRM: Number((basePriceRM * 0.6).toFixed(2)), dailyDepletionRate: 3.0, leadTimeDays: 2, moq: 10,
      supplierName: newProd.supplierName || 'Preferred Supplier', supplierContact: newProd.supplierContact || '+6012-9998888',
      performance: 'Good', visibility: true,
      batchNo: newProd.industry === 'FNB_PERISHABLE' ? parts[0]?.replace('Batch:', '').trim() || 'B-2026-NEW' : undefined,
      expiryDate: newProd.industry === 'FNB_PERISHABLE' ? parts[1]?.replace('Exp:', '').trim() || '2026-11-15' : undefined,
      variantSpec: newProd.industry !== 'FNB_PERISHABLE' ? newProd.extraAttr : undefined,
      photoUrl: newProd.photoUrl, restockStatus: isLow ? 'TRIGGERED_PENDING_APPROVAL' : 'HEALTHY',
    };
    setProducts((p) => [created, ...p]);
    addAuditLog('Catalog', `Added SKU "${created.name}" (${created.stock} ${created.uom}).`);
    setShowAddModal(false);
    pushToast('success', `Added ${created.name} (${created.sku}).`);
  };

  const handleBatchImportProducts = (importedList: Product[]) => {
    if (!importedList.length) return;
    setProducts((p) => [...importedList, ...p]);
    addAuditLog('Import', `Batch-imported ${importedList.length} SKU(s) from file.`);
    pushToast('success', `Imported ${importedList.length} products.`);
  };

  const handleQueueRestockToOrderModule = (product: Product, recommendedQty: number, aiReason: string) => {
    setProducts((p) => p.map((x) => (x.id === product.id ? { ...x, reorderQty: recommendedQty, restockStatus: 'TRIGGERED_PENDING_APPROVAL' } : x)));
    addAuditLog('Forecast', `${aiReason} -> Updated ${product.name} reorder qty to +${recommendedQty} ${product.uom}.`);
    setActiveTab('mod1');
    setInventorySubTab('restock');
    pushToast('info', `Restock quantity updated for ${product.name}.`);
  };

  const handleTriggerShortageRestock = useCallback((shortageItems: { productID: string; neededQty: number }[]) => {
    if (!shortageItems.length) return;
    setProducts((p) => p.map((x) => {
      const m = shortageItems.find((s) => s.productID === x.id);
      return m ? { ...x, reorderQty: Math.max(x.reorderQty, m.neededQty + x.minThreshold), restockStatus: x.restockStatus === 'SUPPLIER_CONTACTED' ? 'SUPPLIER_CONTACTED' : 'TRIGGERED_PENDING_APPROVAL' } : x;
    }));
  }, [setProducts]);

  const handleApproveCustomerOrder = (items: ExtractedNlpOrderItem[], customerName: string, orderId: string, invoiceId: string, inventoryDeducted = true) => {
    if (inventoryDeducted && !deductedOrderIds[orderId]) {
      setDeductedOrderIds((p) => ({ ...p, [orderId]: true }));
      setProducts((p) => p.map((x) => {
        const line = items.find((i) => i.productID === x.id);
        if (!line) return x;
        const nextStock = Math.max(0, x.stock - line.requestedQty);
        return { ...x, stock: nextStock, restockStatus: nextStock <= x.minThreshold && x.restockStatus !== 'SUPPLIER_CONTACTED' ? 'TRIGGERED_PENDING_APPROVAL' : x.restockStatus };
      }));
    }
    const totalOrderRM = items.reduce((s, i) => s + i.lineTotal, 0);
    setInvoices((p) => [{
      id: `INV-${Date.now()}`, invNo: invoiceId, type: 'CUSTOMER_RECEIVABLE', party: customerName,
      date: formatDisplayDate(), amountRM: totalOrderRM,
      aiCategory: `Customer Order (${items.map((i) => `${i.requestedQty} ${i.name}`).join(', ')})`,
      aiConfidence: 97, paid: false, status: 'NEEDS_REVIEW',
    }, ...p]);
    addAuditLog('Orders', `Approved order ${orderId} for ${customerName} (Invoice ${invoiceId}).`);
  };

  const handleDispatchQCToHrStaff = (qcId: string, staff: OrbyteHrStaff, priority: 'URGENT' | 'HIGH' | 'NORMAL', dueDate: string, hrNotes: string) => {
    if (!canApprove) return pushToast('error', 'Access denied: Only Owner or Manager accounts can assign QC tasks.');
    const targetQc = qcAlerts.find((q) => q.id === qcId);
    if (!targetQc) return;
    const hrTaskId = `HR-TASK-${Math.floor(1000 + Math.random() * 9000)}`;
    setQcAlerts((p) => p.map((q) => q.id === qcId ? {
      ...q, status: 'ASSIGNED_TO_STAFF', hrTaskId, assignedStaffId: staff.staffId, assignedStaffName: staff.name,
      assignedStaffDepartment: staff.department, assignedStaffPosition: staff.position,
      assignedStaffRole: `${staff.position} (${staff.department})`, assignedPriority: priority,
      assignedDueDate: dueDate, hrNotes: hrNotes || q.aiSuggestion, assignedAt: new Date().toLocaleTimeString('en-GB', { hour12: false }),
      hrEscalated: false, hrEscalationPayload: undefined,
    } : q));
    addAuditLog('Quality Control', `Assigned task ${hrTaskId} (${targetQc.productName}) to ${staff.name}.`);
    pushToast('success', `Task ${hrTaskId} assigned to ${staff.name} (Due: ${dueDate}).`);
  };

  const handleEscalateOverdueToHr = useCallback((qcId: string, payload: OrbyteHrOverdueEscalationPayload, simulatedPastDate?: string) => {
    setQcAlerts((p) => p.map((q) => q.id === qcId ? { ...q, assignedDueDate: simulatedPastDate ?? q.assignedDueDate, hrEscalated: true, hrEscalationPayload: payload } : q));
    addAuditLog('HR Escalation', `Overdue task ${payload.hrTaskId} (${payload.staff.name}) escalated.`);
    pushToast('warning', `Overdue task ${payload.hrTaskId} escalated to HR.`);
  }, [addAuditLog, pushToast, setQcAlerts]);

  const handleCompleteHrQcTask = async (qcId: string) => {
    const targetQc = qcAlerts.find((q) => q.id === qcId);
    if (!targetQc) return;
    await orbyteApi.approveQcAlert(qcId, userSession?.userID || 'M067');
    const todayIso = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);
    const isLate = Boolean(targetQc.hrEscalated || (targetQc.assignedDueDate && targetQc.assignedDueDate < todayIso));
    setQcAlerts((p) => p.map((q) => q.id === qcId ? { ...q, status: 'APPROVED_RESOLVED', completedAtDate: todayIso, completedLate: isLate } : q));
    addAuditLog('Quality Control', `${targetQc.assignedStaffName || 'Staff'} completed QC task for ${targetQc.productName}.`);
    pushToast(isLate ? 'warning' : 'success', `Task ${targetQc.hrTaskId || targetQc.batchNo} marked complete.`);
  };

  const handleTriggerQCScan = async () => {
    const backendScan = await orbyteApi.triggerQcScan();
    if (backendScan) {
      if (backendScan.newAlerts.length > 0) {
        setQcAlerts((p) => [...backendScan.newAlerts, ...p]);
        pushToast('warning', `Expiry scan found ${backendScan.newAlerts.length} new at-risk batch(es).`);
      } else {
        pushToast('info', `Scanned ${backendScan.scannedCount} batches. No new risks found.`);
      }
      return;
    }
    const existingSkus = new Set(qcAlerts.map((q) => q.sku));
    const newlyDetected: QCAlert[] = products
        .filter((p) => p.industry === 'FNB_PERISHABLE' && !existingSkus.has(p.sku))
        .map((p) => ({
          id: `QC-${Date.now()}-${p.id}`, productName: p.name, sku: p.sku, batchNo: p.batchNo || 'B-2026-SCAN',
          expiryDate: p.expiryDate || '2026-10-26', daysLeft: 4, qtyAffected: `${p.stock} ${p.uom}`,
          triggerReason: 'Shelf-life threshold (< 5 days remaining)',
          aiSuggestion: `Bundle ${p.name} with morning combo deals (-20%) & relabel ${p.stock} ${p.uom}.`,
          actionLabel: 'Approve Promo & Relabel', savedRM: Number((p.stock * p.priceRM * 0.75).toFixed(2)), status: 'PENDING_APPROVAL',
        }));
    if (newlyDetected.length > 0) {
      setQcAlerts((p) => [...newlyDetected, ...p]);
      addAuditLog('Quality Scan', `Detected ${newlyDetected.length} new at-risk batch(es).`);
      pushToast('warning', `Found ${newlyDetected.length} new at-risk batch(es).`);
    } else pushToast('info', 'Batch scan complete. All perishable items are within safe dates.');
  };

  const handleVerifyInvoice = async (invId: string, updatedCategory?: string) => {
    if (!canApprove) return pushToast('error', 'Access denied: Only Owner or Manager accounts can verify invoices.');
    const target = invoices.find((i) => i.id === invId);
    if (!target) return;
    await orbyteApi.verifyBackendInvoice(invId, userSession?.userID || 'M067', updatedCategory);
    setInvoices((p) => p.map((i) => (i.id === invId ? { ...i, aiCategory: updatedCategory ?? i.aiCategory, status: 'VERIFIED' } : i)));
    addAuditLog('Invoices', `Verified invoice ${target.invNo} (${target.party}).`);
  };

  const handleToggleInvoicePaid = async (invId: string) => {
    const target = invoices.find((i) => i.id === invId);
    if (!target) return;
    const nextPaid = !target.paid;
    await orbyteApi.toggleBackendInvoicePaid(invId, nextPaid, userSession?.userID || 'M067');
    setInvoices((p) => p.map((i) => (i.id === invId ? { ...i, paid: nextPaid } : i)));
    addAuditLog('Invoices', `Marked invoice ${target.invNo} as ${nextPaid ? 'PAID' : 'UNPAID'}.`);
  };

  const handleAddValidatedInvoice = async (createdInv: InvoiceItem) => {
    const backendInv = await orbyteApi.createBackendInvoice({
      type: createdInv.type, party: createdInv.party, amountRM: createdInv.amountRM,
      invNo: createdInv.invNo, aiCategory: createdInv.aiCategory,
    });
    setInvoices((p) => [backendInv ?? createdInv, ...p]);
    addAuditLog('Invoices', `Added invoice ${createdInv.invNo} (${createdInv.aiCategory}).`);
    pushToast('success', `Saved invoice ${createdInv.invNo}.`);
  };

  if (isValidatingRefreshSession) return <LoadingScreen isOpen={true} message="Restoring workspace session..." />;
  if (!userSession) return <LoginPage onAuthenticated={handleAuthenticated} />;
  if (!splashDone) return <SplashScreen onComplete={() => setSplashDone(true)} />;

  const pendingRestockCount = products.filter((p) => p.restockStatus === 'TRIGGERED_PENDING_APPROVAL').length;
  const pendingQcCount = qcAlerts.filter((q) => q.status !== 'APPROVED_RESOLVED').length;
  const pendingInvoiceCount = invoices.filter((i) => i.status === 'NEEDS_REVIEW').length;
  const pendingComplianceCount = complianceProfiles.filter((p) => p.complianceStatus === 'EXPIRING_SOON' || p.complianceStatus === 'EXPIRED').length;
  const q = searchQuery.trim().toLowerCase();
  const filteredProducts = products.filter((p) => (industryFilter === 'ALL' || p.industry === industryFilter) && (!q || p.name.toLowerCase().includes(q) || p.sku.toLowerCase().includes(q) || p.category.toLowerCase().includes(q)));

  return (
      <div className="h-screen overflow-hidden bg-[#F6F7F4] flex p-3 gap-3 text-slate-900 font-sans">
        <Sidebar
            activeTab={activeTab} setActiveTab={setActiveTab} role={role}
            operatingCurrency={currency} onCurrencyChange={handleCurrencyChange}
            searchQuery={searchQuery} setSearchQuery={setSearchQuery}
            pendingRestockCount={pendingRestockCount} pendingOrderCount={pendingCustomerOrderCount}
            pendingQcCount={pendingQcCount} pendingInvoiceCount={pendingInvoiceCount}
            pendingComplianceCount={pendingComplianceCount} auditLogCount={auditLogs.length}
            totalProductCount={products.length} userSession={userSession} onLogout={handleLogout}
            debugMockMode={debugMockMode} onToggleDebugMockMode={handleToggleDebugMode}
            onOpenMigratorModal={() => setShowMigratorModal(true)}
        />

        <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col min-w-0 h-full overflow-hidden gap-3">
          <HeaderBar
              activeTab={activeTab}
              inventorySubTab={inventorySubTab}
              userSession={userSession}
              currency={currency}
              exchangeRate={activeExchangeRate}
              rateSourceLabel={rateSourceLabel}
              onRefreshRates={() => void fetchLiveExchangeRates(true)}
              totalSkus={products.length}
              pendingRestocks={pendingRestockCount}
              pendingOrders={pendingCustomerOrderCount}
              pendingQcBatches={pendingQcCount}
              totalInvoices={invoices.length}
              pendingInvoices={pendingInvoiceCount}
              totalComplianceProfiles={complianceProfiles.length}
              pendingCompliance={pendingComplianceCount}
              totalVaultDocs={vaultDocs.length}
              totalRenewalTasks={renewalTasks.length}
              onNavigateFromNotification={handleNavigateFromNotification}
          />

          <main className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto pr-1 space-y-4">
            {(activeTab === 'mod1' || activeTab === 'mod3') && (
                <div className="space-y-3.5">
                  <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
                    <div className="inline-flex items-center bg-slate-200/80 p-1 rounded-xl border border-slate-300/70 text-xs font-semibold">
                      {(['restock', 'orders'] as const).map((sub) => (
                          <button
                              key={sub} type="button" onClick={() => { setActiveTab('mod1'); setInventorySubTab(sub); }}
                              className={`px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg transition cursor-pointer ${inventorySubTab === sub ? 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-900 font-bold shadow-2xs' : 'text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-900'}`}
                          >
                            {sub === 'restock' ? `Inventory & Restock (${pendingRestockCount} Pending)` : `Customer Orders (${pendingCustomerOrderCount})`}
                          </button>
                      ))}
                    </div>

                    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5 bg-white px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-200/90 shadow-2xs text-xs">
                      <span className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-400 px-1.5">Industry Filter:</span>
                      {[
                        { id: 'ALL', label: 'All Items', activeCls: 'bg-slate-900 text-white font-bold', dot: null },
                        { id: 'FNB_PERISHABLE', label: 'F&B / Perishable', activeCls: 'bg-rose-100 text-rose-800 font-bold border border-rose-200', dot: 'bg-rose-500' },
                        { id: 'RETAIL_VARIANT', label: 'Retail & Variants', activeCls: 'bg-blue-100 text-blue-800 font-bold border border-blue-200', dot: 'bg-blue-500' },
                        { id: 'B2B_RAW_MATERIAL', label: 'B2B & Packaging', activeCls: 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-900 font-bold border border-emerald-200', dot: 'bg-emerald-500' },
                      ].map((f) => (
                          <button
                              key={f.id} type="button" onClick={() => setIndustryFilter(f.id)}
                              className={`px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg transition cursor-pointer flex items-center gap-1.5 ${industryFilter === f.id ? f.activeCls : 'text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-100 font-medium'}`}
                          >
                            {f.dot && <span className={`w-2 h-2 rounded-full ${f.dot}`} />}
                            <span>{f.label}</span>
                            {f.id === 'ALL' && (
                                <span className={`px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded text-[10px] font-bold ${industryFilter === 'ALL' ? 'bg-white/20 text-white' : 'bg-slate-200 text-slate-700'}`}>{products.length}</span>
                            )}
                          </button>
                      ))}
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div className={inventorySubTab === 'restock' ? 'block' : 'hidden'}>
                    <RestockModule
                        products={filteredProducts} role={role} currency={currency} exchangeRate={activeExchangeRate}
                        onApproveAndContactSupplier={handleApproveAndContactSupplier} onConfirmSupplierDelivery={handleConfirmSupplierDelivery}
                        onRecordStockUsage={handleRecordStockUsage} onOpenAddModal={() => setShowAddModal(true)} onBatchImportProducts={handleBatchImportProducts}
                    />
                  </div>

                  <div className={inventorySubTab === 'orders' ? 'block' : 'hidden'}>
                    <OrderStatusModule
                        products={products} role={role} userID={userSession.userID} currency={currency} exchangeRate={activeExchangeRate}
                        onApproveCustomerOrder={handleApproveCustomerOrder} onTriggerShortageRestock={handleTriggerShortageRestock}
                        onPendingCountChange={setPendingCustomerOrderCount} onLogAction={addAuditLog}
                        onNavigateToRestock={() => setInventorySubTab('restock')} onNotify={pushToast} onTriggerLoadingScreen={handleTriggerLoadingScreen}
                    />
                  </div>
                </div>
            )}

            {activeTab === 'mod2' && (
                <SalesAnalyticsModule
                    products={products} userID={userSession.userID} currency={currency} exchangeRate={activeExchangeRate}
                    onQueueRestockToOrderModule={handleQueueRestockToOrderModule} onLogAction={addAuditLog} onTriggerLoadingScreen={handleTriggerLoadingScreen}
                />
            )}

            {activeTab === 'mod4' && (
                <QualityControlModule
                    qcAlerts={qcAlerts} products={products} role={role} currency={currency} exchangeRate={activeExchangeRate}
                    onDispatchToHrStaff={handleDispatchQCToHrStaff} onEscalateOverdueToHr={handleEscalateOverdueToHr}
                    onCompleteHrTask={handleCompleteHrQcTask} onTriggerScan={handleTriggerQCScan}
                    onAddCustomQCAlert={(a) => { setQcAlerts((p) => [a, ...p]); addAuditLog('Quality Control', `Logged batch issue for ${a.productName}.`); pushToast('info', `Logged batch alert for ${a.productName}.`); }}
                />
            )}

            {activeTab === 'mod5' && (
                <InvoiceModule
                    invoices={invoices} role={role} currency={currency} exchangeRate={activeExchangeRate}
                    onVerifyInvoice={handleVerifyInvoice} onToggleInvoicePaid={handleToggleInvoicePaid}
                    onAddValidatedInvoice={handleAddValidatedInvoice}
                    onBatchImportInvoices={(list) => { if (list.length) { setInvoices((p) => [...list, ...p]); pushToast('success', `Imported ${list.length} invoices.`); } }}
                />
            )}

            {activeTab === 'mod6' && (
                <ComplianceRenewalModule
                    profiles={complianceProfiles} setProfiles={setComplianceProfiles} vaultDocs={vaultDocs} setVaultDocs={setVaultDocs}
                    renewalTasks={renewalTasks} setRenewalTasks={setRenewalTasks} role={role} userID={userSession.userID}
                    currency={currency} exchangeRate={activeExchangeRate} onLogAction={addAuditLog} onTriggerLoadingScreen={handleTriggerLoadingScreen}
                />
            )}

            {activeTab === 'logs' && <AuditLogPanel logs={auditLogs} role={role} onClearLogs={() => setAuditLogs([])} />}
          </main>
        </div>

        <AddProductModal isOpen={showAddModal} currency={currency} onClose={() => setShowAddModal(false)} onSaveProduct={handleSaveProduct} />

        <UniversalSystemMigratorModal
            isOpen={showMigratorModal}
            onClose={() => setShowMigratorModal(false)}
            onImportSuccess={({ products: newProds, invoices: newInvs, complianceProfiles: newComp, sourceFileName, transitionMode }) => {
              if (newProds.length) setProducts((p) => [...newProds, ...p]);
              if (newInvs.length) setInvoices((p) => [...newInvs, ...p]);
              if (newComp.length) setComplianceProfiles((p) => [...newComp, ...p]);
              addAuditLog(
                  'AI Migration',
                  `Migrated ${newProds.length} SKUs, ${newInvs.length} AR invoices & ${newComp.length} licenses/warranties from "${sourceFileName}" (Mode: ${transitionMode}).`
              );
              pushToast('success', `AI migrated "${sourceFileName}" (${transitionMode} mode active).`);
            }}
        />

        <LoadingScreen isOpen={loadingOverlayMessage !== null} message={loadingOverlayMessage ?? ''} />
        {toasts.length > 0 && (
            <div className="fixed bottom-5 right-5 z-50 space-y-2 max-w-sm">
              {toasts.map((t) => (
                  <div key={t.id} className="px-4 py-3 rounded-xl shadow-xl border bg-slate-900 text-white border-slate-700 text-xs font-semibold flex items-center gap-2">
                    <span>{t.message}</span>
                  </div>
              ))}
            </div>
        )}
      </div>
  );
}

// src/components/LoadingScreen.tsx
import React, { useEffect, useState, useRef } from 'react';
import startAnimData from './start_anim.json';

export interface LoadingScreenProps {
    isOpen?: boolean;
    message?: string;
    onClose?: () => void;
}

interface StreamParticle {
    id: number;
    x: number;
    y: number;
    length: number;
    size: number;
    speed: number;
    color: string;
    opacity: number;
    type: 'streak' | 'dot' | 'ground';
}

const INITIAL_PARTICLES: StreamParticle[] = [
    { id: 1, x: 820, y: 135, length: 65, size: 4, speed: 9.5, color: '#2BB656', opacity: 0.45, type: 'streak' },
    { id: 2, x: 610, y: 175, length: 42, size: 3, speed: 7.2, color: '#0B0C0E', opacity: 0.18, type: 'streak' },
    { id: 3, x: 860, y: 215, length: 80, size: 5, speed: 11.4, color: '#2BB656', opacity: 0.55, type: 'streak' },
    { id: 4, x: 440, y: 145, length: 0, size: 6, speed: 6.5, color: '#2BB656', opacity: 0.4, type: 'dot' },
    { id: 5, x: 760, y: 285, length: 55, size: 3.5, speed: 10.2, color: '#0B0C0E', opacity: 0.22, type: 'streak' },
    { id: 6, x: 310, y: 240, length: 0, size: 5, speed: 8.4, color: '#0B0C0E', opacity: 0.25, type: 'dot' },
    { id: 7, x: 840, y: 340, length: 72, size: 4, speed: 12.0, color: '#2BB656', opacity: 0.6, type: 'streak' },
    { id: 8, x: 530, y: 315, length: 0, size: 7, speed: 9.8, color: '#2BB656', opacity: 0.5, type: 'dot' },
    { id: 9, x: 680, y: 115, length: 0, size: 4.5, speed: 5.8, color: '#0B0C0E', opacity: 0.2, type: 'dot' },
    { id: 10, x: 220, y: 195, length: 48, size: 3, speed: 8.9, color: '#2BB656', opacity: 0.35, type: 'streak' },
    { id: 11, x: 850, y: 390, length: 58, size: 4, speed: 12.8, color: '#0B0C0E', opacity: 0.28, type: 'streak' },
    { id: 12, x: 410, y: 375, length: 0, size: 5.5, speed: 10.6, color: '#2BB656', opacity: 0.65, type: 'dot' },
    { id: 13, x: 140, y: 466, length: 36, size: 4, speed: 11.5, color: '#CBD5E1', opacity: 0.85, type: 'ground' },
    { id: 14, x: 280, y: 466, length: 48, size: 4, speed: 11.5, color: '#94A3B8', opacity: 0.75, type: 'ground' },
    { id: 15, x: 430, y: 466, length: 32, size: 4, speed: 11.5, color: '#2BB656', opacity: 0.65, type: 'ground' },
    { id: 16, x: 580, y: 466, length: 52, size: 4, speed: 11.5, color: '#CBD5E1', opacity: 0.85, type: 'ground' },
    { id: 17, x: 720, y: 466, length: 40, size: 4, speed: 11.5, color: '#94A3B8', opacity: 0.75, type: 'ground' },
    { id: 18, x: 830, y: 466, length: 38, size: 4, speed: 11.5, color: '#2BB656', opacity: 0.6, type: 'ground' },
];

export const LoadingScreen: React.FC<LoadingScreenProps> = ({
                                                                isOpen = true,
                                                                message = 'Syncing Vyte Inventory Engine...',
                                                                onClose,
                                                            }) => {
    const [elapsedMs, setElapsedMs] = useState<number>(0);
    const rafRef = useRef<number | null>(null);
    const startTimeRef = useRef<number | null>(null);

    const { colors } = startAnimData.designTokens;
    const { glyphs } = startAnimData.logoGeometry;

    useEffect(() => {
        if (!isOpen) {
            setElapsedMs(0);
            startTimeRef.current = null;
            return;
        }

        const tick = (timestamp: number) => {
            if (startTimeRef.current === null) startTimeRef.current = timestamp;
            setElapsedMs(timestamp - startTimeRef.current);
            rafRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
        };

        rafRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
        return () => {
            if (rafRef.current) cancelAnimationFrame(rafRef.current);
        };
    }, [isOpen]);

    if (!isOpen) return null;

    const sproutProgress = Math.min(1, elapsedMs / 420);
    const sproutEase = 1 - Math.pow(1 - sproutProgress, 3);

    const walkTime = Math.max(0, elapsedMs - 250) / 1000;
    const walkPhase = walkTime * 9.2;

    const vBobY = sproutEase * Math.abs(Math.sin(walkPhase)) * -6;
    const vLeanDeg = sproutEase * (4.5 + Math.sin(walkPhase * 2) * 1.2);
    const cartBounceY = sproutEase * Math.sin(walkPhase * 2 - 0.7) * 2.2;
    const wheelRotation = (walkTime * 340) % 360;

    const hipX = 238;
    const hipY = 342 + vBobY;

    const computeLegJoints = (phaseOffset: number) => {
        const phase = walkPhase + phaseOffset;
        const thighLen = 56 * sproutEase;
        const shinLen = 54 * sproutEase;
        const footLen = 20 * sproutEase;

        const thighAngle = Math.sin(phase) * 0.52 + 0.08;
        const kneeBend = Math.max(0, -Math.cos(phase + 0.35)) * 0.85;
        const shinAngle = thighAngle - kneeBend;

        const kneeX = hipX + Math.sin(thighAngle) * thighLen;
        const kneeY = hipY + Math.cos(thighAngle) * thighLen;

        const ankleX = kneeX + Math.sin(shinAngle) * shinLen;
        const ankleY = kneeY + Math.cos(shinAngle) * shinLen;

        const footAngle = shinAngle + 1.45;
        const toeX = ankleX + Math.sin(footAngle) * footLen;
        const toeY = ankleY + Math.cos(footAngle) * footLen;

        return { kneeX, kneeY, ankleX, ankleY, toeX, toeY };
    };

    const backLeg = computeLegJoints(Math.PI);
    const frontLeg = computeLegJoints(0);

    const shoulderX = 296;
    const shoulderY = 248 + vBobY;
    const handleGripX = 334;
    const handleGripY = 262 + cartBounceY;
    const elbowX = (shoulderX + handleGripX) / 2 - 6 * sproutEase;
    const elbowY = (shoulderY + handleGripY) / 2 + 18 * sproutEase;

    return (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[9998] bg-slate-900/45 backdrop-blur-xs flex items-center justify-center p-4 select-none">
            {/* Compact Pop-Up Modal Box */}
            <div className="relative bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-3xl shadow-2xl w-full max-w-md px-6 py-6 flex flex-col items-center justify-center overflow-hidden">
                {/* Subtle Green Radial Accent Inside Pop-Up */}
                <div
                    style={{
                        position: 'absolute',
                        width: '280px',
                        height: '280px',
                        borderRadius: '50%',
                        background: `radial-gradient(circle, ${colors.vyteGreenGlow} 0%, rgba(255,255,255,0) 70%)`,
                        pointerEvents: 'none',
                    }}
                />

                <svg
                    viewBox="88.5 73 800 440"
                    preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet"
                    className="w-full max-w-[320px] h-auto block mx-auto overflow-visible relative z-10"
                    aria-label="Vyte Loading Animation"
                >
                    <defs>
                        <filter id="vyte-cart-shadow" x="-20%" y="-20%" width="140%" height="140%">
                            <feDropShadow dx="0" dy="10" stdDeviation="8" floodColor="#0B0C0E" floodOpacity="0.10" />
                        </filter>
                    </defs>

                    <g style={{ pointerEvents: 'none' }}>
                        <line
                            x1={115}
                            y1={466}
                            x2={862}
                            y2={466}
                            stroke="#E2E8F0"
                            strokeWidth={3}
                            strokeLinecap="round"
                        />

                        {INITIAL_PARTICLES.map((p) => {
                            const span = 740;
                            const rawX = (((p.x - (elapsedMs * p.speed) / 16) % span) + span) % span + 115;

                            if (p.type === 'dot') {
                                return (
                                    <circle
                                        key={p.id}
                                        cx={rawX}
                                        cy={p.y + Math.sin(elapsedMs / 240 + p.id) * 4}
                                        r={p.size}
                                        fill={p.color}
                                        opacity={p.opacity * sproutEase}
                                    />
                                );
                            }

                            return (
                                <line
                                    key={p.id}
                                    x1={rawX}
                                    y1={p.y}
                                    x2={rawX + p.length}
                                    y2={p.y}
                                    stroke={p.color}
                                    strokeWidth={p.size}
                                    strokeLinecap="round"
                                    opacity={p.opacity * sproutEase}
                                />
                            );
                        })}
                    </g>

                    {sproutEase > 0.02 && (
                        <g
                            stroke="#334155"
                            strokeWidth={10}
                            strokeLinecap="round"
                            strokeLinejoin="round"
                            fill="none"
                        >
                            <path
                                d={`M ${hipX - 6} ${hipY} L ${backLeg.kneeX} ${backLeg.kneeY} L ${backLeg.ankleX} ${backLeg.ankleY} L ${backLeg.toeX} ${backLeg.toeY}`}
                            />
                            <path
                                d={`M ${shoulderX - 8} ${shoulderY - 10} L ${elbowX - 4} ${elbowY - 10} L ${handleGripX} ${handleGripY - 12}`}
                                strokeWidth={8}
                            />
                        </g>
                    )}

                    <g
                        filter="url(#vyte-cart-shadow)"
                        style={{
                            transform: `translate(22px, ${cartBounceY}px)`,
                        }}
                    >
                        <path
                            d="M 342 418 L 342 252 Q 342 238 326 238 L 312 238"
                            fill="none"
                            stroke={colors.inkBlack}
                            strokeWidth={11}
                            strokeLinecap="round"
                            strokeLinejoin="round"
                            opacity={sproutEase}
                        />
                        <circle
                            cx={312}
                            cy={238}
                            r={8}
                            fill={colors.vyteGreen}
                            opacity={sproutEase}
                        />

                        <g opacity={sproutEase}>
                            <rect
                                x={330}
                                y={414}
                                width={490}
                                height={20}
                                rx={10}
                                fill={colors.inkBlack}
                            />
                            <rect
                                x={344}
                                y={420}
                                width={462}
                                height={6}
                                rx={3}
                                fill={colors.vyteGreen}
                            />
                            <rect
                                x={804}
                                y={392}
                                width={12}
                                height={30}
                                rx={5}
                                fill={colors.inkBlack}
                            />
                        </g>

                        <g style={{ transform: 'translate(14px, -6px)' }}>
                            <path d={glyphs.y_leftFork.d} fill={glyphs.y_leftFork.fill} />
                            <path d={glyphs.y_descender.d} fill={glyphs.y_descender.fill} />
                            <circle
                                cx={glyphs.y_topGreenDot.cx}
                                cy={glyphs.y_topGreenDot.cy}
                                r={glyphs.y_topGreenDot.r}
                                fill={glyphs.y_topGreenDot.fill}
                            />
                            <circle
                                cx={glyphs.y_bottomGreenDot.cx}
                                cy={glyphs.y_bottomGreenDot.cy}
                                r={glyphs.y_bottomGreenDot.r}
                                fill={glyphs.y_bottomGreenDot.fill}
                            />
                            <path d={glyphs.t_body.d} fill={glyphs.t_body.fill} />
                            <path
                                d={glyphs.e_body.d}
                                fill={glyphs.e_body.fill}
                                fillRule="evenodd"
                            />
                        </g>

                        {[425, 735].map((wheelCx) => (
                            <g
                                key={wheelCx}
                                transform={`translate(${wheelCx}, 444) rotate(${wheelRotation})`}
                                opacity={sproutEase}
                            >
                                <circle cx={0} cy={0} r={20} fill={colors.inkBlack} />
                                <circle cx={0} cy={0} r={13} fill="#F8FAF7" />
                                <line x1={-12} y1={0} x2={12} y2={0} stroke={colors.inkBlack} strokeWidth={3.5} />
                                <line x1={0} y1={-12} x2={0} y2={12} stroke={colors.inkBlack} strokeWidth={3.5} />
                                <circle cx={0} cy={0} r={5.5} fill={colors.vyteGreen} />
                            </g>
                        ))}
                    </g>

                    <g>
                        {sproutEase > 0.02 && (
                            <g
                                stroke={colors.inkBlack}
                                strokeWidth={11}
                                strokeLinecap="round"
                                strokeLinejoin="round"
                                fill="none"
                            >
                                <path
                                    d={`M ${hipX} ${hipY} L ${frontLeg.kneeX} ${frontLeg.kneeY} L ${frontLeg.ankleX} ${frontLeg.ankleY} L ${frontLeg.toeX} ${frontLeg.toeY}`}
                                />
                                <circle
                                    cx={frontLeg.kneeX}
                                    cy={frontLeg.kneeY}
                                    r={5}
                                    fill={colors.vyteGreen}
                                    stroke="none"
                                />
                            </g>
                        )}

                        <g
                            style={{
                                transformOrigin: '238px 340px',
                                transform: `translate(0px, ${vBobY}px) rotate(${vLeanDeg}deg)`,
                            }}
                        >
                            <path d={glyphs.V_leftLeg.d} fill={glyphs.V_leftLeg.fill} />
                            <path d={glyphs.V_rightGreenSlash.d} fill={glyphs.V_rightGreenSlash.fill} />
                        </g>

                        {sproutEase > 0.02 && (
                            <g
                                stroke={colors.inkBlack}
                                strokeWidth={9.5}
                                strokeLinecap="round"
                                strokeLinejoin="round"
                                fill="none"
                            >
                                <path
                                    d={`M ${shoulderX} ${shoulderY} L ${elbowX} ${elbowY} L ${handleGripX} ${handleGripY}`}
                                />
                                <circle
                                    cx={handleGripX}
                                    cy={handleGripY}
                                    r={6}
                                    fill={colors.vyteGreen}
                                    stroke="none"
                                />
                            </g>
                        )}
                    </g>
                </svg>

                <div className="mt-3 flex flex-col items-center gap-2 relative z-10">
                    {message && (
                        <div className="px-4 py-1.5 rounded-full bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 shadow-2xs flex items-center gap-2">
                            <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-[#2BB656] animate-ping shrink-0" />
                            <span className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-800 tracking-wide text-center">
                {message}
              </span>
                        </div>
                    )}

                    {onClose && (
                        <button
                            type="button"
                            onClick={onClose}
                            className="px-3.5 py-1 rounded-lg bg-slate-900 hover:bg-emerald-600 text-white text-[11px] font-bold transition cursor-pointer"
                        >
                            Dismiss
                        </button>
                    )}
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    );
};

export default LoadingScreen;

========== FILE: E:\Code\Vyte\orbyteflow\src\components\SplashScreen.tsx ==========

// src/components/SplashScreen.tsx
import React, { useEffect, useState, useRef } from 'react';
import startAnimData from './start_anim.json';

export interface SplashScreenProps {
    onComplete?: () => void;
    animationData?: typeof startAnimData;
    autoDismiss?: boolean;
}

function springPhysics(t: number, damping = 5.5, frequency = 11.5): number {
    if (t <= 0) return 0;
    if (t >= 1) return 1;
    const decay = Math.exp(-damping * t);
    return 1 - decay * (Math.cos(frequency * t) + (damping / frequency) * Math.sin(frequency * t));
}

function progressBetween(elapsed: number, startMs: number, durationMs: number): number {
    return Math.min(1, Math.max(0, (elapsed - startMs) / durationMs));
}

interface MiniParcel {
    id: string;
    shelf: number;
    x: number;
    y: number;
    w: number;
    h: number;
    tone: 'kraft' | 'dark' | 'green';
    delayMs: number;
}

export const SplashScreen: React.FC<SplashScreenProps> = ({
                                                              onComplete,
                                                              animationData = startAnimData,
                                                              autoDismiss = true,
                                                          }) => {
    const [elapsedMs, setElapsedMs] = useState<number>(0);
    const [isPlaying, setIsPlaying] = useState<boolean>(true);
    const [isExiting, setIsExiting] = useState<boolean>(false);
    const rafRef = useRef<number | null>(null);
    const lastTimeRef = useRef<number | null>(null);

    const totalDuration = 3400;
    const { colors } = animationData.designTokens;
    const { glyphs, subtitle } = animationData.logoGeometry;

    const tMorph = springPhysics(progressBetween(elapsedMs, 500, 600), 6.2, 10.5);
    const tPackCompress = springPhysics(progressBetween(elapsedMs, 1350, 500), 5.8, 11.0);
    const tCartonRise = springPhysics(progressBetween(elapsedMs, 1400, 540), 5.2, 12.0);
    const tFlapsClose = springPhysics(progressBetween(elapsedMs, 1650, 400), 5.0, 13.0);
    const tTapeSeal = springPhysics(progressBetween(elapsedMs, 1850, 300), 6.5, 11.0);

    const shipRaw = progressBetween(elapsedMs, 2100, 520);
    const shipAnticipation = shipRaw > 0 && shipRaw < 0.22 ? -Math.sin((shipRaw / 0.22) * Math.PI) * 28 : 0;
    const shipLaunch = shipRaw >= 0.18 ? Math.pow((shipRaw - 0.18) / 0.82, 2.3) * 1180 : 0;
    const shipTranslateX = shipAnticipation + shipLaunch;
    const shipScaleX = shipRaw > 0.2 ? 1 + Math.min(0.18, (shipRaw - 0.2) * 0.35) : 1 - Math.max(0, shipRaw * 0.25);
    const shipScaleY = shipRaw > 0.2 ? 1 - Math.min(0.12, (shipRaw - 0.2) * 0.22) : 1 + Math.max(0, shipRaw * 0.2);

    const miniParcels: MiniParcel[] = [
        { id: 'p0', shelf: 0, x: 314, y: 120, w: 50, h: 32, tone: 'kraft', delayMs: 650 },
        { id: 'p1', shelf: 0, x: 372, y: 116, w: 56, h: 36, tone: 'green', delayMs: 710 },
        { id: 'p2', shelf: 0, x: 436, y: 122, w: 48, h: 30, tone: 'dark', delayMs: 770 },
        { id: 'p3', shelf: 1, x: 316, y: 192, w: 58, h: 34, tone: 'dark', delayMs: 730 },
        { id: 'p4', shelf: 1, x: 382, y: 190, w: 48, h: 36, tone: 'kraft', delayMs: 790 },
        { id: 'p5', shelf: 1, x: 438, y: 194, w: 46, h: 32, tone: 'green', delayMs: 850 },
        { id: 'p6', shelf: 2, x: 314, y: 264, w: 52, h: 34, tone: 'green', delayMs: 810 },
        { id: 'p7', shelf: 2, x: 374, y: 262, w: 60, h: 36, tone: 'kraft', delayMs: 870 },
        { id: 'p8', shelf: 2, x: 442, y: 266, w: 44, h: 32, tone: 'dark', delayMs: 930 },
    ];

    useEffect(() => {
        if (!isPlaying) {
            lastTimeRef.current = null;
            return;
        }

        const tick = (timestamp: number) => {
            if (lastTimeRef.current === null) lastTimeRef.current = timestamp;
            const delta = Math.min(32, timestamp - lastTimeRef.current);
            lastTimeRef.current = timestamp;

            setElapsedMs((prev) => {
                const next = prev + delta;
                if (next >= totalDuration) {
                    setIsPlaying(false);
                    if (autoDismiss) {
                        setTimeout(() => setIsExiting(true), 150);
                        setTimeout(() => onComplete?.(), 580);
                    }
                    return totalDuration;
                }
                return next;
            });

            rafRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
        };

        rafRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
        return () => {
            if (rafRef.current) cancelAnimationFrame(rafRef.current);
        };
    }, [isPlaying, totalDuration, autoDismiss, onComplete]);

    const getGlyphSpring = (delayOffsetMs: number) => {
        const raw = progressBetween(elapsedMs, 2240 + delayOffsetMs, 760);
        const s = springPhysics(raw, 5.2, 11.5);
        return {
            translateX: -420 * (1 - s),
            translateY: 24 * (1 - s),
            scale: 0.82 + 0.18 * s,
            opacity: Math.min(1, raw * 3.8),
        };
    };

    const vSpring = getGlyphSpring(0);
    const ySpring = getGlyphSpring(70);
    const tSpring = getGlyphSpring(140);
    const eSpring = getGlyphSpring(210);
    const subSpring = getGlyphSpring(310);

    return (
        <div
            style={{
                position: 'fixed',
                top: 0,
                left: 0,
                width: '100vw',
                height: '100dvh',
                zIndex: 9999,
                display: 'grid',
                placeItems: 'center',
                backgroundColor: colors.surfaceOffWhite,
                opacity: isExiting ? 0 : 1,
                pointerEvents: isExiting ? 'none' : 'auto',
                transition: 'opacity 420ms cubic-bezier(0.22, 1, 0.36, 1)',
                fontFamily: '"Plus Jakarta Sans", "Inter", system-ui, sans-serif',
                userSelect: 'none',
                overflow: 'hidden',
            }}
        >
            {/* Ambient Radial Glow (Centered dynamically in viewport) */}
            <div
                style={{
                    position: 'absolute',
                    width: 'min(80vw, 640px)',
                    height: 'min(80vw, 640px)',
                    borderRadius: '50%',
                    background: `radial-gradient(circle, ${colors.vyteGreenGlow} 0%, rgba(246,247,244,0) 70%)`,
                    transform: `scale(${0.85 + 0.35 * tCartonRise})`,
                    opacity: elapsedMs > 2200 ? 0.95 : 0.55,
                    transition: 'opacity 400ms ease',
                    pointerEvents: 'none',
                }}
            />

            <svg
                viewBox={animationData.logoGeometry.viewBox}
                preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet"
                style={{
                    width: 'min(88vw, 740px)',
                    maxHeight: '82dvh',
                    height: 'auto',
                    display: 'block',
                    margin: '0 auto',
                    overflow: 'visible',
                }}
            >
                <defs>
                    <filter id="vyte-elevated-shadow" x="-25%" y="-25%" width="150%" height="150%">
                        <feDropShadow dx="0" dy="16" stdDeviation="16" floodColor="#0B0C0E" floodOpacity="0.11" />
                    </filter>
                    <linearGradient id="kraft-box-grad" x1="0%" y1="0%" x2="100%" y2="100%">
                        <stop offset="0%" stopColor="#DFA366" />
                        <stop offset="100%" stopColor="#C48345" />
                    </linearGradient>
                    <linearGradient id="kraft-flap-grad" x1="0%" y1="0%" x2="0%" y2="100%">
                        <stop offset="0%" stopColor="#ECCFA9" />
                        <stop offset="100%" stopColor="#D49658" />
                    </linearGradient>
                </defs>

                {/* ACT 1 - 3: DATABASE -> LOGISTICS DRAWER -> BOXED UP & SHIPPED */}
                {shipRaw < 0.98 && (
                    <g
                        style={{
                            transformOrigin: '400px 235px',
                            transform: `translate(${shipTranslateX}px, 0px) scale(${shipScaleX}, ${shipScaleY})`,
                            opacity: shipRaw > 0.82 ? Math.max(0, 1 - (shipRaw - 0.82) * 5.5) : 1,
                        }}
                    >
                        {/* 1. DATABASE / LOGISTICS DRAWER UNIT */}
                        <g
                            filter="url(#vyte-elevated-shadow)"
                            style={{
                                transformOrigin: '400px 250px',
                                transform: `translateY(${tPackCompress * 34}px) scale(${1 - tPackCompress * 0.22})`,
                                opacity: 1 - Math.max(0, (tFlapsClose - 0.45) * 1.9),
                            }}
                        >
                            <g
                                style={{
                                    transformOrigin: '400px 215px',
                                    transform: `scale(${0.84 + 0.16 * tMorph})`,
                                    opacity: Math.min(1, tMorph * 1.4),
                                }}
                            >
                                <rect x={274} y={84} width={252} height={260} rx={24} fill={colors.inkBlack} />
                                <rect x={284} y={94} width={232} height={240} rx={18} fill="#F8FAF7" />
                                <rect x={294} y={104} width={6} height={220} rx={3} fill="#E2E5DF" />
                                <rect x={500} y={104} width={6} height={220} rx={3} fill="#E2E5DF" />
                            </g>

                            {[0, 1, 2].map((tierIdx) => {
                                const tierY = 104 + tierIdx * 72;
                                const dbEnter = springPhysics(progressBetween(elapsedMs, tierIdx * 90, 500), 4.8, 12.0);
                                const dbScale = 0.7 + 0.3 * dbEnter;
                                const dbTranslateY = (1 - dbEnter) * -45;

                                return (
                                    <g
                                        key={`db-tier-${tierIdx}`}
                                        style={{
                                            transformOrigin: `400px ${tierY + 28}px`,
                                            transform: `translateY(${dbTranslateY}px) scale(${dbScale})`,
                                            opacity: Math.min(1, dbEnter * 2.5),
                                        }}
                                    >
                                        <rect
                                            x={300 - (1 - tMorph) * 10}
                                            y={tierY}
                                            width={200 + (1 - tMorph) * 20}
                                            height={56}
                                            rx={tMorph > 0.5 ? 12 : 26}
                                            fill={tMorph > 0.5 ? '#E7ECE4' : colors.inkBlack}
                                        />

                                        <ellipse
                                            cx={400}
                                            cy={tierY + 13}
                                            rx={106}
                                            ry={12}
                                            fill="#1F242D"
                                            style={{ opacity: Math.max(0, 1 - tMorph * 1.35) }}
                                        />

                                        <g style={{ opacity: Math.max(0, 1 - tMorph * 1.5) }}>
                                            <circle cx={324} cy={tierY + 34} r={6} fill={colors.vyteGreen} />
                                            <rect x={340} y={tierY + 31} width={75} height={6} rx={3} fill="#2E3540" />
                                            <rect x={425} y={tierY + 31} width={42} height={6} rx={3} fill={colors.vyteGreen} opacity={0.65} />
                                        </g>

                                        {miniParcels
                                            .filter((p) => p.shelf === tierIdx)
                                            .map((parcel) => {
                                                const pRaw = progressBetween(elapsedMs, parcel.delayMs, 450);
                                                const pSpring = springPhysics(pRaw, 4.4, 13.5);
                                                const fillMap = {
                                                    kraft: '#D49658',
                                                    dark: colors.inkBlack,
                                                    green: colors.vyteGreen,
                                                };
                                                return (
                                                    <g
                                                        key={parcel.id}
                                                        style={{
                                                            transformOrigin: `${parcel.x + parcel.w / 2}px ${parcel.y + parcel.h}px`,
                                                            transform: `translateY(${(1 - pSpring) * -26}px) scale(${0.3 + 0.7 * pSpring})`,
                                                            opacity: Math.min(1, pRaw * 4),
                                                        }}
                                                    >
                                                        <rect
                                                            x={parcel.x}
                                                            y={parcel.y}
                                                            width={parcel.w}
                                                            height={parcel.h}
                                                            rx={6}
                                                            fill={fillMap[parcel.tone]}
                                                        />
                                                        <rect
                                                            x={parcel.x + parcel.w / 2 - 4}
                                                            y={parcel.y}
                                                            width={8}
                                                            height={parcel.h * 0.45}
                                                            rx={1.5}
                                                            fill={parcel.tone === 'green' ? '#FFFFFF' : colors.vyteGreen}
                                                            opacity={0.9}
                                                        />
                                                        <rect
                                                            x={parcel.x + 6}
                                                            y={parcel.y + parcel.h - 11}
                                                            width={16}
                                                            height={6}
                                                            rx={1.5}
                                                            fill="#FFFFFF"
                                                            opacity={0.85}
                                                        />
                                                    </g>
                                                );
                                            })}

                                        <g style={{ opacity: Math.min(1, tMorph * 1.5) }}>
                                            <rect x={300} y={tierY + 38} width={200} height={20} rx={8} fill="#CFD6C9" />
                                            <rect
                                                x={380}
                                                y={tierY + 45}
                                                width={40}
                                                height={6}
                                                rx={3}
                                                fill={tierIdx === 1 ? colors.vyteGreen : colors.inkBlack}
                                            />
                                        </g>
                                    </g>
                                );
                            })}
                        </g>

                        {/* 2. MASTER CORRUGATED LOGISTICS CARTON */}
                        {elapsedMs >= 1350 && (
                            <g
                                filter="url(#vyte-elevated-shadow)"
                                style={{
                                    transformOrigin: '400px 270px',
                                    transform: `translateY(${(1 - tCartonRise) * 140}px) scale(${0.86 + 0.14 * tCartonRise})`,
                                    opacity: Math.min(1, progressBetween(elapsedMs, 1350, 200) * 2),
                                }}
                            >
                                <g
                                    style={{
                                        transformOrigin: '400px 168px',
                                        transform: `scaleY(${1 - tFlapsClose * 0.88})`,
                                        opacity: 1 - tFlapsClose * 0.6,
                                    }}
                                >
                                    <polygon points="264,168 310,124 398,124 398,168" fill="url(#kraft-flap-grad)" />
                                    <polygon points="536,168 490,124 402,124 402,168" fill="url(#kraft-flap-grad)" />
                                </g>

                                <rect x={264} y={166} width={272} height={186} rx={20} fill="url(#kraft-box-grad)" />

                                    d="M 512 166 L 536 166 L 536 332 C 536 343 527 352 516 352 L 512 352 Z"

                                <line
                                    x1={276}
