========== FILE: E:\Code\Vyte\orbyteflow\src\components\common\VyteDropdown.tsx ==========

// src/components/common/VyteDropdown.tsx
import React, { useState, useRef, useEffect } from 'react';
import { createPortal } from 'react-dom';
import { ChevronDown, Check } from 'lucide-react';

export interface VyteDropdownOption<T extends string | number = string> {
    label: string;
    value: T;
    badge?: string;
}

export interface VyteDropdownProps<T extends string | number = string> {
    value: T;
    options: VyteDropdownOption<T>[];
    onChange: (value: T) => void;
    prefix?: string;
    icon?: React.ReactNode;
    variant?: 'light' | 'soft' | 'dark' | 'purple';
    size?: 'sm' | 'md';
    className?: string;
    disabled?: boolean;
}

export function VyteDropdown<T extends string | number = string>({
                                                                     value,
                                                                     options,
                                                                     onChange,
                                                                     prefix,
                                                                     icon,
                                                                     variant = 'light',
                                                                     size = 'md',
                                                                     className = '',
                                                                     disabled = false,
                                                                 }: VyteDropdownProps<T>) {
    const [isOpen, setIsOpen] = useState(false);
    const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
    const menuRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
    const [menuPosition, setMenuPosition] = useState<React.CSSProperties>({});
    const [openUpward, setOpenUpward] = useState(false);

    const selectedOption = options.find((opt) => opt.value === value) || options[0];

    // Compute dynamic coordinates so the menu overlays over tables and scroll containers
    const updatePosition = () => {
        if (!containerRef.current) return;
        const rect = containerRef.current.getBoundingClientRect();
        const estimatedMenuHeight = 224; // max-h-56

        const spaceBelow = window.innerHeight - rect.bottom;
        const shouldOpenUp = spaceBelow < estimatedMenuHeight && rect.top > estimatedMenuHeight;

        setOpenUpward(shouldOpenUp);
        setMenuPosition({
            position: 'fixed',
            left: `${rect.left}px`,
            width: `${rect.width}px`,
            top: shouldOpenUp ? undefined : `${rect.bottom - 1}px`,
            bottom: shouldOpenUp ? `${window.innerHeight - rect.top - 1}px` : undefined,
            zIndex: 99999,
        });
    };

    useEffect(() => {
        if (!isOpen) return;

        updatePosition();

        const handleScrollOrResize = () => {
            updatePosition();
        };

        window.addEventListener('resize', handleScrollOrResize);
        window.addEventListener('scroll', handleScrollOrResize, true);

        return () => {
            window.removeEventListener('resize', handleScrollOrResize);
            window.removeEventListener('scroll', handleScrollOrResize, true);
        };
    }, [isOpen]);

    // Close on click outside (checking both trigger button and portaled menu)
    useEffect(() => {
        const handleClickOutside = (e: MouseEvent) => {
            const target = e.target as Node;
            const clickedInsideTrigger = containerRef.current?.contains(target);
            const clickedInsideMenu = menuRef.current?.contains(target);

            if (!clickedInsideTrigger && !clickedInsideMenu) {
                setIsOpen(false);
            }
        };

        const handleEscape = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
            if (e.key === 'Escape') setIsOpen(false);
        };

        document.addEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);
        document.addEventListener('keydown', handleEscape);
        return () => {
            document.removeEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);
            document.removeEventListener('keydown', handleEscape);
        };
    }, []);

    const sizeStyles =
        size === 'sm'
            ? 'px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs rounded-lg'
            : 'px-3.5 py-2 text-xs rounded-xl';

    const openTopRadius =
        size === 'sm' ? 'rounded-t-lg rounded-b-none' : 'rounded-t-xl rounded-b-none';
    const openBottomRadius =
        size === 'sm' ? 'rounded-b-lg rounded-t-none' : 'rounded-b-xl rounded-t-none';

    const getTriggerTheme = () => {
        if (variant === 'dark') {
            return isOpen
                ? 'bg-[#1E242B] border-[#2BB656] text-emerald-400 border-b-transparent shadow-lg'
                : 'bg-[#1E242B] border-slate-700/80 hover:border-[#2BB656]/60 text-emerald-400';
        }
        if (variant === 'purple') {
            return isOpen
                ? 'bg-white border-purple-400 text-purple-900 border-b-transparent shadow-md'
                : 'bg-purple-50/80 border-purple-200 hover:border-purple-300 text-purple-800';
        }
        if (variant === 'soft') {
            return isOpen
                ? 'bg-white border-[#2BB656] text-slate-900 border-b-transparent shadow-md'
                : 'bg-slate-50 border-slate-200 hover:border-slate-300 text-slate-800';
        }
        return isOpen
            ? 'bg-white border-[#2BB656] text-slate-900 border-b-transparent shadow-md'
            : 'bg-white border-slate-300 hover:border-slate-400 text-slate-900';
    };

    const getMenuTheme = () => {
        if (variant === 'dark') {
            return 'bg-[#1E242B] border-[#2BB656] text-slate-200 divide-slate-800/80 shadow-2xl';
        }
        if (variant === 'purple') {
            return 'bg-white border-purple-400 text-slate-800 divide-purple-100 shadow-2xl';
        }
        return 'bg-white border-[#2BB656] text-slate-800 divide-slate-100 shadow-2xl';
    };

    const getOptionTheme = (isSelected: boolean) => {
        if (variant === 'dark') {
            return isSelected
                ? 'bg-[#2BB656]/20 text-emerald-400 font-bold'
                : 'text-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-800/90 hover:text-white font-medium';
        }
        if (variant === 'purple') {
            return isSelected
                ? 'bg-purple-50 text-purple-900 font-bold'
                : 'text-slate-700 hover:bg-purple-50/50 hover:text-purple-900 font-medium';
        }
        return isSelected
            ? 'bg-[#EBFDF2] text-emerald-900 font-bold'
            : 'text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-50 hover:text-slate-900 font-medium';
    };

    return (
        <div ref={containerRef} className={`relative select-none ${className}`}>
            <button
                type="button"
                disabled={disabled}
                onClick={() => {
                    if (!isOpen) updatePosition();
                    setIsOpen((prev) => !prev);
                }}
                className={`w-full flex items-center justify-between gap-2 border font-semibold transition-colors cursor-pointer focus:outline-none ${sizeStyles} ${
                    isOpen ? (openUpward ? openBottomRadius : openTopRadius) : ''
                } ${getTriggerTheme()} ${disabled ? 'opacity-50 cursor-not-allowed' : ''}`}
            >
                <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5 min-w-0 truncate">
                    {icon && <span className="shrink-0">{icon}</span>}
                    {prefix && (
                        <span
                            className={`font-semibold shrink-0 ${
                                variant === 'dark' ? 'text-slate-400' : 'text-slate-500'
                            }`}
                        >
                            {prefix}
                        </span>
                    )}
                    <span className="truncate font-bold">{selectedOption?.label}</span>
                </span>
                <ChevronDown
                    className={`w-3.5 h-3.5 shrink-0 transition-transform duration-200 ${
                        isOpen ? 'rotate-180' : ''
                    } ${
                        variant === 'dark'
                            ? 'text-emerald-400'
                            : variant === 'purple'
                                ? 'text-purple-600'
                                : 'text-slate-400'
                    }`}
                />
            </button>

            {/* Render options menu in document.body to break free from table overflow boundaries */}
            {isOpen &&
                createPortal(
                    <div
                        ref={menuRef}
                        style={menuPosition}
                        className={`border overflow-hidden ${
                            openUpward
                                ? size === 'sm'
                                    ? 'rounded-t-lg border-b-0'
                                    : 'rounded-t-xl border-b-0'
                                : openBottomRadius
                        } ${getMenuTheme()}`}
                    >
                        <div
                            className={`border-t ${
                                variant === 'dark'
                                    ? 'border-slate-700/70'
                                    : variant === 'purple'
                                        ? 'border-purple-100'
                                        : 'border-slate-100'
                            }`}
                        />
                        <div className="max-h-56 overflow-y-auto py-1">
                            {options.map((opt) => {
                                const isSelected = opt.value === value;
                                return (
                                    <button
                                        key={String(opt.value)}
                                        type="button"
                                        onClick={() => {
                                            onChange(opt.value);
                                            setIsOpen(false);
                                        }}
                                        className={`w-full px-3.5 py-2 text-left text-xs flex items-center justify-between gap-2 transition-colors cursor-pointer ${getOptionTheme(
                                            isSelected
                                        )}`}
                                    >
                                        <span className="truncate">{opt.label}</span>
                                        {isSelected && (
                                            <Check
                                                className={`w-3.5 h-3.5 shrink-0 ${
                                                    variant === 'dark'
                                                        ? 'text-emerald-400'
                                                        : variant === 'purple'
                                                            ? 'text-purple-600'
                                                            : 'text-[#2BB656]'
                                                }`}
                                            />
                                        )}
                                    </button>
                                );
                            })}
                        </div>
                    </div>,
                    document.body
                )}
        </div>
    );
}

========== FILE: E:\Code\Vyte\orbyteflow\src\components\common\VyteLogo.tsx ==========

// src/components/common/VyteLogo.tsx
import React from 'react';
import startAnimData from '../start_anim.json';

interface VyteLogoProps {
    variant?: 'dark-text' | 'light-text';
    showSubtitle?: boolean;
    iconOnly?: boolean;
    className?: string;
}

export const VyteLogo: React.FC<VyteLogoProps> = ({
                                                      variant = 'dark-text',
                                                      showSubtitle = true,
                                                      iconOnly = false,
                                                      className = 'h-10 w-auto',
                                                  }) => {
    const { glyphs, subtitle } = startAnimData.logoGeometry;
    const primaryInk = variant === 'light-text' ? '#FFFFFF' : '#0B0C0E';
    const vyteGreen = '#2BB656';

    if (iconOnly) {
        return (
            <svg viewBox="115 100 260 270" className={className} aria-label="Vyte">
                <path d={glyphs.V_leftLeg.d} fill={primaryInk} />
                <path d={glyphs.V_rightGreenSlash.d} fill={vyteGreen} />
            </svg>
        );
    }

    return (
        <svg
            viewBox={showSubtitle ? '85 75 760 440' : '85 95 760 345'}
            preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet"
            className={className}
            aria-label="Vyte Inventory Management"
        >
            {/* V */}
            <path d={glyphs.V_leftLeg.d} fill={primaryInk} />
            <path d={glyphs.V_rightGreenSlash.d} fill={vyteGreen} />

            {/* y */}
            <path d={glyphs.y_leftFork.d} fill={primaryInk} />
            <path d={glyphs.y_descender.d} fill={primaryInk} />
            <circle
                cx={glyphs.y_topGreenDot.cx}
                cy={glyphs.y_topGreenDot.cy}
                r={glyphs.y_topGreenDot.r}
                fill={vyteGreen}
            />
            <circle
                cx={glyphs.y_bottomGreenDot.cx}
                cy={glyphs.y_bottomGreenDot.cy}
                r={glyphs.y_bottomGreenDot.r}
                fill={vyteGreen}
            />

            {/* t */}
            <path d={glyphs.t_body.d} fill={primaryInk} />

            {/* e */}
            <path d={glyphs.e_body.d} fill={primaryInk} fillRule="evenodd" />

            {/* Subtitle: INVENTORY MANAGEMENT (Locked to Image 2 proportions) */}
            {showSubtitle && (
                <text
                    x={466}
                    y={472}
                    textAnchor="middle"
                    textLength={546}
                    lengthAdjust="spacing"
                    fill={variant === 'light-text' ? '#CBD5E1' : primaryInk}
                    fontSize={31}
                    fontWeight={700}
                    fontFamily='"Plus Jakarta Sans", "Inter", system-ui, sans-serif'
                >
                    {subtitle.text}
                </text>
            )}
        </svg>
    );
};

// src/components/layout/HeaderBar.tsx
import React, { useState, useRef, useEffect } from 'react';
import {
    Building2, Bell, Layers, RefreshCw, MessageSquare,
    AlertTriangle, FileText, ShieldCheck, ChevronRight, Globe, CheckCheck,
} from 'lucide-react';
import type { ActiveTab, CurrencyCode, UserSession } from '../../types/vyte.ts';
import { CURRENCY_SYMBOLS } from '../../types/vyte.ts';

interface HeaderBarProps {
    activeTab: ActiveTab;
    inventorySubTab?: 'restock' | 'orders';
    userSession: UserSession | null;
    currency?: CurrencyCode;
    exchangeRate?: number;
    rateSourceLabel?: string;
    onRefreshRates?: () => void;
    totalSkus: number;
    pendingRestocks: number;
    pendingOrders: number;
    pendingQcBatches: number;
    totalInvoices: number;
    pendingInvoices: number;
    totalComplianceProfiles?: number;
    pendingCompliance?: number;
    totalVaultDocs?: number;
    totalRenewalTasks?: number;
    onNavigateFromNotification: (tab: ActiveTab, subTab?: 'restock' | 'orders') => void;
}

export const HeaderBar: React.FC<HeaderBarProps> = ({
                                                        activeTab, inventorySubTab = 'restock', userSession, currency = 'MYR', exchangeRate = 1.0,
                                                        rateSourceLabel = 'Live FX Rate', onRefreshRates, totalSkus, pendingRestocks,
                                                        pendingOrders, pendingQcBatches, totalInvoices, pendingInvoices,
                                                        totalComplianceProfiles = 0, pendingCompliance = 0, onNavigateFromNotification,
                                                    }) => {
    const [isBellOpen, setIsBellOpen] = useState(false);
    const [acknowledgedCount, setAcknowledgedCount] = useState(0);
    const bellRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
    const currencySymbol = CURRENCY_SYMBOLS[currency] || 'RM';

    useEffect(() => {
        const onOut = (e: MouseEvent) => { if (bellRef.current && !bellRef.current.contains(e.target as Node)) setIsBellOpen(false); };
        const onEsc = (e: KeyboardEvent) => { if (e.key === 'Escape') setIsBellOpen(false); };
        document.addEventListener('mousedown', onOut);
        document.addEventListener('keydown', onEsc);
        return () => { document.removeEventListener('mousedown', onOut); document.removeEventListener('keydown', onEsc); };
    }, []);

    const titles: Record<ActiveTab, string> = {
        mod1: inventorySubTab === 'orders' ? 'Customer Orders & Fulfillment' : 'Inventory & Supplier Restock',
        mod2: 'Sales Review & Procurement Forecast',
        mod3: 'Customer Orders & Fulfillment',
        mod4: 'Batch Quality & Expiry Control',
        mod5: 'Invoices & Accounts Payable / Receivable',
        mod6: 'Business License & Compliance',
        logs: 'Activity & Audit Log',
    };

    const totalAlerts = pendingRestocks + pendingOrders + pendingQcBatches + pendingInvoices + pendingCompliance;
    const unreadBadgeCount = Math.max(0, totalAlerts - acknowledgedCount);

    const notificationItems = [
        { id: 'notif-restock', label: 'Restock Triggers', value: `${pendingRestocks} Pending`, subtext: 'Low stock awaiting PO approval', valueColor: 'text-amber-600', iconBg: 'bg-amber-500', icon: RefreshCw, onClick: () => onNavigateFromNotification('mod1', 'restock') },
        { id: 'notif-orders', label: 'Customer Orders', value: `${pendingOrders} Pending`, subtext: 'Orders awaiting review', valueColor: 'text-purple-700', iconBg: 'bg-purple-600', icon: MessageSquare, onClick: () => onNavigateFromNotification('mod1', 'orders') },
        { id: 'notif-qc', label: 'Expiry & Quality Risks', value: `${pendingQcBatches} Batches`, subtext: 'Perishable batches at risk', valueColor: 'text-rose-600', iconBg: 'bg-rose-500', icon: AlertTriangle, onClick: () => onNavigateFromNotification('mod4') },
        { id: 'notif-invoices', label: 'Pending Invoices', value: `${pendingInvoices} Review`, subtext: `${totalInvoices} total invoices stored`, valueColor: 'text-emerald-600', iconBg: 'bg-emerald-500', icon: FileText, onClick: () => onNavigateFromNotification('mod5') },
        { id: 'notif-compliance', label: 'License Renewals', value: `${pendingCompliance} Alerts`, subtext: `${totalComplianceProfiles} entities monitored`, valueColor: 'text-teal-700', iconBg: 'bg-teal-600', icon: ShieldCheck, onClick: () => onNavigateFromNotification('mod6') },
        { id: 'notif-skus', label: 'Active Catalog', value: `${totalSkus} SKUs`, subtext: 'All inventory items', valueColor: 'text-slate-900', iconBg: 'bg-slate-700', icon: Layers, onClick: () => onNavigateFromNotification('mod1', 'restock') },
    ];

    return (
        <header className="bg-white border border-slate-200/90 rounded-2xl px-5 py-2.5 flex items-center justify-between gap-4 shrink-0 shadow-2xs z-30">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3 min-w-0">
                <h2 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900 tracking-tight truncate">{titles[activeTab]}</h2>
                {pendingRestocks > 0 && activeTab === 'mod1' && inventorySubTab === 'restock' && (
                    <span className="hidden sm:inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-rose-50 border border-rose-200 text-rose-700 text-[11px] font-semibold">
            <span className="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-rose-500" />
                        {pendingRestocks} low-stock SKU{pendingRestocks > 1 ? 's' : ''} need approval
          </span>
                )}
            </div>

            <div className="flex items-center gap-3 shrink-0">
                <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={onRefreshRates}
                    title={`${rateSourceLabel} (Click to refresh)`}
                    className="hidden md:inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-50 hover:bg-slate-100 border border-slate-200 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 transition cursor-pointer"
                >
                    <Globe className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-600" />
                    <span>{currency} ({currencySymbol})</span>
                    {currency !== 'MYR' && <span className="text-slate-400 font-normal">{exchangeRate.toFixed(2)}</span>}
                </button>

                {userSession && (
                    <div className="text-right hidden sm:block">
                        <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-800 leading-none">{userSession.userName}</p>
                        <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 flex items-center justify-end gap-1 mt-0.5">
                            <Building2 className="w-3 h-3" /> {userSession.companyName}
                        </p>
                    </div>
                )}

                <div ref={bellRef} className="relative pl-2.5 border-l border-slate-200">
                    <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => setIsBellOpen((p) => !p)}
                        title="Actionable Alerts"
                        className={`relative p-2 rounded-xl border transition cursor-pointer flex items-center justify-center ${
                            isBellOpen ? 'bg-emerald-50 border-[#2BB656] text-emerald-700' : 'bg-slate-50 hover:bg-slate-100 border-slate-200 text-slate-700'
                        }`}
                    >
                        <Bell className="w-4 h-4" />
                        {unreadBadgeCount > 0 && (
                            <span className="absolute -top-1 -right-1 min-w-[16px] h-[16px] px-1 rounded-full bg-rose-500 text-white text-[10px] font-bold flex items-center justify-center">
                {unreadBadgeCount}
              </span>
                        )}
                    </button>

                    {isBellOpen && (
                        <div className="absolute right-0 mt-2 w-72 bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 shadow-xl overflow-hidden z-50 select-none">
                            <div className="px-3.5 py-2.5 bg-slate-900 text-white flex items-center justify-between">
                                <span className="text-xs font-semibold">Action Center ({unreadBadgeCount})</span>
                                <button
                                    type="button"
                                    onClick={() => setAcknowledgedCount(totalAlerts)}
                                    className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-white/10 hover:bg-white/20 text-emerald-300 text-[10px] font-medium flex items-center gap-1 cursor-pointer transition"
                                >
                                    <CheckCheck className="w-3 h-3" /> Mark Read
                                </button>
                            </div>
                            <div className="p-2 space-y-1 max-h-80 overflow-y-auto">
                                {notificationItems.map((item) => {
                                    const Icon = item.icon;
                                    return (
                                        <button
                                            key={item.id}
                                            type="button"
                                            onClick={() => { item.onClick(); setIsBellOpen(false); }}
                                            className="w-full text-left bg-white hover:bg-slate-50 px-2.5 py-2 rounded-lg border border-transparent hover:border-slate-200 flex items-center justify-between gap-2 transition cursor-pointer group"
                                        >
                                            <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 min-w-0">
                                                <div className={`w-7 h-7 rounded-lg ${item.iconBg} text-white flex items-center justify-center shrink-0`}>
                                                    <Icon className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                                                </div>
                                                <div className="min-w-0">
                                                    <div className="flex items-baseline gap-1.5">
                                                        <span className={`text-xs font-bold leading-none ${item.valueColor}`}>{item.value}</span>
                                                        <span className="text-xs text-slate-700 font-medium truncate">{item.label}</span>
                                                    </div>
                                                    <span className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 block truncate mt-0.5">{item.subtext}</span>
                                                </div>
                                            </div>
                                            <ChevronRight className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-300 group-hover:text-slate-600 transition shrink-0" />
                                        </button>
                                    );
                                })}
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    )}
                </div>
            </div>
        </header>
    );
};

// src/components/layout/Sidebar.tsx
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import {
    Box, TrendingUp, AlertTriangle, FileText, Clock, Search, ChevronLeft, ChevronRight,
    Settings, LogOut, X, ShieldCheck, Volume2, FileCode, Sliders, Bug, Server, Database,
} from 'lucide-react';
import type { ActiveTab, CurrencyCode, UserRole, UserSession } from '../../types/vyte.ts';
import { VyteLogo } from '../common/VyteLogo';
import { VyteDropdown } from '../common/VyteDropdown';
import { getApiBaseUrl, setApiBaseUrl } from '../../services/api';

interface SidebarProps {
    activeTab: ActiveTab;
    setActiveTab: (tab: ActiveTab) => void;
    role: UserRole;
    operatingCurrency: CurrencyCode;
    onCurrencyChange: (currency: CurrencyCode) => void;
    industryFilter?: string;
    setIndustryFilter?: (filter: string) => void;
    searchQuery: string;
    setSearchQuery: (q: string) => void;
    pendingRestockCount: number;
    pendingOrderCount: number;
    pendingQcCount: number;
    pendingInvoiceCount: number;
    pendingComplianceCount: number;
    auditLogCount: number;
    totalProductCount: number;
    userSession: UserSession | null;
    onLogout: () => void;
    debugMockMode?: boolean;
    onToggleDebugMockMode?: (enabled: boolean) => void;
    onOpenMigratorModal?: () => void; // <-- æŽ¥æ”¶æ‰“å¼€å¼¹çª—çš„å›žè°ƒ
}

export const Sidebar: React.FC<SidebarProps> = ({
                                                    activeTab, setActiveTab, role, operatingCurrency, onCurrencyChange, searchQuery, setSearchQuery,
                                                    pendingRestockCount, pendingOrderCount, pendingQcCount, pendingInvoiceCount, pendingComplianceCount,
                                                    auditLogCount, userSession, onLogout, debugMockMode = false, onToggleDebugMockMode, onOpenMigratorModal,
                                                }) => {
    const [isCollapsed, setIsCollapsed] = useState(false);
    const [showSettingsModal, setShowSettingsModal] = useState(false);
    const [tempCurrency, setTempCurrency] = useState<CurrencyCode>(operatingCurrency);
    const [defaultTurnoverTarget, setDefaultTurnoverTarget] = useState<'7' | '14' | '21' | '30' | '60'>('21');
    const [tempDebugMode, setTempDebugMode] = useState(debugMockMode);
    const [tempServerUrl, setTempServerUrl] = useState(getApiBaseUrl);
    const [prefs, setPrefs] = useState({ audio: true, autoPo: true, wa: true, rbac: true, einv: true });

    useEffect(() => {
        setTempCurrency(operatingCurrency);
        setTempDebugMode(debugMockMode);
        setTempServerUrl(getApiBaseUrl());
    }, [operatingCurrency, debugMockMode, showSettingsModal]);

    const handleSaveSettings = () => {
        setApiBaseUrl(tempServerUrl);
        if (onToggleDebugMockMode && tempDebugMode !== debugMockMode) onToggleDebugMockMode(tempDebugMode);
        onCurrencyChange(tempCurrency);
        setShowSettingsModal(false);
    };

    const roleBadge = {
        SME_OWNER: { label: 'Owner (Full Approval Access)', style: 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-800 border-emerald-200' },
        MANAGER: { label: 'Manager (Approval & Ops Access)', style: 'bg-blue-50 text-blue-800 border-blue-200' },
        OPERATIONS_STAFF: { label: 'Ops Staff (Read & Trigger Only)', style: 'bg-amber-50 text-amber-800 border-amber-200' },
    }[role];

    const coreModules = [
        { id: 'mod1' as ActiveTab, label: 'Restock & Orders', icon: Box, iconColor: 'text-emerald-600', badge: String(pendingRestockCount + pendingOrderCount), badgeClass: 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-800' },
        { id: 'mod2' as ActiveTab, label: 'Sales Analytics', icon: TrendingUp, iconColor: 'text-blue-600', badge: 'AI', badgeClass: 'bg-blue-100 text-blue-700 rounded-md' },
        { id: 'mod4' as ActiveTab, label: 'Quality & Expiry', icon: AlertTriangle, iconColor: 'text-rose-600', badge: String(pendingQcCount), badgeClass: 'bg-rose-100 text-rose-700' },
        { id: 'mod5' as ActiveTab, label: 'Smart Invoices', icon: FileText, iconColor: 'text-emerald-600', badge: String(pendingInvoiceCount), badgeClass: 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700' },
        { id: 'mod6' as ActiveTab, label: 'License & Compliance', icon: ShieldCheck, iconColor: 'text-teal-600', badge: String(pendingComplianceCount), badgeClass: 'bg-teal-100 text-teal-800' },
        { id: 'logs' as ActiveTab, label: 'Audit Logs', icon: Clock, iconColor: 'text-slate-600', badge: String(auditLogCount), badgeClass: 'bg-slate-200 text-slate-800' },
    ];

    const toggleItems = [
        { key: 'audio' as const, icon: Volume2, title: 'Audible Stock Alert', desc: 'Play alert chime when inventory hits min threshold' },
        { key: 'autoPo' as const, icon: Box, title: 'Auto-Draft Purchase Orders', desc: 'Prepare PO in queue immediately upon low stock trigger' },
        { key: 'wa' as const, icon: FileText, title: 'Automated WhatsApp PO Dispatch', desc: 'Send supplier POs upon authorized human approval' },
        { key: 'rbac' as const, icon: ShieldCheck, title: 'Enforce Strict RBAC Permissions', desc: 'Lock approvals and PO releases to Owner and Manager' },
        { key: 'einv' as const, icon: FileCode, title: 'LHDN MyInvois 2.0 Compliance', desc: 'Enable validation schemas for Malaysian e-invoices' },
    ];

    return (
        <>
            <aside className={`${isCollapsed ? 'w-20' : 'w-64'} h-full bg-white border border-slate-200/90 rounded-2xl shadow-xs flex flex-col justify-between shrink-0 transition-all duration-300 ease-in-out select-none relative z-30`}>
                <button
                    type="button" onClick={() => setIsCollapsed(!isCollapsed)} title={isCollapsed ? 'Expand Sidebar' : 'Collapse Sidebar'}
                    className="w-6 h-6 rounded-full bg-white border border-slate-200 shadow-sm flex items-center justify-center text-slate-500 hover:text-emerald-600 hover:border-emerald-300 absolute -right-3 top-8 z-40 transition cursor-pointer"
                >
                    {isCollapsed ? <ChevronRight className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> : <ChevronLeft className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />}
                </button>

                <div className="flex flex-col flex-1 min-h-0 overflow-y-auto">
                    <div className="px-5 py-4 flex items-center justify-center border-b border-slate-100 shrink-0">
                        {isCollapsed ? <VyteLogo iconOnly={true} className="h-9 w-auto" /> : <VyteLogo variant="dark-text" showSubtitle={true} className="w-full max-w-[185px] h-auto" />}
                    </div>

                    <div className="p-3.5 shrink-0">
                        {isCollapsed ? (
                            <button type="button" onClick={() => setIsCollapsed(false)} title="Search SKU or item" className="w-full py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 hover:bg-slate-100 border border-slate-200/80 flex items-center justify-center text-slate-500 cursor-pointer">
                                <Search className="w-4 h-4" />
                            </button>
                        ) : (
                            <div className="relative">
                                <Search className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400 absolute left-3 top-2.5" />
                                <input
                                    type="text" placeholder="Search SKU or item..." value={searchQuery} onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
                                    className="w-full pl-9 pr-3 py-2 bg-slate-50 text-slate-900 placeholder-slate-400 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-xs focus:outline-none focus:border-emerald-500 transition"
                                />
                            </div>
                        )}
                    </div>

                    <nav className="px-3 space-y-1.5">
                        {!isCollapsed && <p className="px-3 pt-1 pb-1 text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-400">CORE MODULES</p>}
                        {coreModules.map((mod) => {
                            const Icon = mod.icon;
                            const isActive = activeTab === mod.id || (mod.id === 'mod1' && activeTab === 'mod3');
                            return (
                                <button
                                    key={mod.id} onClick={() => setActiveTab(mod.id)} title={isCollapsed ? mod.label : undefined}
                                    className={`w-full flex items-center ${isCollapsed ? 'justify-center px-2 py-3' : 'justify-between px-3.5 py-2.5'} rounded-xl text-sm transition-all cursor-pointer border ${
                                        isActive ? 'bg-[#EBFDF2] border-[#A7F3D0] text-emerald-950 font-bold shadow-[0_1px_2px_rgba(16,185,129,0.06)]' : 'border-transparent text-slate-700 font-medium hover:bg-slate-50'
                                    }`}
                                >
                  <span className="flex items-center gap-3">
                    <Icon className={`w-4 h-4 shrink-0 ${mod.iconColor}`} />
                      {!isCollapsed && <span>{mod.label}</span>}
                  </span>
                                    {!isCollapsed && <span className={`text-xs px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full font-bold ${mod.badgeClass}`}>{mod.badge}</span>}
                                </button>
                            );
                        })}

                        {/* AI System Migrator èœå•å…¥å£ */}
                        {onOpenMigratorModal && (
                            <>
                                {!isCollapsed && <p className="px-3 pt-3 pb-1 text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-400">DATA MIGRATION</p>}
                                <button
                                    type="button"
                                    onClick={onOpenMigratorModal}
                                    title={isCollapsed ? 'AI System Migrator' : undefined}
                                    className={`w-full flex items-center ${isCollapsed ? 'justify-center px-2 py-3' : 'justify-between px-3.5 py-2.5'} rounded-xl text-sm transition-all cursor-pointer border border-emerald-200 bg-emerald-50 hover:bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-950 font-bold shadow-2xs`}
                                >
                                    <span className="flex items-center gap-3">
                                        <Database className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0 text-emerald-600" />
                                        {!isCollapsed && <span>AI System Migrator</span>}
                                    </span>
                                    {!isCollapsed && <span className="text-[10px] px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full font-bold bg-emerald-200 text-emerald-800">AI</span>}
                                </button>
                            </>
                        )}
                    </nav>
                </div>

                <div className="p-3 border-t border-slate-100 space-y-2 shrink-0">
                    <div title={roleBadge.label} className={`w-full rounded-xl border text-[11px] font-bold flex items-center ${isCollapsed ? 'justify-center py-2 px-1' : 'justify-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2'} ${roleBadge.style}`}>
                        <ShieldCheck className="w-3.5 h-3.5 shrink-0" />
                        {!isCollapsed && <span className="truncate">{roleBadge.label}</span>}
                    </div>

                    {isCollapsed ? (
                        <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-2">
                            <button type="button" onClick={() => setShowSettingsModal(true)} title="Workspace Settings" className="w-full py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 hover:bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-900 border border-slate-200/80 flex items-center justify-center transition cursor-pointer"><Settings className="w-4 h-4" /></button>
                            <button type="button" onClick={onLogout} title="Log Out" className="w-full py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-rose-50/70 hover:bg-rose-100 text-rose-600 border border-rose-200/70 flex items-center justify-center transition cursor-pointer"><LogOut className="w-4 h-4" /></button>
                        </div>
                    ) : (
                        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
                            <button type="button" onClick={() => setShowSettingsModal(true)} className="py-2 px-3 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 hover:bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 hover:text-slate-900 border border-slate-200/90 text-xs font-bold flex items-center justify-center gap-2 transition cursor-pointer">
                                <Settings className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-500" /><span>Settings</span>
                            </button>
                            <button type="button" onClick={onLogout} className="py-2 px-3 rounded-xl bg-rose-50/70 hover:bg-rose-100 text-rose-600 border border-rose-200/80 text-xs font-bold flex items-center justify-center gap-2 transition cursor-pointer">
                                <LogOut className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /><span>Log Out</span>
                            </button>
                        </div>
                    )}
                </div>
            </aside>

            {showSettingsModal && (
                <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-slate-900/50 backdrop-blur-xs flex items-center justify-center p-4 z-50">
                    <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 max-w-lg w-full p-6 shadow-2xl space-y-4 max-h-[92vh] overflow-y-auto">
                        <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-100 pb-3">
                            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                                <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-600 flex items-center justify-center"><Sliders className="w-4 h-4" /></div>
                                <div>
                                    <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900">Workspace Settings</h3>
                                    <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">System configurations &amp; operational preferences</p>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                            <button type="button" onClick={() => setShowSettingsModal(false)} className="p-1.5 rounded-lg text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-100 transition cursor-pointer"><X className="w-4 h-4" /></button>
                        </div>

                        <div className="space-y-3 text-xs">
                            <div className="p-3.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 space-y-1">
                                <p className="text-[11px] font-bold uppercase text-slate-400">Authenticated Session</p>
                                <p className="font-bold text-slate-900">{userSession?.userName || 'Vyte Operator'} ({userSession?.userID || 'M067'})</p>
                                <p className="text-slate-600">{userSession?.companyName}</p>
                                <p className="text-emerald-700 font-semibold">Role: {userSession?.userType} ({userSession?.role})</p>
                            </div>

                            <div className="p-3.5 rounded-xl bg-amber-50/70 border border-amber-200 space-y-2.5">
                                <label className="flex items-center justify-between cursor-pointer">
                                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
                                        <Bug className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-600 shrink-0" />
                                        <div>
                                            <p className="font-bold text-slate-900">Debug Mode (Use Local Mock Data)</p>
                                            <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-600">When enabled, bypasses Ktor backend and uses built-in mock dataset</p>
                                        </div>
                                    </div>
                                    <input type="checkbox" checked={tempDebugMode} onChange={(e) => setTempDebugMode(e.target.checked)} className="w-4 h-4 accent-[#2BB656] cursor-pointer" />
                                </label>
                                {!tempDebugMode && (
                                    <div className="pt-1 border-t border-amber-200/70 flex items-center gap-2">
                                        <Server className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-500 shrink-0" />
                                        <input
                                            type="text" value={tempServerUrl} onChange={(e) => setTempServerUrl(e.target.value)}
                                            placeholder="http://localhost:8080"
                                            className="flex-1 px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-white border border-slate-300 text-xs font-mono text-slate-800 focus:outline-none focus:border-emerald-500"
                                        />
                                    </div>
                                )}
                            </div>

                            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 pt-1">
                                <div>
                                    <label className="font-bold text-slate-700 block mb-1">Operating Currency</label>
                                    <VyteDropdown<CurrencyCode>
                                        variant="light" size="sm" value={tempCurrency} onChange={setTempCurrency}
                                        options={[
                                            { label: 'MYR (RM) - Malaysia', value: 'MYR' },
                                            { label: 'SGD (S$) - Singapore', value: 'SGD' },
                                            { label: 'USD ($) - International', value: 'USD' },
                                        ]}
                                    />
                                </div>
                                <div>
                                    <label className="font-bold text-slate-700 block mb-1">Default AI Turnover</label>
                                    <VyteDropdown<'7' | '14' | '21' | '30' | '60'>
                                        variant="light" size="sm" value={defaultTurnoverTarget} onChange={setDefaultTurnoverTarget}
                                        options={[
                                            { label: '7 Days (Fast FMCG)', value: '7' },
                                            { label: '14 Days (Retail Regular)', value: '14' },
                                            { label: '21 Days (Vyte Optimal)', value: '21' },
                                            { label: '30 Days (Standard)', value: '30' },
                                            { label: '60 Days (Slow B2B)', value: '60' },
                                        ]}
                                    />
                                </div>
                            </div>

                            <div className="space-y-2 pt-2">
                                {toggleItems.map(({ key, icon: Icon, title, desc }) => (
                                    <label key={key} className="flex items-center justify-between p-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 cursor-pointer hover:bg-slate-50">
                                        <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
                                            <Icon className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-600 shrink-0" />
                                            <div>
                                                <p className="font-bold text-slate-800">{title}</p>
                                                <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-500">{desc}</p>
                                            </div>
                                        </div>
                                        <input
                                            type="checkbox" checked={prefs[key]} onChange={(e) => setPrefs((p) => ({ ...p, [key]: e.target.checked }))}
                                            className="w-4 h-4 accent-[#2BB656] cursor-pointer"
                                        />
                                    </label>
                                ))}
                            </div>
                        </div>

                        <div className="flex justify-end pt-3 border-t border-slate-100">
                            <button type="button" onClick={handleSaveSettings} className="px-5 py-2 bg-[#2BB656] hover:bg-emerald-600 text-white text-xs font-bold rounded-xl transition cursor-pointer shadow-xs">
                                Save Preferences
                            </button>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            )}
        </>
    );
};

========== FILE: E:\Code\Vyte\orbyteflow\src\components\migration\UniversalSystemMigratorModal.tsx ==========

// src/components/migration/UniversalSystemMigratorModal.tsx
import React, { useState, useRef } from 'react';
import {
    Upload, Database, Sparkles, CheckCircle2, AlertCircle,
    ArrowRight, ShieldCheck, Loader2, X, Sliders
} from 'lucide-react';
import type { Product, InvoiceItem, BusinessComplianceProfile } from '../../types/vyte.ts';
import { orbyteApi } from '../../services/api';

interface UniversalSystemMigratorModalProps {
    isOpen: boolean;
    onClose: () => void;
    onImportSuccess: (payload: {
        products: Product[];
        invoices: InvoiceItem[];
        complianceProfiles: BusinessComplianceProfile[];
        sourceFileName: string;
        transitionMode: 'SHADOW' | 'COPILOT' | 'AUTOPILOT';
    }) => void;
}

interface AiParsedPreview {
    detectedSystem: string;
    fileFormat: string;
    aiConfidence: number;
    fieldMappings: { sourceField: string; targetVyteField: string; sampleValue: string }[];
    products: Product[];
    invoices: InvoiceItem[];
    complianceProfiles: BusinessComplianceProfile[];
    aiSummary: string;
}

export const UniversalSystemMigratorModal: React.FC<UniversalSystemMigratorModalProps> = ({
                                                                                              isOpen,
                                                                                              onClose,
                                                                                              onImportSuccess,
                                                                                          }) => {
    const fileInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
    const [selectedFile, setSelectedFile] = useState<File | null>(null);
    const [isAnalyzing, setIsAnalyzing] = useState(false);
    const [isImporting, setIsImporting] = useState(false);
    const [transitionMode, setTransitionMode] = useState<'SHADOW' | 'COPILOT' | 'AUTOPILOT'>('COPILOT');
    const [preview, setPreview] = useState<AiParsedPreview | null>(null);
    const [errorMsg, setErrorMsg] = useState<string | null>(null);

    if (!isOpen) return null;

    // Read any file (.md, .db, .sql, .json, .csv, etc.) and send to Backend AI / Local AI Parser
    const handleFileChange = async (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
        const file = e.target.files?.[0];
        if (!file) return;
        setSelectedFile(file);
        setErrorMsg(null);
        setIsAnalyzing(true);

        try {
            const ext = file.name.split('.').pop()?.toLowerCase() || 'unknown';
            const rawContent = await readFileContentSafely(file, ext);

            // Call backend AI endpoint (with automatic fallback for Hackathon live demo)
            const parsedResult = await orbyteApi.parseLegacySystemFile(file, rawContent);
            setPreview(parsedResult);
        } catch (err) {
            setErrorMsg(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'AI failed to parse file.');
        } finally {
            setIsAnalyzing(false);
            e.target.value = '';
        }
    };

    // Safely extract readable strings even from binary SQLite .db files or .md/.sql files
    const readFileContentSafely = (file: File, ext: string): Promise<string> => {
        return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
            const reader = new FileReader();
            if (ext === 'db' || ext === 'sqlite' || ext === 'sqlite3') {
                reader.onload = () => {
                    const buffer = reader.result as ArrayBuffer;
                    const bytes = new Uint8Array(buffer.slice(0, 65536)); // Read first 64KB
                    // Extract printable ASCII/UTF-8 strings from SQLite binary pages (CREATE TABLE & row text)
                    let extractedStrings = '';
                    let currentChunk = '';
                    for (let i = 0; i < bytes.length; i++) {
                        const code = bytes[i];
                        if (code >= 32 && code <= 126) {
                            currentChunk += String.fromCharCode(code);
                        } else {
                            if (currentChunk.length >= 4) extractedStrings += currentChunk + '\n';
                            currentChunk = '';
                        }
                    }
                    resolve(extractedStrings || `SQLite Binary Database (${file.name})`);
                };
                reader.onerror = () => reject(new Error('Failed to read binary database file.'));
                reader.readAsArrayBuffer(file);
            } else {
                reader.onload = () => resolve(String(reader.result || '').slice(0, 50000));
                reader.onerror = () => reject(new Error('Failed to read file text.'));
                reader.readAsText(file);
            }
        });
    };

    const handleConfirmMigration = async () => {
        if (!preview || !selectedFile) return;
        setIsImporting(true);
        await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 650));
        onImportSuccess({
            products: preview.products,
            invoices: preview.invoices,
            complianceProfiles: preview.complianceProfiles,
            sourceFileName: selectedFile.name,
            transitionMode,
        });
        setIsImporting(false);
        setPreview(null);
        setSelectedFile(null);
        onClose();
    };

    return (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-slate-900/65 backdrop-blur-xs flex items-center justify-center p-4 z-50 select-none">
            <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 max-w-3xl w-full p-6 shadow-2xl space-y-5 max-h-[92vh] overflow-y-auto">
                {/* Header */}
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-100 pb-4">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                        <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-200 text-[#2BB656] flex items-center justify-center">
                            <Database className="w-5 h-5" />
                        </div>
                        <div>
                            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                                <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900">
                                    AI Universal Legacy System Migrator
                                </h3>
                                <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800 text-[10px] font-bold">
                  Zero Switching Cost
                </span>
                            </div>
                            <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">
                                Upload any file from your current software (.db, .sqlite, .md, .sql, .json, .csv, SQL Accounting, AutoCount) â€” Vyte AI maps &amp; imports it automatically.
                            </p>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                    <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={onClose}
                        className="p-1.5 rounded-lg text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-100 cursor-pointer"
                    >
                        <X className="w-4 h-4" />
                    </button>
                </div>

                {/* Mentor Point #2 & #3: Smooth Transition Mode Selector (Overcoming SME Resistance) */}
                <div className="p-4 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 space-y-2.5">
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <span className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-800 flex items-center gap-1.5">
              <Sliders className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-600" />
              Step 1: Choose Smooth Transition Pace (Zero Disruption to Staff Habits)
            </span>
                        <span className="text-[11px] text-emerald-700 font-semibold">
              Solves Change Resistance
            </span>
                    </div>
                    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-2.5 text-xs">
                        {[
                            {
                                id: 'SHADOW' as const,
                                title: '1. Shadow Sync Mode',
                                desc: 'Keep using your old software & WhatsApp. Vyte syncs silently in the background.',
                            },
                            {
                                id: 'COPILOT' as const,
                                title: '2. AI Co-Pilot (Recommended)',
                                desc: 'AI drafts POs, orders & reminders; boss approves in 1 click on WhatsApp/Web.',
                            },
                            {
                                id: 'AUTOPILOT' as const,
                                title: '3. Full Auto-Pilot',
                                desc: 'Autonomous restock & payment chasing within your safety thresholds.',
                            },
                        ].map((m) => (
                            <button
                                key={m.id}
                                type="button"
                                onClick={() => setTransitionMode(m.id)}
                                className={`p-3 rounded-xl border text-left transition cursor-pointer ${
                                    transitionMode === m.id
                                        ? 'bg-emerald-50/90 border-[#2BB656] text-emerald-950 shadow-2xs'
                                        : 'bg-white border-slate-200 text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-100'
                                }`}
                            >
                                <p className="font-bold text-xs">{m.title}</p>
                                <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 mt-1 leading-relaxed">{m.desc}</p>
                            </button>
                        ))}
                    </div>
                </div>

                {/* Step 2: Universal Dropzone */}
                <div className="space-y-3">
                    <input
                        ref={fileInputRef}
                        type="file"
                        accept="*/*,.md,.db,.sqlite,.sqlite3,.sql,.json,.csv,.xlsx,.xls,.txt,.xml"
                        onChange={handleFileChange}
                        className="hidden"
                    />

                    <div
                        onClick={() => fileInputRef.current?.click()}
                        className="border-2 border-dashed border-emerald-300 hover:border-[#2BB656] bg-emerald-50/30 hover:bg-emerald-50/60 rounded-2xl p-6 text-center transition cursor-pointer space-y-2"
                    >
                        <div className="w-11 h-11 rounded-2xl bg-[#2BB656]/15 text-[#2BB656] flex items-center justify-center mx-auto">
                            {isAnalyzing ? <Loader2 className="w-5 h-5 animate-spin" /> : <Upload className="w-5 h-5" />}
                        </div>
                        <p className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-900">
                            {isAnalyzing
                                ? `Vyte AI is deconstructing "${selectedFile?.name}" & mapping database schema...`
                                : 'Click to Upload Any Legacy System File (.db, .md, .sql, .json, .csv, .xlsx, .txt)'}
                        </p>
                        <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-500">
                            Supports SQLite raw <code className="font-mono bg-white px-1 rounded">.db</code> files, Markdown notes <code className="font-mono bg-white px-1 rounded">.md</code>, SQL dumps, and ERP exports.
                        </p>
                    </div>
                </div>

                {errorMsg && (
                    <div className="p-3 rounded-xl bg-rose-50 border border-rose-200 text-rose-700 text-xs font-semibold flex items-center gap-2">
                        <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" />
                        <span>{errorMsg}</span>
                    </div>
                )}

                {/* Step 3: AI Schema Mapping & Data Preview */}
                {preview && (
                    <div className="p-4 rounded-2xl bg-slate-900 text-slate-100 space-y-4 border border-slate-800">
                        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 border-b border-slate-800 pb-3">
                            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                                <Sparkles className="w-4 h-4 text-[#2BB656]" />
                                <span className="text-xs font-bold text-white">
                  Detected Source: {preview.detectedSystem} ({preview.fileFormat})
                </span>
                            </div>
                            <span className="px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-300 border border-emerald-500/30 text-[11px] font-bold">
                AI Schema Match: {preview.aiConfidence}%
              </span>
                        </div>

                        <p className="text-xs text-slate-300 leading-relaxed">{preview.aiSummary}</p>

                        {/* Field Mappings */}
                        <div className="space-y-1.5">
                            <p className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-400">
                                AI Automatic Schema Mapping (Old System &rarr; Vyte DB)
                            </p>
                            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-2 text-[11px]">
                                {preview.fieldMappings.map((m, idx) => (
                                    <div
                                        key={idx}
                                        className="px-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-slate-800/90 border border-slate-700 flex items-center justify-between gap-2"
                                    >
                                        <span className="font-mono text-amber-300 truncate">{m.sourceField}</span>
                                        <ArrowRight className="w-3 h-3 text-slate-400 shrink-0" />
                                        <span className="font-mono font-bold text-emerald-400 truncate">
                      {m.targetVyteField}
                    </span>
                                    </div>
                                ))}
                            </div>
                        </div>

                        {/* Extracted Counts */}
                        <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2.5 text-center pt-1">
                            <div className="p-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-800/60 border border-slate-700">
                                <p className="text-lg font-extrabold text-emerald-400">{preview.products.length}</p>
                                <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-semibold">Inventory SKUs Mapped</p>
                            </div>
                            <div className="p-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-800/60 border border-slate-700">
                                <p className="text-lg font-extrabold text-blue-400">{preview.invoices.length}</p>
                                <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-semibold">Unpaid Invoices / AR</p>
                            </div>
                            <div className="p-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-800/60 border border-slate-700">
                                <p className="text-lg font-extrabold text-amber-400">
                                    {preview.complianceProfiles.length}
                                </p>
                                <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-semibold">
                                    Licenses &amp; Warranties
                                </p>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                )}

                {/* Footer Actions */}
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between pt-3 border-t border-slate-100">
          <span className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 flex items-center gap-1.5">
            <ShieldCheck className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-600" />
            Existing data is non-destructively merged with rollback protection.
          </span>
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
                        <button
                            type="button"
                            onClick={onClose}
                            className="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 text-slate-700 text-xs font-semibold cursor-pointer"
                        >
                            Cancel
                        </button>
                        <button
                            type="button"
                            disabled={!preview || isImporting}
                            onClick={() => void handleConfirmMigration()}
                            className="px-5 py-2 rounded-xl bg-[#2BB656] hover:bg-emerald-600 disabled:opacity-50 text-white text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-1.5 shadow-xs transition cursor-pointer"
                        >
                            {isImporting ? <Loader2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 animate-spin" /> : <CheckCircle2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />}
                            <span>
                {isImporting ? 'Writing to Vyte Database...' : 'Import Parsed Data into Vyte'}
              </span>
                        </button>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    );
};
