========== FILE: E:\Code\Vyte\orbyteflow\src\modules\module1-restock\AddProductModal.tsx ==========

// src/modules/module1-restock/AddProductModal.tsx
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { AlertCircle } from 'lucide-react';
import type { CurrencyCode, IndustryProfile, NewProductForm } from '../../types/vyte.ts';
import { CURRENCY_SYMBOLS } from '../../types/vyte.ts';
import { VyteDropdown } from '../../components/common/VyteDropdown';

interface AddProductModalProps {
    isOpen: boolean;
    currency?: CurrencyCode;
    onClose: () => void;
    onSaveProduct: (form: NewProductForm) => void;
}

interface FormDraft {
    name: string; sku: string; category: string; industry: IndustryProfile; uom: string;
    stock: string; minThreshold: string; reorderQty: string; priceRM: string; unitCostRM: string;
    supplierName: string; supplierContact: string; batchNo: string; expiryDate: string;
    storageCondition: 'Ambient' | 'Chilled' | 'Frozen'; variantSpec: string; b2bMoq: string; serialOrWarranty: string;
}

const DRAFT_KEY = 'vyte_add_product_draft_v2';
const MY_PHONE_RE = /^(\+?60|0)1[0-9]-?[0-9]{7,8}$/;

export const AddProductModal: React.FC<AddProductModalProps> = ({ isOpen, currency = 'MYR', onClose, onSaveProduct }) => {
    const sym = CURRENCY_SYMBOLS[currency] || 'RM';
    const [draft, setDraft] = useState<FormDraft>(() => {
        try { const s = localStorage.getItem(DRAFT_KEY); if (s) return JSON.parse(s); } catch { /* ignore */ }
        return {
            name: '', sku: '', category: 'F&B Raw Ingredient', industry: 'FNB_PERISHABLE', uom: 'kg',
            stock: '', minThreshold: '', reorderQty: '', priceRM: '', unitCostRM: '', supplierName: '',
            supplierContact: '', batchNo: '', expiryDate: '', storageCondition: 'Chilled', variantSpec: '', b2bMoq: '', serialOrWarranty: '',
        };
    });
    const [errors, setErrors] = useState<Partial<Record<keyof FormDraft, string>>>({});

    useEffect(() => { localStorage.setItem(DRAFT_KEY, JSON.stringify(draft)); }, [draft]);
    if (!isOpen) return null;

    const validateField = (f: keyof FormDraft, val: string, d: FormDraft): string => {
        const v = val.trim();
        switch (f) {
            case 'name': return !v ? 'Product name is required.' : v.length < 3 ? 'Product name must be at least 3 characters.' : '';
            case 'sku': return v && !/^[A-Za-z0-9-_]{3,20}$/.test(v) ? 'SKU must be 3-20 alphanumeric characters (e.g., FNB-4886).' : '';
            case 'priceRM': return !v ? 'Selling price is required.' : v.startsWith('0') ? 'Selling price cannot start with 0.' : !/^[1-9]\d*(\.\d{1,2})?$/.test(v) ? 'Enter a valid price greater than 0 (e.g., 45.00).' : Number(v) > 1000000 ? `Selling price cannot exceed ${sym} 1,000,000.` : '';
            case 'stock': return !v ? 'Initial current stock is required.' : !/^\d+$/.test(v) ? 'Initial stock must be whole numbers (0-9).' : Number(v) > 999999 ? 'Initial stock cannot exceed 999,999.' : '';
            case 'minThreshold': return !v ? 'Alert threshold (min stock) is required.' : v.startsWith('0') || !/^[1-9]\d*$/.test(v) ? 'Alert threshold cannot start with 0 and must be at least 1.' : Number(v) > 99999 ? 'Alert threshold cannot exceed 99,999.' : '';
            case 'reorderQty': return !v ? 'Default reorder quantity is required.' : v.startsWith('0') || !/^[1-9]\d*$/.test(v) ? 'Reorder quantity cannot start with 0 and must be at least 1.' : '';
            case 'supplierName': return !v ? 'Supplier business name is required.' : v.length < 2 ? 'Supplier name must be at least 2 characters.' : '';
            case 'supplierContact': return !v ? 'Supplier WhatsApp number is required.' : !MY_PHONE_RE.test(v) ? 'Invalid WhatsApp number. Use MY format (e.g., +6012-3456789).' : '';
            case 'batchNo': return d.industry === 'FNB_PERISHABLE' && !v ? 'Batch number is required for F&B items.' : '';
            case 'expiryDate': return d.industry === 'FNB_PERISHABLE' && !v ? 'Expiry date is required for F&B items.' : '';
            case 'variantSpec': return d.industry === 'RETAIL_VARIANT' && !v ? 'Variant specification is required.' : '';
            case 'b2bMoq': return d.industry === 'B2B_RAW_MATERIAL' && !v ? 'B2B MOQ and lead time constraint is required.' : '';
            default: return '';
        }
    };

    const handleChange = (f: keyof FormDraft, val: string) => {
        const next = { ...draft, [f]: val };
        setDraft(next);
        setErrors((p) => ({ ...p, [f]: validateField(f, val, next) }));
    };

    const handleSubmit = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        const keys: (keyof FormDraft)[] = ['name', 'sku', 'priceRM', 'stock', 'minThreshold', 'reorderQty', 'supplierName', 'supplierContact', 'batchNo', 'expiryDate', 'variantSpec', 'b2bMoq'];
        const nextErrs: Partial<Record<keyof FormDraft, string>> = {};
        keys.forEach((k) => { const err = validateField(k, draft[k], draft); if (err) nextErrs[k] = err; });
        setErrors(nextErrs);
        if (Object.keys(nextErrs).length > 0) return;

        const extraAttr = draft.industry === 'FNB_PERISHABLE'
            ? `Batch: ${draft.batchNo.trim()} | Exp: ${draft.expiryDate.trim()} | Storage: ${draft.storageCondition}`
            : draft.industry === 'RETAIL_VARIANT' ? draft.variantSpec.trim() : draft.b2bMoq.trim();

        onSaveProduct({
            name: draft.name.trim(), sku: draft.sku.trim(), category: draft.category, industry: draft.industry, uom: draft.uom,
            stock: Number(draft.stock), minThreshold: Number(draft.minThreshold), reorderQty: Number(draft.reorderQty),
            priceRM: Number(draft.priceRM), supplierName: draft.supplierName.trim(), supplierContact: draft.supplierContact.trim(), extraAttr,
        });
        onClose();
    };

    const inputCls = (f: keyof FormDraft) =>
        `w-full px-3 py-2 bg-white text-slate-900 placeholder-slate-400 border rounded-xl text-xs font-medium focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 transition ${
            errors[f] ? 'border-rose-500 focus:border-rose-500 focus:ring-rose-500/20 bg-rose-50/20' : 'border-slate-300 focus:border-[#2BB656] focus:ring-[#2BB656]/20'
        }`;

    const errEl = (f: keyof FormDraft) => errors[f] ? <p className="text-[11px] text-rose-600 font-semibold mt-1 flex items-center gap-1"><AlertCircle className="w-3 h-3 shrink-0" /> {errors[f]}</p> : null;

    return (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-slate-900/60 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-center justify-center p-4 z-50 overflow-y-auto">
            <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} noValidate className="bg-white text-slate-900 rounded-2xl max-w-lg w-full p-6 space-y-4 shadow-2xl border border-slate-200 my-8">
                <div className="border-b border-slate-100 pb-3"><h3 className="text-lg font-bold text-slate-900">Add New Product</h3></div>
                <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 text-xs">
                    <div className="col-span-2">
                        <label className="font-semibold text-slate-700 block mb-1">Industry Profile</label>
                        <VyteDropdown<IndustryProfile>
                            variant="light" value={draft.industry}
                            onChange={(ind) => {
                                setDraft({
                                    ...draft, industry: ind,
                                    category: ind === 'FNB_PERISHABLE' ? 'F&B Raw Ingredient' : ind === 'RETAIL_VARIANT' ? 'Apparel & Accessories' : 'Packaging & Raw Material',
                                    uom: ind === 'FNB_PERISHABLE' ? 'kg' : ind === 'RETAIL_VARIANT' ? 'pcs' : 'box',
                                });
                                setErrors({});
                            }}
                            options={[
                                { label: 'F&B / Cafe / Grocery (Batch & Expiry)', value: 'FNB_PERISHABLE' },
                                { label: 'Retail & Boutique (Sizes & Colors)', value: 'RETAIL_VARIANT' },
                                { label: 'B2B Manufacturing (MOQ & Lead Time)', value: 'B2B_RAW_MATERIAL' },
                            ]}
                        />
                    </div>

                    <div className="col-span-2 sm:col-span-1">
                        <label className="font-semibold text-slate-700 block mb-1">Product Name *</label>
                        <input type="text" placeholder="e.g., Premium Matcha Powder" value={draft.name} onChange={(e) => handleChange('name', e.target.value)} className={inputCls('name')} />
                        {errEl('name')}
                    </div>
                    <div className="col-span-2 sm:col-span-1">
                        <label className="font-semibold text-slate-700 block mb-1">SKU / Barcode</label>
                        <input type="text" placeholder="e.g., FNB-4886" value={draft.sku} onChange={(e) => handleChange('sku', e.target.value)} className={inputCls('sku')} />
                        {errEl('sku')}
                    </div>

                    <div>
                        <label className="font-semibold text-slate-700 block mb-1">Unit of Measure (UOM)</label>
                        <VyteDropdown<string>
                            variant="light" value={draft.uom} onChange={(v) => handleChange('uom', v)}
                            options={[
                                { label: 'Pieces (pcs)', value: 'pcs' }, { label: 'Kilograms (kg)', value: 'kg' },
                                { label: 'Liters (L)', value: 'L' }, { label: 'Boxes (box)', value: 'box' }, { label: 'Packs (pack)', value: 'pack' },
                            ]}
                        />
                    </div>
                    <div>
                        <label className="font-semibold text-slate-700 block mb-1">Selling Price ({currency}) *</label>
                        <input type="text" placeholder="e.g., 45.00" value={draft.priceRM} onChange={(e) => handleChange('priceRM', e.target.value)} className={inputCls('priceRM')} />
                        {errEl('priceRM')}
                    </div>

                    <div>
                        <label className="font-semibold text-slate-700 block mb-1">Initial Current Stock *</label>
                        <input type="text" placeholder="e.g., 10" value={draft.stock} onChange={(e) => handleChange('stock', e.target.value)} className={inputCls('stock')} />
                        {errEl('stock')}
                    </div>
                    <div>
                        <label className="font-semibold text-slate-700 block mb-1">Alert Threshold (Min Stock) *</label>
                        <input type="text" placeholder="e.g., 15" value={draft.minThreshold} onChange={(e) => handleChange('minThreshold', e.target.value)} className={inputCls('minThreshold')} />
                        {errEl('minThreshold')}
                    </div>

                    <div className="col-span-2">
                        <label className="font-semibold text-slate-700 block mb-1">Default Reorder Quantity *</label>
                        <input type="text" placeholder="e.g., 30" value={draft.reorderQty} onChange={(e) => handleChange('reorderQty', e.target.value)} className={inputCls('reorderQty')} />
                        {errEl('reorderQty')}
                    </div>

                    <div>
                        <label className="font-semibold text-slate-700 block mb-1">Supplier Business Name *</label>
                        <input type="text" placeholder="e.g., Uji Wholesale MY" value={draft.supplierName} onChange={(e) => handleChange('supplierName', e.target.value)} className={inputCls('supplierName')} />
                        {errEl('supplierName')}
                    </div>
                    <div>
                        <label className="font-semibold text-slate-700 block mb-1">Supplier WhatsApp Number *</label>
                        <input type="tel" placeholder="e.g., +6012-3456789" value={draft.supplierContact} onChange={(e) => handleChange('supplierContact', e.target.value)} className={inputCls('supplierContact')} />
                        {errEl('supplierContact')}
                    </div>

                    {draft.industry === 'FNB_PERISHABLE' && (
                        <>
                            <div>
                                <label className="font-semibold text-slate-700 block mb-1">Batch Number *</label>
                                <input type="text" placeholder="e.g., B-2026-10" value={draft.batchNo} onChange={(e) => handleChange('batchNo', e.target.value)} className={inputCls('batchNo')} />
                                {errEl('batchNo')}
                            </div>
                            <div>
                                <label className="font-semibold text-slate-700 block mb-1">Expiry Date *</label>
                                <input type="date" value={draft.expiryDate} onChange={(e) => handleChange('expiryDate', e.target.value)} className={inputCls('expiryDate')} />
                                {errEl('expiryDate')}
                            </div>
                            <div className="col-span-2">
                                <label className="font-semibold text-slate-700 block mb-1">Storage Condition</label>
                                <VyteDropdown<'Ambient' | 'Chilled' | 'Frozen'>
                                    variant="light" value={draft.storageCondition} onChange={(v) => setDraft({ ...draft, storageCondition: v })}
                                    options={[
                                        { label: 'Ambient / Room Temperature', value: 'Ambient' },
                                        { label: 'Chilled (2\u00B0C - 4\u00B0C)', value: 'Chilled' },
                                        { label: 'Frozen (-18\u00B0C)', value: 'Frozen' },
                                    ]}
                                />
                            </div>
                        </>
                    )}

                    {draft.industry === 'RETAIL_VARIANT' && (
                        <>
                            <div className="col-span-2">
                                <label className="font-semibold text-slate-700 block mb-1">Variant Specification *</label>
                                <input type="text" placeholder="e.g., Size: L | Color: Matte Black" value={draft.variantSpec} onChange={(e) => handleChange('variantSpec', e.target.value)} className={inputCls('variantSpec')} />
                                {errEl('variantSpec')}
                            </div>
                            <div className="col-span-2">
                                <label className="font-semibold text-slate-700 block mb-1">Serial / Warranty Info</label>
                                <input type="text" placeholder="e.g., 1-Year Local Manufacturer Warranty" value={draft.serialOrWarranty} onChange={(e) => setDraft({ ...draft, serialOrWarranty: e.target.value })} className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-white border border-slate-300 rounded-xl text-xs font-medium focus:outline-none focus:border-[#2BB656]" />
                            </div>
                        </>
                    )}

                    {draft.industry === 'B2B_RAW_MATERIAL' && (
                        <div className="col-span-2">
                            <label className="font-semibold text-slate-700 block mb-1">B2B Order Constraints *</label>
                            <input type="text" placeholder="e.g., MOQ: 20 Boxes | Lead Time: 3 Days" value={draft.b2bMoq} onChange={(e) => handleChange('b2bMoq', e.target.value)} className={inputCls('b2bMoq')} />
                            {errEl('b2bMoq')}
                        </div>
                    )}
                </div>

                <div className="flex justify-end gap-2.5 pt-3 border-t border-slate-100">
                    <button type="button" onClick={onClose} className="px-4 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-200 bg-slate-100 rounded-xl transition cursor-pointer">Cancel</button>
                    <button type="submit" className="px-4 py-2 text-xs font-bold text-white bg-[#2BB656] hover:bg-emerald-600 rounded-xl shadow-xs transition cursor-pointer">Save Product</button>
                </div>
            </form>
        </div>
    );
};

========== FILE: E:\Code\Vyte\orbyteflow\src\modules\module1-restock\RestockModule.tsx ==========

// src/modules/module1-restock/RestockModule.tsx
import React, { useState, useRef, useEffect } from 'react';
import {
    Plus, Send, CheckCircle2, Clock, AlertTriangle, ShoppingBag,
    Lock, AlertCircle, Loader2, FileSpreadsheet, Camera, X, Database,
} from 'lucide-react';
import type { CurrencyCode, Product, UserRole } from '../../types/vyte.ts';
import { CURRENCY_SYMBOLS } from '../../types/vyte.ts';

interface RestockModuleProps {
    products: Product[];
    role: UserRole;
    currency?: CurrencyCode;
    exchangeRate?: number;
    onApproveAndContactSupplier: (productId: string, customReorderQty?: number) => Promise<void> | void;
    onConfirmSupplierDelivery: (productId: string) => Promise<void> | void;
    onRecordStockUsage: (productId: string, usedQty: number) => void;
    onOpenAddModal: () => void;
    onBatchImportProducts?: (imported: Product[]) => void;
    onOpenMigratorModal?: () => void; // æ–°å¢žï¼šè§¦å‘æ—§ç³»ç»Ÿä¸€é”®è¿ç§» Modal
}

const USAGE_KEY = 'vyte_restock_usage_inputs_v2';
const REORDER_KEY = 'vyte_restock_reorder_inputs_v2';
const sanitizeInt = (raw: string, maxDigits = 5) => raw.replace(/[^0-9]/g, '').replace(/^0+/, '').slice(0, maxDigits);

export const RestockModule: React.FC<RestockModuleProps> = ({
                                                                products, role, currency = 'MYR', exchangeRate = 1.0,
                                                                onApproveAndContactSupplier, onConfirmSupplierDelivery, onRecordStockUsage, onOpenAddModal, onBatchImportProducts, onOpenMigratorModal,
                                                            }) => {
    const sym = CURRENCY_SYMBOLS[currency] || 'RM';

    const [usageInputs, setUsageInputs] = useState<Record<string, string>>(() => {
        try { const s = localStorage.getItem(USAGE_KEY); return s ? JSON.parse(s) : {}; } catch { return {}; }
    });
    const [usageErrors, setUsageErrors] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});

    const [reorderInputs, setReorderInputs] = useState<Record<string, string>>(() => {
        try { const s = localStorage.getItem(REORDER_KEY); return s ? JSON.parse(s) : {}; } catch { return {}; }
    });
    const [reorderErrors, setReorderErrors] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});

    const [pendingSaleConfirm, setPendingSaleConfirm] = useState<{ item: Product; qty: number } | null>(null);
    const [approvingIds, setApprovingIds] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>({});
    const [confirmingIds, setConfirmingIds] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>({});

    const excelInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
    const photoInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);

    useEffect(() => { localStorage.setItem(USAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(usageInputs)); }, [usageInputs]);
    useEffect(() => { localStorage.setItem(REORDER_KEY, JSON.stringify(reorderInputs)); }, [reorderInputs]);

    const handleSpreadsheetImport = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
        const file = e.target.files?.[0];
        if (!file || !onBatchImportProducts) return;
        const reader = new FileReader();
        reader.onload = () => {
            const lines = String(reader.result || '').split(/\r?\n/).map((l) => l.trim()).filter(Boolean);
            const imported: Product[] = [];
            lines.forEach((line, idx) => {
                if (idx === 0 && line.toLowerCase().includes('sku')) return;
                const cols = line.split(',').map((c) => c.trim().replace(/^"|"$/g, ''));
                if (cols.length >= 4) {
                    const stockNum = Number(cols[2]) || 8;
                    const minNum = Number(cols[3]) || 15;
                    imported.push({
                        id: `P-XLS-${Date.now()}-${idx}`, sku: cols[0] || `XLS-${1000 + idx}`,
                        name: cols[1] || `Imported SKU #${idx + 1}`, category: cols[4] || 'F&B Raw Ingredient',
                        industry: 'FNB_PERISHABLE', uom: cols[5] || 'kg', stock: stockNum, minThreshold: minNum,
                        reorderQty: Number(cols[6]) || 30, priceRM: Number(cols[7]) || 52.0, unitCostRM: 31.0,
                        dailyDepletionRate: 3.5, leadTimeDays: 2, moq: 10,
                        supplierName: cols[8] || 'Verified Supplier MY', supplierContact: cols[9] || '+6012-8899221',
                        performance: 'Good', visibility: true, batchNo: `B-2026-X${idx}`, expiryDate: '2026-11-20',
                        restockStatus: stockNum <= minNum ? 'TRIGGERED_PENDING_APPROVAL' : 'HEALTHY',
                    });
                }
            });
            if (!imported.length) {
                imported.push({
                    id: `P-XLS-${Date.now()}`, sku: `XLS-${Math.floor(1000 + Math.random() * 9000)}`,
                    name: `Batch (${file.name.replace(/\.[^/.]+$/, '')})`, category: 'Packaging & Supplies',
                    industry: 'B2B_RAW_MATERIAL', uom: 'box', stock: 6, minThreshold: 12, reorderQty: 40,
                    priceRM: 58.0, unitCostRM: 34.0, dailyDepletionRate: 3.2, leadTimeDays: 2, moq: 15,
                    supplierName: 'EcoPack Malaysia', supplierContact: '+6017-8899001', performance: 'Good',
                    visibility: true, variantSpec: `Imported from ${file.name}`, restockStatus: 'TRIGGERED_PENDING_APPROVAL',
                });
            }
            onBatchImportProducts(imported);
            e.target.value = '';
        };
        reader.readAsText(file);
    };

    const handleShelfPhotoUpload = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
        const file = e.target.files?.[0];
        if (!file || !onBatchImportProducts) return;
        const reader = new FileReader();
        reader.onload = () => {
            onBatchImportProducts([{
                id: `P-IMG-${Date.now()}`, sku: `IMG-${Math.floor(1000 + Math.random() * 9000)}`,
                name: `Scanned Item (${file.name.slice(0, 14)})`, category: 'F&B Raw Ingredient',
                industry: 'FNB_PERISHABLE', uom: 'pack', stock: 5, minThreshold: 12, reorderQty: 30,
                priceRM: 38.0, unitCostRM: 22.0, dailyDepletionRate: 3.0, leadTimeDays: 2, moq: 10,
                supplierName: 'Klang Valley Roasters Sdn Bhd', supplierContact: '+6012-3456789',
                performance: 'Good', visibility: true, batchNo: 'B-PHOTO-26', expiryDate: '2026-11-10',
                photoUrl: String(reader.result), restockStatus: 'TRIGGERED_PENDING_APPROVAL',
            }]);
            e.target.value = '';
        };
        reader.readAsDataURL(file);
    };

    const handleRequestSaleConfirmation = (item: Product) => {
        if (item.stock <= 0) return;
        const rawStr = usageInputs[item.id] ?? '3';
        const qty = Number(rawStr);
        if (!rawStr || isNaN(qty) || qty < 1) return setUsageErrors((p) => ({ ...p, [item.id]: 'Enter at least 1.' }));
        if (qty > item.stock) return setUsageErrors((p) => ({ ...p, [item.id]: `Max available is ${item.stock} ${item.uom}.` }));
        setUsageErrors((p) => ({ ...p, [item.id]: '' }));
        setPendingSaleConfirm({ item, qty });
    };

    const handleApprovePO = async (item: Product) => {
        const rawReorder = reorderInputs[item.id] ?? String(item.reorderQty);
        const qty = Number(rawReorder);
        const minMoq = item.moq ?? 5;
        if (!rawReorder || isNaN(qty) || qty < minMoq) return setReorderErrors((p) => ({ ...p, [item.id]: `Min supplier order is ${minMoq} ${item.uom}.` }));
        if (qty > 10000) return setReorderErrors((p) => ({ ...p, [item.id]: 'Max single PO limit is 10,000 units.' }));

        setReorderErrors((p) => ({ ...p, [item.id]: '' }));
        setApprovingIds((p) => ({ ...p, [item.id]: true }));
        try { await onApproveAndContactSupplier(item.id, qty); } finally { setApprovingIds((p) => ({ ...p, [item.id]: false })); }
    };

    const handleConfirmDeliveryClick = async (item: Product) => {
        setConfirmingIds((p) => ({ ...p, [item.id]: true }));
        try { await onConfirmSupplierDelivery(item.id); } finally { setConfirmingIds((p) => ({ ...p, [item.id]: false })); }
    };

    const sortedProducts = React.useMemo(() => {
        return [...products].sort((a, b) => {
            const rank = (s: Product['restockStatus']) =>
                s === 'TRIGGERED_PENDING_APPROVAL' ? 0 : s === 'SUPPLIER_CONTACTED' ? 1 : s === 'CONFIRMED' ? 2 : 3;
            return rank(a.restockStatus) - rank(b.restockStatus);
        });
    }, [products]);

    return (
        <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200 shadow-xs overflow-hidden">
            <input ref={excelInputRef} type="file" accept=".csv,.xlsx,.xls" onChange={handleSpreadsheetImport} className="hidden" />
            <input ref={photoInputRef} type="file" accept="image/*" onChange={handleShelfPhotoUpload} className="hidden" />

            <div className="px-5 py-3.5 border-b border-slate-100 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
                <div>
                    <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900">Product Catalog &amp; Supplier Restock</h3>
                    <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-500">Manage SKU thresholds, record daily stock usage, and dispatch purchase orders.</p>
                </div>

                <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                    {onOpenMigratorModal && (
                        <button type="button" onClick={onOpenMigratorModal} className="px-3.5 py-1.5 bg-emerald-50 hover:bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800 border border-emerald-200 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-1.5 transition cursor-pointer">
                            <Database className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-[#2BB656]" /> Migrate Old System (.db / .md / ERP)
                        </button>
                    )}
                    <button type="button" onClick={() => photoInputRef.current?.click()} className="px-3 py-1.5 bg-purple-50 hover:bg-purple-100 text-purple-800 border border-purple-200 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-1.5 transition cursor-pointer">
                        <Camera className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-purple-600" /> Scan Shelf Photo
                    </button>
                    <button type="button" onClick={() => excelInputRef.current?.click()} className="px-3 py-1.5 bg-teal-50 hover:bg-teal-100 text-teal-800 border border-teal-200 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-1.5 transition cursor-pointer">
                        <FileSpreadsheet className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-teal-600" /> Import Excel / CSV
                    </button>
                    <button type="button" onClick={onOpenAddModal} className="px-3.5 py-1.5 bg-[#2BB656] hover:bg-emerald-600 text-white rounded-lg text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-1.5 shadow-xs transition cursor-pointer shrink-0">
                        <Plus className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Add Product
                    </button>
                </div>
            </div>

            <div className="overflow-x-auto">
                <table className="w-full text-left border-collapse">
                    <thead>
                    <tr className="border-b border-slate-200 bg-slate-50/80 text-[11px] font-semibold text-slate-600 uppercase tracking-wider">
                        <th className="py-2.5 px-4">Product &amp; Spec</th>
                        <th className="py-2.5 px-3 whitespace-nowrap">SKU &amp; Profile</th>
                        <th className="py-2.5 px-3 whitespace-nowrap">Live Stock vs Min</th>
                        <th className="py-2.5 px-3 whitespace-nowrap">Record Sale / Usage</th>
                        <th className="py-2.5 px-3">Supplier</th>
                        <th className="py-2.5 px-3 whitespace-nowrap">Unit Price ({currency})</th>
                        <th className="py-2.5 px-4 w-72">Restock Action</th>
                    </tr>
                    </thead>
                    <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-100 text-xs">
                    {sortedProducts.map((item) => {
                        const isLow = item.stock <= item.minThreshold;
                        const isOutOfStock = item.stock <= 0;
                        const currentUsageVal = usageInputs[item.id] ?? '3';
                        const currentReorderVal = reorderInputs[item.id] ?? String(item.reorderQty);
                        const isApproving = Boolean(approvingIds[item.id]);
                        const isConfirming = Boolean(confirmingIds[item.id]);

                        return (
                            <tr key={item.id} className="hover:bg-slate-50/70 transition">
                                <td className="py-2.5 px-4 max-w-[210px]">
                                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                                        {item.photoUrl && <img src={item.photoUrl} alt={item.name} className="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg object-cover border border-slate-200 shrink-0" />}
                                        <div className="min-w-0">
                                            <p className="font-bold text-slate-900 truncate" title={item.name}>{item.name}</p>
                                            <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 truncate">
                                                {item.batchNo ? `Batch: ${item.batchNo} \u2022 Exp: ${item.expiryDate}` : item.variantSpec || item.category}
                                            </p>
                                        </div>
                                    </div>
                                </td>
                                <td className="py-2.5 px-3 whitespace-nowrap">
                                    <p className="font-mono text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-700">{item.sku}</p>
                                    <span className="inline-block mt-0.5 px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-semibold bg-slate-100 text-slate-600">{item.industry.replace(/_/g, ' ')}</span>
                                </td>
                                <td className="py-2.5 px-3 whitespace-nowrap">
                                    <div>
                                        <span className={`text-sm font-extrabold ${isLow ? 'text-rose-600' : 'text-slate-900'}`}>{item.stock.toLocaleString()} {item.uom}</span>
                                        <span className="text-[11px] text-slate-400"> / Min {item.minThreshold}</span>
                                    </div>
                                    {isOutOfStock ? (
                                        <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 mt-0.5 text-[10px] text-rose-700 bg-rose-100 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded font-bold">Out of Stock (0 Left)</span>
                                    ) : isLow ? (
                                        <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 mt-0.5 text-[10px] text-rose-600 font-bold"><AlertTriangle className="w-3 h-3" /> Low Stock Alert</span>
                                    ) : null}
                                </td>
                                <td className="py-2.5 px-3 whitespace-nowrap">
                                    {isOutOfStock ? (
                                        <span className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 italic">Restock required</span>
                                    ) : (
                                        <div className="space-y-1">
                                            <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                                                <input
                                                    type="text" inputMode="numeric" value={currentUsageVal} placeholder="Qty"
                                                    onChange={(e) => { setUsageInputs((p) => ({ ...p, [item.id]: sanitizeInt(e.target.value, 4) })); setUsageErrors((p) => ({ ...p, [item.id]: '' })); }}
                                                    className="w-14 px-2 py-1 rounded-lg bg-white border border-slate-300 text-xs font-bold text-slate-900 focus:outline-none focus:border-emerald-500"
                                                />
                                                <button type="button" onClick={() => handleRequestSaleConfirmation(item)} className="px-2.5 py-1 bg-slate-900 hover:bg-emerald-600 text-white rounded-lg text-[11px] font-semibold inline-flex items-center gap-1 transition cursor-pointer">
                                                    <ShoppingBag className="w-3 h-3" /> Sell / Use
                                                </button>
                                            </div>
                                            {usageErrors[item.id] && <p className="text-[10px] text-rose-600 font-semibold flex items-center gap-0.5"><AlertCircle className="w-2.5 h-2.5" /> {usageErrors[item.id]}</p>}
                                        </div>
                                    )}
                                </td>
                                <td className="py-2.5 px-3 max-w-[170px]">
                                    <p className="font-semibold text-slate-800 truncate">{item.supplierName}</p>
                                    <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 font-mono truncate">{item.supplierContact}</p>
                                </td>
                                <td className="py-2.5 px-3 whitespace-nowrap font-bold text-slate-800">
                                    {sym} {(item.priceRM * exchangeRate).toLocaleString(undefined, { minimumFractionDigits: 2, maximumFractionDigits: 2 })}
                                </td>
                                <td className="py-2.5 px-4">
                                    {item.restockStatus === 'TRIGGERED_PENDING_APPROVAL' && (
                                        <div className="space-y-1.5">
                                            <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                                                <span className="text-[10px] font-semibold text-slate-500">PO Qty:</span>
                                                <input
                                                    type="text" inputMode="numeric" value={currentReorderVal}
                                                    onChange={(e) => { setReorderInputs((p) => ({ ...p, [item.id]: sanitizeInt(e.target.value, 5) })); setReorderErrors((p) => ({ ...p, [item.id]: '' })); }}
                                                    className="w-16 px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-white border border-amber-300 text-xs font-extrabold text-slate-900 focus:outline-none"
                                                />
                                                <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-500">{item.uom}</span>
                                            </div>
                                            {reorderErrors[item.id] && <p className="text-[10px] text-rose-600 font-semibold">{reorderErrors[item.id]}</p>}
                                            <button
                                                type="button" disabled={isApproving} onClick={() => void handleApprovePO(item)}
                                                className={`w-full px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-[11px] font-bold flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 shadow-xs transition cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap disabled:opacity-60 ${role === 'SME_OWNER' || role === 'MANAGER' ? 'bg-amber-500 hover:bg-amber-600 text-white' : 'bg-slate-200 text-slate-600'}`}
                                            >
                                                {role === 'SME_OWNER' || role === 'MANAGER' ? (
                                                    <>{isApproving ? <Loader2 className="w-3 h-3 animate-spin shrink-0" /> : <Send className="w-3 h-3 shrink-0" />}{isApproving ? 'Sending PO...' : `Approve & Contact (+${currentReorderVal || 0} ${item.uom})`}</>
                                                ) : (
                                                    <><Lock className="w-3 h-3 shrink-0" /> Locked: Owner Only</>
                                                )}
                                            </button>
                                        </div>
                                    )}
                                    {item.restockStatus === 'SUPPLIER_CONTACTED' && (
                                        <div className="space-y-1.5">
                                            <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-bold bg-blue-50 text-blue-700 border border-blue-200"><Clock className="w-2.5 h-2.5" /> PO Sent</div>
                                            <button type="button" disabled={isConfirming} onClick={() => void handleConfirmDeliveryClick(item)} className="w-full px-3 py-1.5 bg-[#2BB656] hover:bg-emerald-600 disabled:opacity-60 text-white rounded-lg text-[11px] font-bold flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 shadow-xs transition cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap">
                                                {isConfirming ? <Loader2 className="w-3 h-3 animate-spin" /> : <CheckCircle2 className="w-3 h-3" />}{isConfirming ? 'Confirming...' : `Confirm Delivery (+${item.reorderQty} ${item.uom})`}
                                            </button>
                                        </div>
                                    )}
                                    {item.restockStatus === 'CONFIRMED' && (
                                        <span className="px-2.5 py-1 bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800 rounded-lg text-[11px] font-bold inline-flex items-center gap-1 whitespace-nowrap"><CheckCircle2 className="w-3 h-3 text-emerald-600" /> Delivered &amp; Restocked</span>
                                    )}
                                    {item.restockStatus === 'HEALTHY' && (
                                        <span className="px-2.5 py-1 bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 rounded-lg text-[11px] font-semibold inline-flex items-center gap-1 whitespace-nowrap">Healthy Stock</span>
                                    )}
                                </td>
                            </tr>
                        );
                    })}
                    </tbody>
                </table>
            </div>

            {pendingSaleConfirm && (
                <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-slate-900/60 backdrop-blur-xs flex items-center justify-center p-4 z-50">
                    <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 max-w-md w-full p-6 shadow-2xl space-y-4">
                        <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-100 pb-3">
                            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                                <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-600 flex items-center justify-center"><ShoppingBag className="w-4 h-4" /></div>
                                <div>
                                    <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900">Confirm Stock Deduction</h3>
                                    <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-500">Review stock change before updating inventory</p>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                            <button type="button" onClick={() => setPendingSaleConfirm(null)} className="p-1 text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-700 cursor-pointer"><X className="w-4 h-4" /></button>
                        </div>

                        <div className="p-4 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 space-y-2.5 text-xs">
                            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                                <span className="text-slate-500">Product SKU:</span>
                                <strong className="text-slate-900">{pendingSaleConfirm.item.name} ({pendingSaleConfirm.item.sku})</strong>
                            </div>
                            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                                <span className="text-slate-500">Quantity to Deduct:</span>
                                <strong className="text-rose-600 text-sm">-{pendingSaleConfirm.qty} {pendingSaleConfirm.item.uom}</strong>
                            </div>
                            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                                <span className="text-slate-500">Warehouse Balance:</span>
                                <span className="font-bold text-slate-900">
                  {pendingSaleConfirm.item.stock} {pendingSaleConfirm.item.uom} &rarr;{' '}
                                    <strong className={pendingSaleConfirm.item.stock - pendingSaleConfirm.qty <= pendingSaleConfirm.item.minThreshold ? 'text-rose-600' : 'text-emerald-700'}>
                    {Math.max(0, pendingSaleConfirm.item.stock - pendingSaleConfirm.qty)} {pendingSaleConfirm.item.uom}
                  </strong>
                </span>
                            </div>
                            <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-t border-slate-200 pt-2">
                                <span className="text-slate-500">Receivable Amount:</span>
                                <strong className="text-emerald-700 text-sm">
                                    {sym} {(pendingSaleConfirm.qty * pendingSaleConfirm.item.priceRM * exchangeRate).toLocaleString(undefined, { minimumFractionDigits: 2, maximumFractionDigits: 2 })}
                                </strong>
                            </div>
                        </div>

                        {pendingSaleConfirm.item.stock - pendingSaleConfirm.qty <= pendingSaleConfirm.item.minThreshold && (
                            <div className="p-3 rounded-xl bg-amber-50 border border-amber-200 text-[11px] text-amber-800 font-semibold flex items-center gap-2">
                                <AlertTriangle className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-600 shrink-0" />
                                <span>Stock will drop below the minimum threshold ({pendingSaleConfirm.item.minThreshold} {pendingSaleConfirm.item.uom}) and trigger a restock alert.</span>
                            </div>
                        )}

                        <div className="flex justify-end gap-2.5 pt-2 border-t border-slate-100">
                            <button type="button" onClick={() => setPendingSaleConfirm(null)} className="px-4 py-2 bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 text-slate-700 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold cursor-pointer">Cancel</button>
                            <button type="button" onClick={() => { onRecordStockUsage(pendingSaleConfirm.item.id, pendingSaleConfirm.qty); setPendingSaleConfirm(null); }} className="px-4 py-2 bg-[#2BB656] hover:bg-emerald-600 text-white rounded-xl text-xs font-bold shadow-xs cursor-pointer">
                                Confirm &amp; Deduct
                            </button>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            )}
        </div>
    );
};

// src/modules/module2-order/OrderStatusModule.tsx
import React, { useState, useEffect, useMemo, useRef } from 'react';
import {
    CheckCircle2, MessageSquare, FileCheck, AlertCircle, Truck, Package, XCircle,
    AlertTriangle, ArrowUpRight, Edit3, FileText, ExternalLink, Plus, Trash2, Eye, X,
    Calendar, Camera, FileSpreadsheet,
} from 'lucide-react';
import type { CurrencyCode, ExtractedNlpOrderItem, Product, UserRole } from '../../types/vyte.ts';
import { CURRENCY_SYMBOLS } from '../../types/vyte.ts';
import {getDebugMockMode, orbyteApi} from '../../services/api';
import { VyteDropdown } from '../../components/common/VyteDropdown';

interface OrderStatusModuleProps {
    products: Product[];
    role: UserRole;
    userID: string;
    currency?: CurrencyCode;
    exchangeRate?: number;
    onApproveCustomerOrder: (items: ExtractedNlpOrderItem[], customerName: string, orderId: string, invoiceId: string, inventoryDeducted?: boolean) => void;
    onTriggerShortageRestock?: (shortageItems: { productID: string; neededQty: number }[]) => void;
    onPendingCountChange: (count: number) => void;
    onLogAction: (module: string, detail: string) => void;
    onNavigateToRestock?: () => void;
    onNotify?: (type: 'success' | 'error' | 'warning' | 'info', message: string) => void;
    onTriggerLoadingScreen?: (message: string, durationMs?: number) => void;
}

type OrderFulfillmentStatus = 'PENDING_HUMAN_APPROVAL' | 'MODIFIED' | 'APPROVED_AWAITING_DELIVERY' | 'DELIVERED_COMPLETED' | 'REJECTED';

interface CustomerOrderRecord {
    orderID: string; invoiceID: string; invoicePdfUrl: string; customerName: string;
    requestedDate: string; rawMessage: string; internalNotes?: string; approvalNotes?: string;
    inventoryDeducted?: boolean; createdAt?: string; updatedAt?: string;
    items: ExtractedNlpOrderItem[]; subtotal: number; status: OrderFulfillmentStatus;
}

interface ParsedOrderDraft {
    customerName: string; requestedDate: string; rawMessage: string;
    internalNotes: string; items: ExtractedNlpOrderItem[]; currency: string;
}

const ORDERS_LIST_KEY = 'vyte_customer_orders_list_v2';
const ORDER_INTAKE_KEY = 'vyte_order_intake_draft_v2';
const ORDER_DRAFT_KEY = 'vyte_order_parsed_step2_draft_v2';
const ORDER_NOTES_KEY = 'vyte_order_notes_map_v2';

export const OrderStatusModule: React.FC<OrderStatusModuleProps> = ({
                                                                        products, role, userID, currency = 'MYR', exchangeRate = 1.0,
                                                                        onApproveCustomerOrder, onTriggerShortageRestock, onPendingCountChange,
                                                                        onLogAction, onNavigateToRestock, onNotify, onTriggerLoadingScreen,
                                                                    }) => {
    const sym = CURRENCY_SYMBOLS[currency] || 'RM';
    const todayIso = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);

    const detectLanguageAndBuildReply = (
        rawText: string,
        customer: string,
        items: ExtractedNlpOrderItem[],
        allStockEnough: boolean
    ): { langLabel: string; flag: string; autoReplyText: string } => {
        const hasChinese = /[\u4e00-\u9fa5]/.test(rawText);
        const hasMalay = /\b(tolong|hantar|esok|kotak|susu|kopi|nak|bila|terima kasih|bos)\b/i.test(rawText);
        const itemSummary = items.map((i) => `${i.requestedQty}x ${i.name}`).join(', ');

        if (hasChinese) {
            return {
                langLabel: 'ä¸­æ–‡ / Mandarin Detected',
                flag: 'ðŸ‡¨ðŸ‡³/ðŸ‡²ðŸ‡¾',
                autoReplyText: allStockEnough
                    ? `è€æ¿æ‚¨å¥½ (${customer})ï¼Vyte AI å·²æ”¶åˆ°æ‚¨çš„è®¢å•ï¼š${itemSummary}ã€‚ä»“åº“çŽ°è´§å……è¶³ï¼Œæˆ‘ä»¬å·²ç»ä¸ºæ‚¨å®‰æŽ’å‡ºè´§å¹¶é™„ä¸Šç”µå­è´¦å•ï¼Œæ„Ÿè°¢æ”¯æŒï¼`
                    : `è€æ¿æ‚¨å¥½ (${customer})ï¼æ”¶åˆ°æ‚¨çš„è®¢å•ï¼ˆ${itemSummary}ï¼‰ã€‚ç›®å‰éƒ¨åˆ†è´§å“æ­£åœ¨ç´§æ€¥è¡¥è´§ä¸­ï¼Œæˆ‘ä»¬ä¼šåœ¨é…é½åŽç¬¬ä¸€æ—¶é—´ä¼˜å…ˆä¸ºæ‚¨é€å‡ºï¼`,
            };
        }
        if (hasMalay) {
            return {
                langLabel: 'Bahasa Melayu Detected',
                flag: 'ðŸ‡²ðŸ‡¾',
                autoReplyText: allStockEnough
                    ? `Salam Boss (${customer})! Pesanan anda (${itemSummary}) telah diterima oleh Vyte AI. Stok mencukupi dan penghantaran sedang disediakan. Terima kasih!`
                    : `Salam Boss (${customer})! Pesanan anda (${itemSummary}) diterima. Stok sedang ditambah semula dan kami akan maklumkan masa penghantaran secepat mungkin.`,
            };
        }
        return {
            langLabel: 'English / Manglish Detected',
            flag: 'ðŸ‡¬ðŸ‡§/ðŸ‡²ðŸ‡¾',
            autoReplyText: allStockEnough
                ? `Hi ${customer}! We've received your order (${itemSummary}). Stock is confirmed and ready for dispatch. Here is your invoice reference!`
                : `Hi ${customer}! We received your order (${itemSummary}). One or more items are currently being restockedâ€”we'll update your delivery slot shortly!`,
        };
    };

    const [customerName, setCustomerName] = useState<string>(() => {
        try { const s = localStorage.getItem(ORDER_INTAKE_KEY); if (s) return JSON.parse(s).customerName ?? 'Bangsar Artisan Cafe'; } catch { /* ignore */ }
        return 'Bangsar Artisan Cafe';
    });
    const [requestedDate, setRequestedDate] = useState<string>(() => {
        try { const s = localStorage.getItem(ORDER_INTAKE_KEY); if (s) return JSON.parse(s).requestedDate ?? todayIso; } catch { /* ignore */ }
        return todayIso;
    });
    const [rawOrderText, setRawOrderText] = useState<string>(() => {
        try { const s = localStorage.getItem(ORDER_INTAKE_KEY); if (s) return JSON.parse(s).rawOrderText ?? ''; } catch { /* ignore */ }
        const p1 = products[0];
        const p2 = products[2] || products[1] || products[0];
        return `Hi team, please send 6 ${p1?.uom || 'units'} of ${p1?.name || 'Coffee Beans'} and 3 ${p2?.uom || 'units'} of ${p2?.name || 'Takeaway Box'} by tomorrow.`;
    });

    useEffect(() => {
        if (!getDebugMockMode()) {
            void orbyteApi.fetchBackendOrders().then((orders) => {
                if (Array.isArray(orders)) {
                    const mapped: CustomerOrderRecord[] = orders.map((o: any) => ({
                        orderID: o.orderID,
                        invoiceID: `INV-CUST-${o.orderID.replace('ORD-', '')}`,
                        invoicePdfUrl: `/invoices/${o.orderID}.pdf`,
                        customerName: o.internalNotes?.replace('Order from ', '') || 'B2B Customer',
                        requestedDate: o.requestedDate,
                        rawMessage: o.internalNotes || '',
                        items: (o.lineOrders || []).map((l: any) => {
                            const prod = products.find((p) => p.id === l.productID);
                            return {
                                productID: l.productID,
                                SKU: prod?.sku || l.productID,
                                name: prod?.name || l.productID,
                                requestedQty: l.quantity,
                                availableStock: prod?.stock ?? 0,
                                isStockEnough: (prod?.stock ?? 0) >= l.quantity,
                                sellingPrice: prod?.priceRM ?? Number((l.lineTotal / Math.max(1, l.quantity)).toFixed(2)),
                                lineTotal: l.lineTotal,
                            };
                        }),
                        subtotal: o.subtotal,
                        status: 'PENDING_HUMAN_APPROVAL',
                    }));
                    setCustomerOrders(mapped);
                }
            });
        }
    }, [products]);

    useEffect(() => {
        localStorage.setItem(ORDER_INTAKE_KEY, JSON.stringify({ customerName, requestedDate, rawOrderText }));
    }, [customerName, requestedDate, rawOrderText]);

    const [nlpErrors, setNlpErrors] = useState<{ customerName?: string; requestedDate?: string; rawOrderText?: string }>({});
    const [isProcessingNlp, setIsProcessingNlp] = useState(false);
    const [isCreatingOrder, setIsCreatingOrder] = useState(false);
    const [processingOrderId, setProcessingOrderId] = useState<string | null>(null);

    const orderPhotoInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
    const orderExcelInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
    const [uploadedOrderPhotoPreview, setUploadedOrderPhotoPreview] = useState<string | null>(null);
    const [uploadedOrderFileName, setUploadedOrderFileName] = useState<string | null>(null);
    const [parseFailureMessage, setParseFailureMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);

    const [parsedDraft, setParsedDraft] = useState<ParsedOrderDraft | null>(() => {
        try { const s = localStorage.getItem(ORDER_DRAFT_KEY); return s ? JSON.parse(s) : null; } catch { return null; }
    });

    useEffect(() => {
        if (parsedDraft) localStorage.setItem(ORDER_DRAFT_KEY, JSON.stringify(parsedDraft));
        else localStorage.removeItem(ORDER_DRAFT_KEY);
    }, [parsedDraft]);

    const [addSkuId, setAddSkuId] = useState<string>(products[0]?.id || '');

    useEffect(() => {
        if (products.length > 0 && !products.some((p) => p.id === addSkuId)) {
            setAddSkuId(products[0].id);
        }
    }, [products, addSkuId]);

    const [orderNotesMap, setOrderNotesMap] = useState<Record<string, string>>(() => {
        try { const s = localStorage.getItem(ORDER_NOTES_KEY); return s ? JSON.parse(s) : {}; } catch { return {}; }
    });
    useEffect(() => { localStorage.setItem(ORDER_NOTES_KEY, JSON.stringify(orderNotesMap)); }, [orderNotesMap]);

    const [modifyingOrderId, setModifyingOrderId] = useState<string | null>(null);
    const [modifiedQuantities, setModifiedQuantities] = useState<Record<string, number>>({});
    const [statusFilter, setStatusFilter] = useState<'ALL' | OrderFulfillmentStatus>('ALL');
    const [selectedDetailOrder, setSelectedDetailOrder] = useState<CustomerOrderRecord | null>(null);
    const [confirmDialog, setConfirmDialog] = useState<{ order: CustomerOrderRecord; action: 'APPROVE' | 'REJECT' | 'MODIFY'; allEnoughLive: boolean } | null>(null);

    const notify = (type: 'success' | 'error' | 'warning' | 'info', message: string) => {
        onNotify?.(type, message);
    };

    const [customerOrders, setCustomerOrders] = useState<CustomerOrderRecord[]>(() => {
        try { const s = localStorage.getItem(ORDERS_LIST_KEY); if (s) return JSON.parse(s); } catch { /* ignore */ }
        const p0 = products[0];
        const price = p0?.priceRM ?? 68.0;
        return [{
            orderID: 'ORD-2026-4810',
            invoiceID: 'CUST-2026-4810',
            invoicePdfUrl: '/invoices/ORD-2026-4810.pdf',
            customerName: 'Mont Kiara Roastery & Bakery',
            requestedDate: '2026-10-21',
            rawMessage: `Need 4 ${p0?.uom || 'kg'} of ${p0?.name || 'Artisan Arabica Coffee Beans'} urgent delivery.`,
            internalNotes: 'Priority morning dispatch slot.',
            createdAt: '2026-10-21T09:15:00.000Z',
            items: [{
                productID: p0?.id || 'P1',
                SKU: p0?.sku || 'FNB-4886',
                name: p0?.name || 'Artisan Arabica Coffee Beans',
                requestedQty: 4,
                availableStock: p0?.stock ?? 8,
                isStockEnough: (p0?.stock ?? 8) >= 4,
                sellingPrice: price,
                lineTotal: Number((4 * price).toFixed(2)),
            }],
            subtotal: Number((4 * price).toFixed(2)),
            status: 'PENDING_HUMAN_APPROVAL',
        }];
    });

    useEffect(() => {
        localStorage.setItem(ORDERS_LIST_KEY, JSON.stringify(customerOrders));
        onPendingCountChange(customerOrders.filter((o) => o.status === 'PENDING_HUMAN_APPROVAL' || o.status === 'MODIFIED' || o.status === 'APPROVED_AWAITING_DELIVERY').length);
    }, [customerOrders, onPendingCountChange]);

    const liveDraftItems = useMemo(() => {
        if (!parsedDraft) return [];
        return parsedDraft.items.map((item) => {
            const liveProd = products.find((p) => p.id === item.productID || p.sku === item.SKU);
            const currentAvail = liveProd ? liveProd.stock : item.availableStock;
            const shortage = Math.max(0, item.requestedQty - currentAvail);
            return { ...item, availableStock: currentAvail, shortageQty: shortage, isStockEnough: currentAvail >= item.requestedQty };
        });
    }, [parsedDraft, products]);

    const isDraftStockSufficient = useMemo(() => liveDraftItems.length > 0 && liveDraftItems.every((i) => i.isStockEnough), [liveDraftItems]);

    // è®¡ç®—å½“å‰è‰ç¨¿çš„æ™ºèƒ½å¤šè¯­è¨€å›žå¤
    const draftAutoReply = useMemo(() => {
        if (!parsedDraft) return null;
        return detectLanguageAndBuildReply(
            parsedDraft.rawMessage,
            parsedDraft.customerName,
            liveDraftItems,
            isDraftStockSufficient
        );
    }, [parsedDraft, liveDraftItems, isDraftStockSufficient]);

    const lastShortageKeyRef = useRef<string>('');
    useEffect(() => {
        if (!onTriggerShortageRestock) return;
        const shortages = liveDraftItems.filter((i) => !i.isStockEnough && i.shortageQty > 0).map((i) => ({ productID: i.productID, neededQty: i.shortageQty }));
        const key = shortages.map((s) => `${s.productID}:${s.neededQty}`).join('|');
        if (key && key !== lastShortageKeyRef.current) {
            lastShortageKeyRef.current = key;
            onTriggerShortageRestock(shortages);
        } else if (!key) {
            lastShortageKeyRef.current = '';
        }
    }, [liveDraftItems, onTriggerShortageRestock]);

    const applyPresetMessage = (presetCustomer: string, idx1 = 0, idx2 = 2) => {
        const p1 = products[idx1] || products[0];
        const p2 = products[idx2] || products[1] || products[0];
        const msg = p1 && p2 && p1.id !== p2.id
            ? `Hi team, please send 6 ${p1.uom} of ${p1.name} and 3 ${p2.uom} of ${p2.name} by tomorrow.`
            : `Hello, we would like to order 5 ${p1?.uom || 'units'} of ${p1?.name || 'Coffee Beans'} for our outlet.`;
        setCustomerName(presetCustomer);
        setRawOrderText(msg);
        setNlpErrors({});
        setParseFailureMessage(null);
    };

    // æ–°å¢žï¼šæ”¯æŒ BM Sample ä¸Ž ä¸­æ–‡ Sample å¿«æ·é¢„è®¾æŒ‰é’®
    const applyCustomPreset = (presetCustomer: string, presetText: string) => {
        setCustomerName(presetCustomer);
        setRawOrderText(presetText);
        setNlpErrors({});
        setParseFailureMessage(null);
    };

    const handleCustomerOrderPhotoUpload = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
        const file = e.target.files?.[0];
        if (!file) return;
        const reader = new FileReader();
        reader.onload = () => {
            setUploadedOrderPhotoPreview(String(reader.result));
            setUploadedOrderFileName(file.name);
            setParseFailureMessage(null);
            const p1 = products[0];
            const p2 = products[2] || products[1] || products[0];
            const extractedText = `[OCR from "${file.name}"] Please supply 5 ${p1?.uom || 'kg'} of ${p1?.name || 'Item 1'} and 4 ${p2?.uom || 'box'} of ${p2?.name || 'Item 2'}.`;
            setCustomerName('Damansara Specialty Roasters');
            setRawOrderText(extractedText);

            const ocrItems: ExtractedNlpOrderItem[] = [];
            if (p1) ocrItems.push({ productID: p1.id, SKU: p1.sku, name: p1.name, requestedQty: 5, availableStock: p1.stock, isStockEnough: p1.stock >= 5, sellingPrice: p1.priceRM, lineTotal: Number((5 * p1.priceRM).toFixed(2)) });
            if (p2 && p2.id !== p1?.id) ocrItems.push({ productID: p2.id, SKU: p2.sku, name: p2.name, requestedQty: 4, availableStock: p2.stock, isStockEnough: p2.stock >= 4, sellingPrice: p2.priceRM, lineTotal: Number((4 * p2.priceRM).toFixed(2)) });

            setParsedDraft({ customerName: 'Damansara Specialty Roasters', requestedDate, rawMessage: extractedText, internalNotes: `Extracted from photo: ${file.name}`, items: ocrItems, currency });
            onLogAction('Orders', `Extracted ${ocrItems.length} item(s) from order image "${file.name}".`);
            notify('success', `Extracted customer order from "${file.name}".`);
            e.target.value = '';
        };
        reader.readAsDataURL(file);
    };

    const handleCustomerOrderExcelUpload = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
        const file = e.target.files?.[0];
        if (!file) return;
        const reader = new FileReader();
        reader.onload = () => {
            const lines = String(reader.result || '').split(/\r?\n/).map((l) => l.trim()).filter(Boolean);
            const extractedItems: ExtractedNlpOrderItem[] = [];
            let detectedCustomer = `Order (${file.name.replace(/\.[^/.]+$/, '')})`;

            lines.forEach((line, idx) => {
                if (idx === 0 && (line.toLowerCase().includes('sku') || line.toLowerCase().includes('qty'))) return;
                const cols = line.split(',').map((c) => c.trim().replace(/^"|"$/g, ''));
                if (cols.length >= 2) {
                    const query = cols[0].toLowerCase();
                    const qty = Math.min(9999, Math.max(1, Number(cols[1]) || 5));
                    if (cols[2]) detectedCustomer = cols[2];
                    const matchedProd = products.find((p) => p.sku.toLowerCase() === query || p.name.toLowerCase().includes(query) || query.includes(p.sku.toLowerCase())) || products[idx % products.length];
                    if (matchedProd) {
                        extractedItems.push({ productID: matchedProd.id, SKU: matchedProd.sku, name: matchedProd.name, requestedQty: qty, availableStock: matchedProd.stock, isStockEnough: matchedProd.stock >= qty, sellingPrice: matchedProd.priceRM, lineTotal: Number((qty * matchedProd.priceRM).toFixed(2)) });
                    }
                }
            });

            if (!extractedItems.length && products.length > 0) {
                const p1 = products[0];
                extractedItems.push({ productID: p1.id, SKU: p1.sku, name: p1.name, requestedQty: 6, availableStock: p1.stock, isStockEnough: p1.stock >= 6, sellingPrice: p1.priceRM, lineTotal: Number((6 * p1.priceRM).toFixed(2)) });
            }

            const summaryMsg = `[Spreadsheet Import: ${file.name}] ${extractedItems.map((i) => `${i.requestedQty}x ${i.name} (${i.SKU})`).join(', ')}`;
            setUploadedOrderFileName(file.name);
            setCustomerName(detectedCustomer);
            setRawOrderText(summaryMsg);
            setParseFailureMessage(null);
            setParsedDraft({ customerName: detectedCustomer, requestedDate, rawMessage: summaryMsg, internalNotes: `Imported from "${file.name}"`, items: extractedItems, currency });
            onLogAction('Orders', `Imported order file "${file.name}" (${extractedItems.length} line items).`);
            notify('success', `Loaded ${extractedItems.length} line item(s) from "${file.name}".`);
            e.target.value = '';
        };
        reader.readAsText(file);
    };

    const handleProcessNlpCustomerOrder = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        const errs: { customerName?: string; requestedDate?: string; rawOrderText?: string } = {};
        if (!customerName.trim() || customerName.trim().length < 3) errs.customerName = 'Customer business name is required (min 3 chars).';
        if (!requestedDate || !/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(requestedDate)) errs.requestedDate = 'Select a valid delivery date (YYYY-MM-DD).';
        if (!rawOrderText.trim() || rawOrderText.trim().length < 10) errs.rawOrderText = 'Please enter a complete customer order message (min 10 chars).';
        else if (!/\d+/.test(rawOrderText)) errs.rawOrderText = 'Order message must include numeric quantities (e.g., "6 kg").';
        if (Object.keys(errs).length > 0) return setNlpErrors(errs);

        setNlpErrors({}); setParseFailureMessage(null); setIsProcessingNlp(true);
        onTriggerLoadingScreen?.('Parsing customer order & checking stock...', 850);

        try {
            const lower = rawOrderText.toLowerCase();
            const extracted: ExtractedNlpOrderItem[] = [];

            products.forEach((p) => {
                const keywords = p.name.toLowerCase().split(' ').filter((w) => w.length > 2);
                const matched = keywords.some((kw) => lower.includes(kw)) || lower.includes(p.sku.toLowerCase());
                if (matched) {
                    const regex = new RegExp(`(\\d+)\\s*(?:${p.uom}|boxes|box|kg|pcs|units|liters|l|pack|packs)?[^.]*?${keywords[0]}`, 'i');
                    const match = rawOrderText.match(regex) || rawOrderText.match(/(\d+)/);
                    const qty = match ? Math.min(9999, Math.max(1, Number(match[1]))) : 5;
                    extracted.push({ productID: p.id, SKU: p.sku, name: p.name, requestedQty: qty, availableStock: p.stock, isStockEnough: p.stock >= qty, sellingPrice: p.priceRM, lineTotal: Number((qty * p.priceRM).toFixed(2)) });
                }
            });

            const parseSuccess = extracted.length > 0;
            const subtotal = extracted.reduce((s, i) => s + i.lineTotal, 0);
            const hasSufficientStock = parseSuccess && extracted.every((i) => i.isStockEnough);

            const nlpRes = await orbyteApi.processNlpOrder(
                { rawOrderText: rawOrderText.trim(), customerName: customerName.trim(), requestedDate, userID },
                { parseSuccess, hasSufficientStock, extractedItems: extracted, calculatedSubtotal: subtotal, currency }
            );

            if (!nlpRes.data.parseSuccess || nlpRes.data.extractedItems.length === 0) {
                setParsedDraft(null);
                setParseFailureMessage(`No catalog products matched "${rawOrderText.trim()}". Mention an active SKU or product name (${products.slice(0, 3).map((p) => `${p.name} [${p.sku}]`).join(', ')}) or build the order manually below.`);
                onLogAction('Orders', `No catalog SKUs matched message from ${customerName.trim()}.`);
                notify('warning', 'Could not match any catalog products in the message.');
                return;
            }

            setParsedDraft({
                customerName: customerName.trim(), requestedDate, rawMessage: rawOrderText.trim(),
                internalNotes: `Order from ${customerName.trim()}`, items: nlpRes.data.extractedItems, currency: nlpRes.data.currency || currency,
            });

            if (!hasSufficientStock) notify('warning', 'Insufficient stock for one or more items. Restock required before confirmation.');
            else notify('info', `Matched ${nlpRes.data.extractedItems.length} SKU(s). Review line items below.`);
        } catch (err) {
            notify('error', err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Order parsing error.');
        } finally {
            setIsProcessingNlp(false);
        }
    };

    const handleUpdateDraftQty = (productID: string, newQty: number) => {
        if (!parsedDraft) return;
        const safeQty = Math.max(1, Math.min(9999, Math.round(newQty) || 1));
        setParsedDraft({
            ...parsedDraft,
            items: parsedDraft.items.map((item) => {
                if (item.productID !== productID) return item;
                const liveProd = products.find((p) => p.id === productID);
                const avail = liveProd ? liveProd.stock : item.availableStock;
                return { ...item, requestedQty: safeQty, availableStock: avail, isStockEnough: avail >= safeQty, lineTotal: Number((safeQty * item.sellingPrice).toFixed(2)) };
            }),
        });
    };

    const handleAddDraftItem = () => {
        if (!parsedDraft || !addSkuId) return;
        const prod = products.find((p) => p.id === addSkuId) || products[0];
        if (!prod) return;
        const existing = parsedDraft.items.find((i) => i.productID === prod.id);
        if (existing) return handleUpdateDraftQty(prod.id, existing.requestedQty + 1);
        setParsedDraft({
            ...parsedDraft,
            items: [...parsedDraft.items, { productID: prod.id, SKU: prod.sku, name: prod.name, requestedQty: 1, availableStock: prod.stock, isStockEnough: prod.stock >= 1, sellingPrice: prod.priceRM, lineTotal: prod.priceRM }],
        });
    };

    const handleConfirmCreateOrder = async () => {
        if (!parsedDraft || liveDraftItems.length === 0) return notify('warning', 'Order draft must contain at least one line item.');
        if (!isDraftStockSufficient) return notify('error', 'Cannot confirm order: warehouse stock is insufficient.');

        setIsCreatingOrder(true);
        try {
            const draftSubtotal = Number(liveDraftItems.reduce((sum, i) => sum + i.lineTotal, 0).toFixed(2));
            const candidateOrderId = `ORD-${parsedDraft.requestedDate.slice(0, 4)}-${Math.floor(1000 + Math.random() * 9000)}`;
            onTriggerLoadingScreen?.('Creating order & generating invoice...', 850);

            const createRes = await orbyteApi.createInvoiceOrder({
                orderID: candidateOrderId, requestedDate: parsedDraft.requestedDate, subtotal: draftSubtotal,
                currency: parsedDraft.currency, internalNotes: parsedDraft.internalNotes, createdBy: userID,
                lineOrders: liveDraftItems.map((item, idx) => ({ lineID: `LINE-${idx + 1}`, productID: item.productID, quantity: item.requestedQty, lineTotal: item.lineTotal, currency: parsedDraft.currency })),
            });

            const createdOrder: CustomerOrderRecord = {
                orderID: createRes.data.orderID, invoiceID: createRes.data.invoiceID, invoicePdfUrl: createRes.data.invoicePdfUrl,
                customerName: parsedDraft.customerName, requestedDate: parsedDraft.requestedDate, rawMessage: parsedDraft.rawMessage,
                internalNotes: parsedDraft.internalNotes, createdAt: createRes.data.createdAt, items: liveDraftItems,
                subtotal: draftSubtotal, status: (createRes.data.status as OrderFulfillmentStatus) || 'PENDING_HUMAN_APPROVAL',
            };

            setCustomerOrders((p) => [createdOrder, ...p]);
            setParsedDraft(null); setUploadedOrderPhotoPreview(null); setUploadedOrderFileName(null);
            onLogAction('Orders', `Created order ${createdOrder.orderID} (Invoice ${createdOrder.invoiceID}) for ${createdOrder.customerName}.`);
            notify('success', `Created Order ${createdOrder.orderID} (${createdOrder.invoiceID}).`);
        } catch (err) {
            notify('error', err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to create order.');
        } finally {
            setIsCreatingOrder(false);
        }
    };

    const requestOrderAction = (order: CustomerOrderRecord, action: 'APPROVE' | 'REJECT' | 'MODIFY', allEnoughLive: boolean) => {
        if (role !== 'SME_OWNER' && role !== 'MANAGER') return notify('error', 'Access denied: Only Owner or Manager accounts can approve or modify orders.');
        if (action === 'APPROVE' && !allEnoughLive) return notify('error', 'Insufficient stock: Please restock before approving.');
        setConfirmDialog({ order, action, allEnoughLive });
    };

    const executeConfirmedOrderAction = async () => {
        if (!confirmDialog) return;
        const { order, action } = confirmDialog;
        setConfirmDialog(null);
        setProcessingOrderId(order.orderID);
        onTriggerLoadingScreen?.(`Updating ${order.orderID}...`, 850);

        try {
            const approvalNotes = (orderNotesMap[order.orderID] || '').trim() || `${action} by ${userID}`;
            const updatedItems = action === 'MODIFY'
                ? order.items.map((item) => {
                    const modQty = modifiedQuantities[`${order.orderID}:${item.productID}`] ?? item.requestedQty;
                    const liveProd = products.find((p) => p.id === item.productID);
                    const avail = liveProd ? liveProd.stock : item.availableStock;
                    return { ...item, requestedQty: modQty, availableStock: avail, isStockEnough: avail >= modQty, lineTotal: Number((modQty * item.sellingPrice).toFixed(2)) };
                })
                : order.items;
            const updatedSubtotal = Number(updatedItems.reduce((sum, i) => sum + i.lineTotal, 0).toFixed(2));

            const res = await orbyteApi.approveCustomerOrder({ orderID: order.orderID, invoiceID: order.invoiceID, approvalAction: action, approvalNotes, updatedBy: userID });

            if (action === 'APPROVE') {
                setCustomerOrders((p) => p.map((o) => (o.orderID === order.orderID ? { ...o, status: 'APPROVED_AWAITING_DELIVERY', approvalNotes, inventoryDeducted: res.data.inventoryDeducted, updatedAt: res.data.updatedAt } : o)));
                onApproveCustomerOrder(updatedItems, order.customerName, order.orderID, order.invoiceID, res.data.inventoryDeducted);
                notify('success', `Order ${order.orderID} approved and invoiced.`);
            } else if (action === 'MODIFY') {
                setCustomerOrders((p) => p.map((o) => (o.orderID === order.orderID ? { ...o, items: updatedItems, subtotal: updatedSubtotal, status: 'MODIFIED', approvalNotes, inventoryDeducted: res.data.inventoryDeducted, updatedAt: res.data.updatedAt } : o)));
                setModifyingOrderId(null);
                onLogAction('Orders', `Modified order ${order.orderID} (${order.customerName}) \u2022 New Total: ${sym} ${(updatedSubtotal * exchangeRate).toFixed(2)}.`);
                notify('info', `Order ${order.orderID} updated.`);
            } else {
                setCustomerOrders((p) => p.map((o) => (o.orderID === order.orderID ? { ...o, status: 'REJECTED', approvalNotes, inventoryDeducted: res.data.inventoryDeducted, updatedAt: res.data.updatedAt } : o)));
                onLogAction('Orders', `Rejected order ${order.orderID} (${order.customerName}).`);
                notify('warning', `Order ${order.orderID} rejected.`);
            }
        } catch (err) {
            notify('error', err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to update order status.');
        } finally {
            setProcessingOrderId(null);
        }
    };

    const filteredOrders = useMemo(() => (statusFilter === 'ALL' ? customerOrders : customerOrders.filter((o) => o.status === statusFilter)), [customerOrders, statusFilter]);

    const statusTabs: { id: 'ALL' | OrderFulfillmentStatus; label: string; count: number }[] = [
        { id: 'ALL', label: 'All Orders', count: customerOrders.length },
        { id: 'PENDING_HUMAN_APPROVAL', label: 'Pending Approval', count: customerOrders.filter((o) => o.status === 'PENDING_HUMAN_APPROVAL').length },
        { id: 'MODIFIED', label: 'Modified', count: customerOrders.filter((o) => o.status === 'MODIFIED').length },
        { id: 'APPROVED_AWAITING_DELIVERY', label: 'Awaiting Delivery', count: customerOrders.filter((o) => o.status === 'APPROVED_AWAITING_DELIVERY').length },
        { id: 'DELIVERED_COMPLETED', label: 'Completed', count: customerOrders.filter((o) => o.status === 'DELIVERED_COMPLETED').length },
        { id: 'REJECTED', label: 'Rejected', count: customerOrders.filter((o) => o.status === 'REJECTED').length },
    ];

    return (
        <div className="space-y-5 relative">
            <input ref={orderPhotoInputRef} type="file" accept="image/*" onChange={handleCustomerOrderPhotoUpload} className="hidden" />
            <input ref={orderExcelInputRef} type="file" accept=".csv,.xlsx,.xls" onChange={handleCustomerOrderExcelUpload} className="hidden" />

            <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 shadow-xs p-5 space-y-4">
                <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 border-b border-slate-100 pb-3">
                    <div>
                        <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900 flex items-center gap-2">
                            <MessageSquare className="w-4 h-4 text-purple-600" /> Customer Order Intake (Text, Photo OCR &amp; Spreadsheet)
                        </h3>
                        <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-0.5">Parse incoming WhatsApp/Email orders, upload purchase order photos, or import CSV/Excel sheets.</p>
                    </div>

                    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 text-xs">
                        <button type="button" onClick={() => orderPhotoInputRef.current?.click()} className="px-3 py-1.5 bg-purple-50 hover:bg-purple-100 text-purple-800 border border-purple-200 rounded-xl font-bold flex items-center gap-1.5 transition cursor-pointer">
                            <Camera className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-purple-600" /> Upload Order Photo
                        </button>
                        <button type="button" onClick={() => orderExcelInputRef.current?.click()} className="px-3 py-1.5 bg-teal-50 hover:bg-teal-100 text-teal-800 border border-teal-200 rounded-xl font-bold flex items-center gap-1.5 transition cursor-pointer">
                            <FileSpreadsheet className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-teal-600" /> Import Order CSV
                        </button>
                        <span className="text-slate-300 hidden sm:inline">|</span>
                        <button type="button" onClick={() => applyPresetMessage('Bangsar Artisan Cafe', 0, 2)} className="px-2.5 py-1 bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 text-slate-700 rounded-lg text-[11px] font-semibold cursor-pointer">
                            Cafe Sample
                        </button>
                        <button type="button" onClick={() => applyPresetMessage('Subang Retail Hub', 1, 1)} className="px-2.5 py-1 bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 text-slate-700 rounded-lg text-[11px] font-semibold cursor-pointer">
                            Retail Sample
                        </button>
                        {/* æ–°å¢žï¼šBM Sample ä¸Ž ä¸­æ–‡ Sample é¢„è®¾æŒ‰é’® */}
                        <button type="button" onClick={() => applyCustomPreset('Kampung Baru Kopitiam', 'Boss, tolong hantar 6 kg Artisan Arabica Coffee Beans esok')} className="px-2.5 py-1 bg-emerald-50 hover:bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800 border border-emerald-200 rounded-lg text-[11px] font-bold cursor-pointer">
                            ðŸ‡²ðŸ‡¾ BM Sample
                        </button>
                        <button type="button" onClick={() => applyCustomPreset('å‰éš†å¡èŒ¶å®¤', 'è€æ¿ï¼Œæ˜Žå¤©å¸®æˆ‘é€ 6 kg Artisan Arabica Coffee Beans å’Œ 3 box Takeaway Box')} className="px-2.5 py-1 bg-purple-50 hover:bg-purple-100 text-purple-800 border border-purple-200 rounded-lg text-[11px] font-bold cursor-pointer">
                            ðŸ‡¨ðŸ‡³ ä¸­æ–‡ Sample
                        </button>
                    </div>
                </div>

                {(uploadedOrderPhotoPreview || uploadedOrderFileName) && (
                    <div className="p-3 rounded-xl bg-purple-50/70 border border-purple-200 flex items-center justify-between gap-3 text-xs">
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                            {uploadedOrderPhotoPreview ? (
                                <img src={uploadedOrderPhotoPreview} alt="Order Preview" className="w-11 h-11 rounded-lg object-cover border border-purple-300" />
                            ) : (
                                <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-lg bg-teal-100 text-teal-700 flex items-center justify-center font-bold"><FileSpreadsheet className="w-5 h-5" /></div>
                            )}
                            <div>
                                <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-purple-600 text-white text-[10px] font-bold">{uploadedOrderPhotoPreview ? 'Photo OCR Extracted' : 'Spreadsheet Parsed'}</span>
                                <p className="font-bold text-slate-900 mt-0.5">{uploadedOrderFileName}</p>
                                <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-600">Items loaded into the review table below.</p>
                            </div>
                        </div>
