                                className={`mt-1 w-full px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-white text-slate-900 font-bold text-xs focus:outline-none border-2 ${inputErrors.holdingCost ? 'border-rose-500' : 'border-transparent focus:border-emerald-400'}`}
                            />
                            {inputErrors.holdingCost && <p className="text-[10px] text-rose-300 font-semibold mt-1 flex items-center gap-1"><AlertCircle className="w-3 h-3 shrink-0" /> {inputErrors.holdingCost}</p>}
                        </div>

                        <div>
                            <label className="text-[11px] text-slate-300 font-semibold block">Target Turnover (5â€“90d)</label>
                            <input
                                type="text" inputMode="numeric" placeholder="21" value={turnoverDaysInput}
                                onChange={(e) => setTurnoverDaysInput(e.target.value.replace(/[^0-9]/g, '').replace(/^0+(?=\d)/, ''))}
                                className={`mt-1 w-full px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg bg-white text-slate-900 font-bold text-xs focus:outline-none border-2 ${inputErrors.turnoverDays ? 'border-rose-500' : 'border-transparent focus:border-emerald-400'}`}
                            />
                            {inputErrors.turnoverDays && <p className="text-[10px] text-rose-300 font-semibold mt-1 flex items-center gap-1"><AlertCircle className="w-3 h-3 shrink-0" /> {inputErrors.turnoverDays}</p>}
                        </div>

                        <button
                            type="button" onClick={() => void fetchSalesAndProcurement('manual-ai')}
                            disabled={isLoading || Boolean(inputErrors.holdingCost) || Boolean(inputErrors.turnoverDays)}
                            className={`w-full py-2.5 px-3 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 shadow-sm transition ${inputErrors.holdingCost || inputErrors.turnoverDays ? 'bg-slate-600 text-slate-300 cursor-not-allowed' : 'bg-[#2BB656] hover:bg-emerald-500 text-white cursor-pointer'}`}
                        >
                            <Sparkles className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />{isRunningAi ? 'Recalculating...' : 'Recalculate AI Forecast'}
                        </button>
                    </div>
                </div>

                <div className="bg-white p-4 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 shadow-xs flex items-center justify-between">
                    <div className="min-w-0">
                        <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase">{activeReport.period} Revenue</p>
                        <p className="text-lg font-extrabold text-slate-900 mt-0.5 truncate">{sym} {activeReport.totalRevenue.toLocaleString(undefined, { minimumFractionDigits: 2, maximumFractionDigits: 2 })}</p>
                        {topProduct && <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 block truncate mt-0.5">Top: {topProduct.SKU} ({sym} {topProduct.totalSalesAmount.toLocaleString()})</span>}
                    </div>
                    <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-600 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0"><DollarSign className="w-4 h-4" /></div>
                </div>

                <div className="bg-white p-4 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 shadow-xs flex items-center justify-between">
                    <div>
                        <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase">Total Units Sold</p>
                        <p className="text-lg font-extrabold text-slate-900 mt-0.5">{activeReport.totalUnitsSold.toLocaleString()} Units</p>
                        <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 block mt-0.5">Across {activeReport.productBreakdown.length} active SKUs</span>
                    </div>
                    <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl bg-blue-50 text-blue-600 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0"><PackageCheck className="w-4 h-4" /></div>
                </div>

                <div className="bg-white p-4 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 shadow-xs flex items-center justify-between">
                    <div>
                        <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase">Est. Holding Cost</p>
                        <p className="text-lg font-extrabold text-amber-600 mt-0.5">{sym} {totalHoldingCostFormatted}</p>
                        <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 block mt-0.5">At {holdingCostPercentage}% annual rate</span>
                    </div>
                    <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl bg-amber-50 text-amber-600 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0"><Clock className="w-4 h-4" /></div>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    );
};

========== FILE: E:\Code\Vyte\orbyteflow\src\modules\module4-quality\QualityControlModule.tsx ==========

// src/modules/module4-quality/QualityControlModule.tsx
import React, { useState, useMemo, useEffect } from 'react';
import {
    Sparkles, AlertTriangle, RefreshCw, CheckCircle2, Plus, AlertCircle, Users, X,
    UserCheck, Search, Send, Calendar, Clock, FileCode, Copy, Check,
} from 'lucide-react';
import type {
    CurrencyCode, OrbyteHrOverdueEscalationPayload, OrbyteHrStaff, Product, QCAlert, UserRole,
} from '../../types/vyte.ts';
import { CURRENCY_SYMBOLS } from '../../types/vyte.ts';

interface QualityControlModuleProps {
    qcAlerts: QCAlert[];
    products?: Product[];
    role: UserRole;
    currency?: CurrencyCode;
    exchangeRate?: number;
    onDispatchToHrStaff: (qcId: string, staff: OrbyteHrStaff, priority: 'URGENT' | 'HIGH' | 'NORMAL', dueDate: string, hrNotes: string) => void;
    onEscalateOverdueToHr: (qcId: string, payload: OrbyteHrOverdueEscalationPayload, simulatedPastDate?: string) => void;
    onCompleteHrTask: (qcId: string) => void;
    onTriggerScan: () => void;
    onAddCustomQCAlert: (newAlert: QCAlert) => void;
}

const ORBYTE_HR_STAFF_ROSTER: OrbyteHrStaff[] = [
    { staffId: 'HR-S204', name: 'Amirul Hakim', department: 'Cold-Chain & Quality Ops', position: 'Senior QC Inspector', shiftStatus: 'ON_DUTY' },
    { staffId: 'HR-S209', name: 'Nurul Aina', department: 'Storefront & Promotions', position: 'Floor Operations Lead', shiftStatus: 'ON_DUTY' },
    { staffId: 'HR-S215', name: 'Tan Wei Ming', department: 'Warehouse & Receiving', position: 'Inventory Supervisor', shiftStatus: 'ON_DUTY' },
    { staffId: 'HR-S221', name: 'Priya Nair', department: 'Supplier Compliance', position: 'Procurement & Return Specialist', shiftStatus: 'ON_DUTY' },
];

const sanitizePosInt = (raw: string) => raw.replace(/[^0-9]/g, '').replace(/^0+/, '');

export const QualityControlModule: React.FC<QualityControlModuleProps> = ({
                                                                              qcAlerts, products = [], role, currency = 'MYR', exchangeRate = 1.0,
                                                                              onDispatchToHrStaff, onEscalateOverdueToHr, onCompleteHrTask, onTriggerScan, onAddCustomQCAlert,
                                                                          }) => {
    const sym = CURRENCY_SYMBOLS[currency] || 'RM';
    const canApprove = role === 'SME_OWNER' || role === 'MANAGER';
    const todayIso = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);

    const [isScanning, setIsScanning] = useState(false);
    const [showLogForm, setShowLogForm] = useState(false);
    const [statusFilter, setStatusFilter] = useState<'ALL' | 'PENDING_APPROVAL' | 'ASSIGNED_TO_STAFF' | 'OVERDUE_HR' | 'APPROVED_RESOLVED'>('ALL');
    const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');

    const [selectedQcModal, setSelectedQcModal] = useState<QCAlert | null>(null);
    const [selectedStaffId, setSelectedStaffId] = useState(ORBYTE_HR_STAFF_ROSTER[0].staffId);
    const [taskPriority, setTaskPriority] = useState<'URGENT' | 'HIGH' | 'NORMAL'>('URGENT');
    const [taskDueDate, setTaskDueDate] = useState(todayIso);
    const [dueDateError, setDueDateError] = useState('');
    const [taskInstruction, setTaskInstruction] = useState('');

    const [inspectingPayload, setInspectingPayload] = useState<OrbyteHrOverdueEscalationPayload | null>(null);
    const [copiedPayload, setCopiedPayload] = useState(false);

    const [selectedProductId, setSelectedProductId] = useState(products[0]?.id || '');
    useEffect(() => {
        if (products.length > 0 && !products.some((p) => p.id === selectedProductId)) {
            setSelectedProductId(products[0].id);
        }
    }, [products, selectedProductId]);
    const [batchNo, setBatchNo] = useState('');
    const [qtyAffectedInput, setQtyAffectedInput] = useState('');
    const [daysLeftInput, setDaysLeftInput] = useState('');
    const [triggerReason, setTriggerReason] = useState('');
    const [errors, setErrors] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});

    const buildOverduePayload = (qc: QCAlert, effectiveDueDate: string): OrbyteHrOverdueEscalationPayload => {
        const diffDays = Math.max(1, Math.round((new Date(todayIso).getTime() - new Date(effectiveDueDate).getTime()) / 86400000));
        return {
            event: 'ORBYTE_HR_TASK_OVERDUE_ESCALATION',
            escalationId: `ESC-HR-${Math.floor(1000 + Math.random() * 9000)}`,
            hrTaskId: qc.hrTaskId || `HR-TASK-${qc.id}`,
            qcAlertId: qc.id,
            staff: {
                staffId: qc.assignedStaffId || 'HR-S204',
                name: qc.assignedStaffName || 'Amirul Hakim',
                department: qc.assignedStaffDepartment || 'Cold-Chain & Quality Ops',
                position: qc.assignedStaffPosition || 'Senior QC Inspector',
            },
            taskDetails: {
                sku: qc.sku, productName: qc.productName, batchNo: qc.batchNo,
                actionRequired: qc.hrNotes || qc.aiSuggestion, priority: qc.assignedPriority || 'URGENT',
                assignedDueDate: effectiveDueDate, currentDate: todayIso, daysOverdue: diffDays,
                taskStatus: 'UNDONE_PAST_DUE', atRiskValueRM: qc.savedRM,
            },
            hrSystemDirective: 'LOG_KPI_BREACH_AND_ESCALATE_SUPERVISOR',
            dispatchedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
        };
    };

    useEffect(() => {
        qcAlerts.forEach((qc) => {
            if (qc.status === 'ASSIGNED_TO_STAFF' && qc.assignedDueDate && qc.assignedDueDate < todayIso && !qc.hrEscalated) {
                onEscalateOverdueToHr(qc.id, buildOverduePayload(qc, qc.assignedDueDate), qc.assignedDueDate);
            }
        });
    }, [qcAlerts, todayIso, onEscalateOverdueToHr]);

    const openAiRecommendationModal = (qc: QCAlert) => {
        setSelectedQcModal(qc);
        setSelectedStaffId(qc.assignedStaffId || ORBYTE_HR_STAFF_ROSTER[0].staffId);
        setTaskPriority(qc.daysLeft <= 3 ? 'URGENT' : 'HIGH');
        setTaskDueDate(qc.assignedDueDate || todayIso);
        setDueDateError('');
        setTaskInstruction(qc.hrNotes || qc.aiSuggestion);
    };

    const handleConfirmHrDispatch = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        if (!selectedQcModal) return;
        if (!taskDueDate || !/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(taskDueDate)) return setDueDateError('Please select a valid completion due date.');
        const staff = ORBYTE_HR_STAFF_ROSTER.find((s) => s.staffId === selectedStaffId) || ORBYTE_HR_STAFF_ROSTER[0];
        onDispatchToHrStaff(selectedQcModal.id, staff, taskPriority, taskDueDate, taskInstruction.trim());
        setSelectedQcModal(null);
    };

    const handleSimulatePastDueAndEscalate = (qc: QCAlert) => {
        const d = new Date();
        d.setDate(d.getDate() - 2);
        const pastIso = d.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
        const payload = buildOverduePayload(qc, pastIso);
        onEscalateOverdueToHr(qc.id, payload, pastIso);
        setInspectingPayload(payload);
    };

    const handleCreateCustomAlert = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        const errs: Record<string, string> = {};
        const prod = products.find((p) => p.id === selectedProductId) || products[0];
        if (!batchNo.trim() || batchNo.trim().length < 3) errs.batchNo = 'Batch/Lot number is required (min 3 chars).';
        if (!qtyAffectedInput || Number(qtyAffectedInput) < 1) errs.qtyAffected = 'Affected quantity must be at least 1.';
        if (!daysLeftInput || Number(daysLeftInput) < 1 || Number(daysLeftInput) > 365) errs.daysLeft = 'Days until expiry must be between 1 and 365.';
        if (!triggerReason.trim() || triggerReason.trim().length < 6) errs.triggerReason = 'Please describe the quality defect or expiry risk (min 6 chars).';
        if (Object.keys(errs).length > 0) return setErrors(errs);

        const qtyNum = Number(qtyAffectedInput);
        const daysNum = Number(daysLeftInput);
        onAddCustomQCAlert({
            id: `QC-${Date.now()}`,
            productName: prod?.name || 'Inspecting Batch Item',
            sku: prod?.sku || 'SKU-QC',
            batchNo: batchNo.trim().toUpperCase(),
            expiryDate: '2026-10-28',
            daysLeft: daysNum,
            qtyAffected: `${qtyNum} ${prod?.uom || 'units'}`,
            triggerReason: triggerReason.trim(),
            aiSuggestion: daysNum <= 5
                ? `Assign store staff to apply 25% flash clearance bundle on ${qtyNum} ${prod?.uom || 'units'} & move to front chiller.`
                : `Assign warehouse staff to quarantine ${batchNo.trim().toUpperCase()} immediately & schedule supplier return pickup.`,
            actionLabel: daysNum <= 5 ? 'Clearance & Relabel' : 'Quarantine & Return',
            savedRM: Number(((prod?.priceRM || 35) * qtyNum * 0.8).toFixed(2)),
            status: 'PENDING_APPROVAL',
        });
        setBatchNo(''); setQtyAffectedInput(''); setDaysLeftInput(''); setTriggerReason(''); setErrors({}); setShowLogForm(false);
    };

    const isCompletedPastDue = (qc: QCAlert) =>
        qc.status === 'APPROVED_RESOLVED' && Boolean(qc.completedLate || qc.hrEscalated || (qc.assignedDueDate && qc.assignedDueDate < (qc.completedAtDate || todayIso)));

    const filteredAlerts = useMemo(() => {
        const q = searchQuery.trim().toLowerCase();
        return qcAlerts.filter((qc) => {
            const isOverdue = (qc.status === 'ASSIGNED_TO_STAFF' && Boolean(qc.assignedDueDate && qc.assignedDueDate < todayIso)) || Boolean(qc.hrEscalated);
            const matchStatus = statusFilter === 'ALL' ? true : statusFilter === 'OVERDUE_HR' ? isOverdue : qc.status === statusFilter;
            const matchQuery = !q || qc.productName.toLowerCase().includes(q) || qc.sku.toLowerCase().includes(q) || qc.batchNo.toLowerCase().includes(q) || qc.triggerReason.toLowerCase().includes(q) || Boolean(qc.assignedStaffName?.toLowerCase().includes(q));
            return matchStatus && matchQuery;
        });
    }, [qcAlerts, statusFilter, searchQuery, todayIso]);

    const overdueCount = qcAlerts.filter((q) => (q.status === 'ASSIGNED_TO_STAFF' && Boolean(q.assignedDueDate && q.assignedDueDate < todayIso)) || Boolean(q.hrEscalated)).length;
    const totalPreventableRM = qcAlerts.reduce((sum, q) => sum + q.savedRM, 0);

    return (
        <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 shadow-xs overflow-hidden space-y-4">
            <div className="px-5 py-4 border-b border-slate-100 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-4">
                <div>
                    <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900 flex items-center gap-2">
                        <Sparkles className="w-5 h-5 text-rose-600" /> Perishable Expiry &amp; Batch Quality Control Repository
                    </h3>
                    <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-0.5">
                        Assign tasks with strict completion due dates to <strong>Orbyte HR Staff</strong>. Tasks left undone past their due date automatically escalate to Orbyte HR.
                    </p>
                </div>

                <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2.5">
                    <div className="hidden sm:flex items-center gap-2 bg-rose-50 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl border border-rose-200 text-xs font-bold text-rose-800">
                        <span>Preventable Waste:</span>
                        <span>{sym} {(totalPreventableRM * exchangeRate).toLocaleString(undefined, { minimumFractionDigits: 2, maximumFractionDigits: 2 })}</span>
                    </div>
                    <button type="button" onClick={() => setShowLogForm(!showLogForm)} className="px-3.5 py-2 bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 text-slate-800 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-1.5 transition cursor-pointer">
                        <Plus className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Log Batch Issue
                    </button>
                    <button
                        type="button" disabled={isScanning}
                        onClick={() => { setIsScanning(true); setTimeout(() => { onTriggerScan(); setIsScanning(false); }, 350); }}
                        className="px-4 py-2 bg-[#2BB656] hover:bg-emerald-600 text-white rounded-xl text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-1.5 shadow-xs transition cursor-pointer"
                    >
                        <RefreshCw className={`w-3.5 h-3.5 ${isScanning ? 'animate-spin' : ''}`} />
                        {isScanning ? 'Scanning Batches...' : '+ Run AI Expiry Scan'}
                    </button>
                </div>
            </div>

            {showLogForm && (
                <form onSubmit={handleCreateCustomAlert} noValidate className="mx-5 p-4 rounded-xl bg-rose-50/50 border border-rose-200 grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-4 gap-3 text-xs">
                    <div>
                        <label className="font-bold text-slate-700 block mb-1">Select Product SKU *</label>
                        <select value={selectedProductId} onChange={(e) => setSelectedProductId(e.target.value)} className="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-lg bg-white border border-slate-300 font-semibold text-slate-900">
                            {products.map((p) => <option key={p.id} value={p.id}>{p.name} ({p.sku})</option>)}
                        </select>
                    </div>
                    <div>
                        <label className="font-bold text-slate-700 block mb-1">Batch / Lot Number *</label>
                        <input type="text" placeholder="e.g., LOT-2026-99" value={batchNo} onChange={(e) => { setBatchNo(e.target.value); if (errors.batchNo) setErrors({ ...errors, batchNo: '' }); }} className={`w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-lg bg-white border ${errors.batchNo ? 'border-rose-500' : 'border-slate-300'}`} />
                        {errors.batchNo && <p className="text-[10px] text-rose-600 font-semibold mt-1 flex items-center gap-1"><AlertCircle className="w-3 h-3" /> {errors.batchNo}</p>}
                    </div>
                    <div>
                        <label className="font-bold text-slate-700 block mb-1">Affected Quantity *</label>
                        <input type="text" inputMode="numeric" placeholder="e.g., 15" value={qtyAffectedInput} onChange={(e) => { setQtyAffectedInput(sanitizePosInt(e.target.value)); if (errors.qtyAffected) setErrors({ ...errors, qtyAffected: '' }); }} className={`w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-lg bg-white border ${errors.qtyAffected ? 'border-rose-500' : 'border-slate-300'}`} />
                        {errors.qtyAffected && <p className="text-[10px] text-rose-600 font-semibold mt-1 flex items-center gap-1"><AlertCircle className="w-3 h-3" /> {errors.qtyAffected}</p>}
                    </div>
                    <div>
                        <label className="font-bold text-slate-700 block mb-1">Days Until Expiry (1â€“365) *</label>
                        <input type="text" inputMode="numeric" placeholder="e.g., 3" value={daysLeftInput} onChange={(e) => { setDaysLeftInput(sanitizePosInt(e.target.value)); if (errors.daysLeft) setErrors({ ...errors, daysLeft: '' }); }} className={`w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-lg bg-white border ${errors.daysLeft ? 'border-rose-500' : 'border-slate-300'}`} />
                        {errors.daysLeft && <p className="text-[10px] text-rose-600 font-semibold mt-1 flex items-center gap-1"><AlertCircle className="w-3 h-3" /> {errors.daysLeft}</p>}
                    </div>
                    <div className="md:col-span-3">
                        <label className="font-bold text-slate-700 block mb-1">Quality Defect / Expiry Trigger Reason *</label>
                        <input type="text" placeholder="e.g., Seal moisture detected or expires in 72 hours" value={triggerReason} onChange={(e) => { setTriggerReason(e.target.value); if (errors.triggerReason) setErrors({ ...errors, triggerReason: '' }); }} className={`w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-lg bg-white border ${errors.triggerReason ? 'border-rose-500' : 'border-slate-300'}`} />
                        {errors.triggerReason && <p className="text-[10px] text-rose-600 font-semibold mt-1 flex items-center gap-1"><AlertCircle className="w-3 h-3" /> {errors.triggerReason}</p>}
                    </div>
                    <div className="flex items-end">
                        <button type="submit" className="w-full py-2 px-4 bg-rose-600 hover:bg-rose-700 text-white font-bold rounded-lg transition cursor-pointer">Add to QC Table</button>
                    </div>
                </form>
            )}

            <div className="px-5 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
                <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center bg-slate-100 p-1 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 text-xs font-semibold">
                    {[
                        { id: 'ALL' as const, label: `All (${qcAlerts.length})`, active: 'bg-white text-slate-900 shadow-2xs font-bold' },
                        { id: 'PENDING_APPROVAL' as const, label: `Needs HR Assignment (${qcAlerts.filter((q) => q.status === 'PENDING_APPROVAL').length})`, active: 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-900 font-bold' },
                        { id: 'ASSIGNED_TO_STAFF' as const, label: `Assigned to HR (${qcAlerts.filter((q) => q.status === 'ASSIGNED_TO_STAFF').length})`, active: 'bg-blue-100 text-blue-900 font-bold' },
                        { id: 'OVERDUE_HR' as const, label: `Overdue Escalated (${overdueCount})`, active: 'bg-rose-600 text-white font-bold', extra: overdueCount > 0 ? 'text-rose-600 font-bold' : '' },
                        { id: 'APPROVED_RESOLVED' as const, label: `Completed (${qcAlerts.filter((q) => q.status === 'APPROVED_RESOLVED').length})`, active: 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-900 font-bold' },
                    ].map((t) => (
                        <button key={t.id} type="button" onClick={() => setStatusFilter(t.id)} className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg transition cursor-pointer ${statusFilter === t.id ? t.active : t.extra || 'text-slate-600'}`}>
                            {t.label}
                        </button>
                    ))}
                </div>

                <div className="relative w-64">
                    <Search className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-400 absolute left-3 top-2.5" />
                    <input type="text" placeholder="Filter batch, SKU or staff..." value={searchQuery} onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)} className="w-full pl-8 pr-3 py-1.5 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-xs focus:outline-none focus:border-emerald-500" />
                </div>
            </div>

            <div className="overflow-x-auto">
                <table className="w-full text-left border-collapse">
                    <thead>
                    <tr className="border-b border-slate-200 bg-slate-50/70 text-[11px] font-semibold text-slate-600 uppercase">
                        <th className="py-3 px-4">Batch &amp; SKU</th>
                        <th className="py-3 px-2.5">Risk Level</th>
                        <th className="py-3 px-2.5">Product &amp; Trigger Reason</th>
                        <th className="py-3 px-2.5">AI Recommendation (View SOP)</th>
                        <th className="py-3 px-2.5">Loss Preventable ({currency})</th>
                        <th className="py-3 px-2.5">Orbyte HR Assignee</th>
                        <th className="py-3 px-2.5">Task Due Date &amp; SLA</th>
                        <th className="py-3 px-4">HR Task Action</th>
                    </tr>
                    </thead>
                    <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-100 text-xs">
                    {filteredAlerts.map((qc) => {
                        const isUrgent = qc.daysLeft <= 5;
                        const isOverdueActive = qc.status === 'ASSIGNED_TO_STAFF' && Boolean(qc.assignedDueDate && qc.assignedDueDate < todayIso);
                        const wasCompletedLate = isCompletedPastDue(qc);

                        return (
                            <tr key={qc.id} className={`transition ${isOverdueActive ? 'bg-rose-50/35 hover:bg-rose-50/60' : 'hover:bg-slate-50/80'}`}>
                                <td className="py-3.5 px-4 whitespace-nowrap">
                                    <p className="font-mono font-bold text-slate-900">{qc.batchNo}</p>
                                    <span className="inline-block mt-0.5 px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-bold bg-slate-100 text-slate-700">{qc.sku} â€¢ {qc.qtyAffected}</span>
                                </td>
                                <td className="py-3.5 px-2.5 whitespace-nowrap">
                    <span className={`px-2 py-1 rounded-md text-[11px] font-bold inline-flex items-center gap-1 ${isUrgent ? 'bg-rose-50 text-rose-700 border border-rose-200' : 'bg-amber-50 text-amber-800 border border-amber-200'}`}>
                      <AlertTriangle className="w-3 h-3" />{isUrgent ? `${qc.daysLeft}d Left (Urgent)` : 'Batch Defect'}
                    </span>
                                </td>
                                <td className="py-3.5 px-2.5 max-w-[190px]">
                                    <p className="font-bold text-slate-900 truncate" title={qc.productName}>{qc.productName}</p>
                                    <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 truncate" title={qc.triggerReason}>{qc.triggerReason}</p>
                                </td>
                                <td className="py-3.5 px-2.5">
                                    <button type="button" onClick={() => openAiRecommendationModal(qc)} className="px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-purple-50 hover:bg-purple-100 border border-purple-200 text-purple-800 font-bold text-xs inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 transition cursor-pointer max-w-[215px]">
                                        <Sparkles className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-purple-600 shrink-0" />
                                        <span className="truncate">{qc.actionLabel}</span>
                                        <span className="bg-purple-200/80 text-purple-900 text-[10px] px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded font-extrabold shrink-0">Click-In</span>
                                    </button>
                                </td>
                                <td className="py-3.5 px-2.5 font-extrabold text-emerald-700 whitespace-nowrap">
                                    {sym} {Number((qc.savedRM * exchangeRate).toFixed(2)).toLocaleString(undefined, { minimumFractionDigits: 2 })}
                                </td>
                                <td className="py-3.5 px-2.5 whitespace-nowrap">
                                    {qc.assignedStaffName ? (
                                        <div>
                        <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-xs font-bold text-blue-800 bg-blue-50 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md border border-blue-200">
                          <UserCheck className="w-3 h-3 text-blue-600" /> {qc.assignedStaffName}
                        </span>
                                            <p className="text-[10px] font-mono text-slate-400 mt-0.5">{qc.hrTaskId} â€¢ {qc.assignedPriority}</p>
                                        </div>
                                    ) : (
                                        <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[11px] font-semibold bg-slate-100 text-slate-500">Unassigned (HR Roster)</span>
                                    )}
                                </td>
                                <td className="py-3.5 px-2.5 whitespace-nowrap">
                                    {qc.assignedDueDate ? (
                                        <div className="space-y-1">
                                            <div className="flex items-center gap-1 font-mono text-xs font-bold text-slate-800">
                                                <Calendar className="w-3 h-3 text-slate-400" /><span>{qc.assignedDueDate}</span>
                                            </div>
                                            {qc.status === 'APPROVED_RESOLVED' ? (
                                                wasCompletedLate ? (
                                                    <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
                                                        <span className="inline-block px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-bold bg-amber-100 text-amber-900 border border-amber-300">Completed Late (Past Due)</span>
                                                        {qc.hrEscalationPayload && (
                                                            <button type="button" onClick={() => setInspectingPayload(qc.hrEscalationPayload!)} className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-bold bg-slate-900 hover:bg-slate-700 text-emerald-300 inline-flex items-center gap-1 cursor-pointer">
                                                                <FileCode className="w-2.5 h-2.5" /> Payload
                                                            </button>
                                                        )}
                                                    </div>
                                                ) : (
                                                    <span className="inline-block px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-bold bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 border border-emerald-200">Completed On Time</span>
                                                )
                                            ) : isOverdueActive ? (
                                                <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
                                                    <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-extrabold bg-rose-600 text-white">OVERDUE â€¢ HR ESCALATED</span>
                                                    {qc.hrEscalationPayload && (
                                                        <button type="button" onClick={() => setInspectingPayload(qc.hrEscalationPayload!)} className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-bold bg-slate-900 hover:bg-slate-700 text-emerald-300 inline-flex items-center gap-1 cursor-pointer">
                                                            <FileCode className="w-2.5 h-2.5" /> Payload
                                                        </button>
                                                    )}
                                                </div>
                                            ) : (
                                                <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                                                    <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-bold bg-blue-50 text-blue-700 border border-blue-200">On Track</span>
                                                    <button type="button" onClick={() => handleSimulatePastDueAndEscalate(qc)} className="text-[10px] text-rose-600 hover:underline font-semibold cursor-pointer">Simulate Overdue</button>
                                                </div>
                                            )}
                                        </div>
                                    ) : (
                                        <span className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 italic">No due date set</span>
                                    )}
                                </td>
                                <td className="py-3.5 px-4 whitespace-nowrap">
                                    {qc.status === 'PENDING_APPROVAL' && (
                                        <button type="button" onClick={() => openAiRecommendationModal(qc)} className="px-3 py-1.5 bg-amber-400 hover:bg-amber-500 text-slate-900 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 transition cursor-pointer">
                                            <Users className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Assign HR Staff
                                        </button>
                                    )}
                                    {qc.status === 'ASSIGNED_TO_STAFF' && (
                                        <button type="button" onClick={() => onCompleteHrTask(qc.id)} className="px-3 py-1.5 bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-700 text-white rounded-xl text-xs font-bold inline-flex items-center gap-1 transition cursor-pointer">
                                            <CheckCircle2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Mark Done
                                        </button>
                                    )}
                                    {qc.status === 'APPROVED_RESOLVED' && (
                                        <span className={`px-3 py-1 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold inline-flex items-center gap-1 ${wasCompletedLate ? 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-900 border border-amber-300' : 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800'}`}>
                        <CheckCircle2 className={`w-3.5 h-3.5 ${wasCompletedLate ? 'text-amber-700' : 'text-emerald-600'}`} />
                                            {wasCompletedLate ? 'Resolved (Late)' : 'Resolved by HR'}
                      </span>
                                    )}
                                </td>
                            </tr>
                        );
                    })}
                    </tbody>
                </table>
            </div>

            {selectedQcModal && (
                <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-slate-900/60 backdrop-blur-xs flex items-center justify-center p-4 z-50">
                    <form onSubmit={handleConfirmHrDispatch} noValidate className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 max-w-lg w-full p-6 shadow-2xl space-y-4">
                        <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3 border-b border-slate-100 pb-3">
                            <div>
                                <span className="px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold bg-purple-100 text-purple-800 uppercase">Orbyte HR System Connector</span>
                                <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900 mt-1">{selectedQcModal.productName} ({selectedQcModal.batchNo})</h3>
                                <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">SKU: {selectedQcModal.sku} â€¢ Affected: {selectedQcModal.qtyAffected} â€¢ Expiry: {selectedQcModal.expiryDate}</p>
                            </div>
                            <button type="button" onClick={() => setSelectedQcModal(null)} className="p-1 text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-700 cursor-pointer"><X className="w-4 h-4" /></button>
                        </div>

                        <div className="p-4 rounded-xl bg-purple-50/70 border border-purple-200 space-y-2 text-xs">
                            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                                <span className="font-bold text-purple-900 flex items-center gap-1.5"><Sparkles className="w-4 h-4 text-purple-600" /> AI Root-Cause &amp; Recommended SOP</span>
                                <span className="font-extrabold text-emerald-700">Saves {sym} {(selectedQcModal.savedRM * exchangeRate).toFixed(2)}</span>
                            </div>
                            <p className="text-slate-700 leading-relaxed">{selectedQcModal.aiSuggestion}</p>
                            <p className="text-[11px] text-rose-600 font-semibold">Trigger: {selectedQcModal.triggerReason}</p>
                        </div>

                        <div className="space-y-3 text-xs">
                            <div>
                                <label className="font-bold text-slate-700 block mb-1">Assign to Orbyte HR Staff Member *</label>
                                <select value={selectedStaffId} onChange={(e) => setSelectedStaffId(e.target.value)} className="w-full px-3 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-white border border-slate-300 font-semibold text-slate-900 focus:outline-none focus:border-emerald-500">
                                    {ORBYTE_HR_STAFF_ROSTER.map((st) => <option key={st.staffId} value={st.staffId}>{st.name} ({st.staffId}) â€” {st.position} [{st.department}]</option>)}
                                </select>
                            </div>

                            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                                <div>
                                    <label className="font-bold text-slate-700 block mb-1">Task Priority</label>
                                    <select value={taskPriority} onChange={(e) => setTaskPriority(e.target.value as 'URGENT' | 'HIGH' | 'NORMAL')} className="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-white border border-slate-300 font-bold text-slate-900">
                                        <option value="URGENT">URGENT (Execute &lt; 2 hrs)</option>
                                        <option value="HIGH">HIGH (Same Shift)</option>
                                        <option value="NORMAL">NORMAL (Within 24 hrs)</option>
                                    </select>
                                </div>
                                <div>
                                    <label className="font-bold text-slate-700 flex items-center gap-1 mb-1"><Clock className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-rose-600" /> Completion Due Date *</label>
                                    <input type="date" value={taskDueDate} onChange={(e) => { setTaskDueDate(e.target.value); setDueDateError(''); }} className={`w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-white border font-mono font-bold text-slate-900 focus:outline-none ${dueDateError ? 'border-rose-500' : 'border-slate-300 focus:border-emerald-500'}`} />
                                    {dueDateError && <p className="text-[10px] text-rose-600 font-semibold mt-1">{dueDateError}</p>}
                                </div>
                            </div>

                            <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 bg-slate-50 px-3 py-2 rounded-lg border border-slate-200">
                                <strong>SLA Governance:</strong> If the assigned staff member leaves this task undone past the due date, Vyte automatically dispatches an overdue escalation payload to Orbyte&apos;s HR system.
                            </p>

                            <div>
                                <label className="font-bold text-slate-700 block mb-1">Action Instructions for Staff (Editable)</label>
                                <textarea rows={2} value={taskInstruction} onChange={(e) => setTaskInstruction(e.target.value)} className="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-white border border-slate-300 text-slate-800 focus:outline-none focus:border-emerald-500" />
                            </div>
                        </div>

                        <div className="flex justify-end gap-2 pt-3 border-t border-slate-100">
                            <button type="button" onClick={() => setSelectedQcModal(null)} className="px-4 py-2 bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 text-slate-700 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold cursor-pointer">Cancel</button>
                            <button type="submit" disabled={!canApprove} className="px-4 py-2 bg-[#2BB656] hover:bg-emerald-600 disabled:opacity-50 text-white rounded-xl text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-1.5 shadow-xs cursor-pointer">
                                <Send className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />{canApprove ? 'Approve & Dispatch to Orbyte HR Staff' : 'Requires Owner/Manager Role'}
                            </button>
                        </div>
                    </form>
                </div>
            )}

            {inspectingPayload && (
                <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-slate-900/65 backdrop-blur-xs flex items-center justify-center p-4 z-50">
                    <div className="bg-[#14181D] text-slate-100 rounded-2xl border border-slate-700 max-w-xl w-full p-6 shadow-2xl space-y-4">
                        <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3 border-b border-slate-800 pb-3">
                            <div>
                                <span className="px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold bg-rose-500/20 text-rose-300 border border-rose-500/40 uppercase">POST /api/orbyte-hr/overdue-escalation</span>
                                <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-white mt-1.5">Orbyte HR Overdue Task Escalation Payload</h3>
                                <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">
                                    Automatically dispatched to Orbyte HR because task <strong>{inspectingPayload.hrTaskId}</strong> remained undone past its due date (<strong>{inspectingPayload.taskDetails.assignedDueDate}</strong>).
                                </p>
                            </div>
                            <button type="button" onClick={() => setInspectingPayload(null)} className="p-1 text-slate-400 hover:text-white cursor-pointer"><X className="w-4 h-4" /></button>
                        </div>

                        <div className="relative">
                            <button
                                type="button"
                                onClick={() => { void navigator.clipboard.writeText(JSON.stringify(inspectingPayload, null, 2)); setCopiedPayload(true); setTimeout(() => setCopiedPayload(false), 2000); }}
                                className="absolute right-3 top-3 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-xs font-semibold text-emerald-400 flex items-center gap-1 cursor-pointer border border-slate-700"
                            >
                                {copiedPayload ? <><Check className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Copied</> : <><Copy className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Copy JSON</>}
                            </button>
                            <pre className="p-4 rounded-xl bg-[#0B0C0E] border border-slate-800 text-[11px] font-mono text-emerald-300 overflow-x-auto max-h-80 leading-relaxed">
                {JSON.stringify(inspectingPayload, null, 2)}
              </pre>
                        </div>

                        <div className="flex justify-end pt-2 border-t border-slate-800">
                            <button type="button" onClick={() => setInspectingPayload(null)} className="px-4 py-2 bg-[#2BB656] hover:bg-emerald-500 text-slate-950 font-bold text-xs rounded-xl cursor-pointer">Close Payload Inspector</button>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            )}
        </div>
    );
};

// src/modules/module5-invoice/InvoiceModule.tsx
import React, { useState, useRef } from 'react';
import {
    Plus, CheckCircle2, FileText, Check, AlertCircle, Sparkles,
    ArrowUpRight, ArrowDownLeft, X, Camera, FileSpreadsheet, Upload,
} from 'lucide-react';
import type { CurrencyCode, InvoiceItem, UserRole } from '../../types/vyte.ts';
import { CURRENCY_SYMBOLS } from '../../types/vyte.ts';
import { getDebugMockMode, orbyteApi } from "../../services/api.ts";

interface InvoiceModuleProps {
    invoices: InvoiceItem[];
    role: UserRole;
    currency?: CurrencyCode;
    exchangeRate?: number;
    onVerifyInvoice: (invId: string, updatedCategory?: string) => void;
    onToggleInvoicePaid: (invId: string) => void;
    onAddValidatedInvoice: (createdInv: InvoiceItem) => void;
    onBatchImportInvoices?: (importedInvoices: InvoiceItem[]) => void;
}

const ACCOUNTING_CATEGORIES = [
    'Inventory Procurement (Raw Materials)',
    'Packaging & Sustainable Supplies',
    'Cold-Chain Logistics & Freight',
    'Wholesale B2B Product Sales',
    'Direct Product Sales Revenue',
    'Store Utilities & Equipment Maintenance',
];

const sanitizeDecimal = (raw: string): string => {
    const parts = raw.replace(/[^0-9.]/g, '').split('.');
    const intPart = parts[0].replace(/^0+/, '').slice(0, 7);
    return parts.length > 1 ? `${intPart || '0'}.${parts[1].slice(0, 2)}` : intPart;
};

export const InvoiceModule: React.FC<InvoiceModuleProps> = ({
                                                                invoices, role, currency = 'MYR', exchangeRate = 1.0,
                                                                onVerifyInvoice, onToggleInvoicePaid, onAddValidatedInvoice, onBatchImportInvoices,
                                                            }) => {
    const sym = CURRENCY_SYMBOLS[currency] || 'RM';
    const canApprove = role === 'SME_OWNER' || role === 'MANAGER';

    const [directionFilter, setDirectionFilter] = useState<'ALL' | 'SUPPLIER_PAYABLE' | 'CUSTOMER_RECEIVABLE'>('ALL');
    const [statusFilter, setStatusFilter] = useState<'ALL' | 'NEEDS_REVIEW' | 'VERIFIED'>('ALL');
    const [selectedCategories, setSelectedCategories] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
    const [showUploadForm, setShowUploadForm] = useState(false);

    const photoInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
    const excelInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
    const [photoPreviewUrl, setPhotoPreviewUrl] = useState<string | null>(null);
    const [uploadedFileName, setUploadedFileName] = useState<string | null>(null);

    const [invNo, setInvNo] = useState('');
    const [invType, setInvType] = useState<'SUPPLIER_PAYABLE' | 'CUSTOMER_RECEIVABLE'>('SUPPLIER_PAYABLE');
    const [partyName, setPartyName] = useState('');
    const [amountInput, setAmountInput] = useState('');
    const [rawLineDescription, setRawLineDescription] = useState('');
    const [formErrors, setFormErrors] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});

    const filtered = invoices.filter((i) => (directionFilter === 'ALL' || i.type === directionFilter) && (statusFilter === 'ALL' || i.status === statusFilter));
    const totalPayablesRM = invoices.filter((i) => i.type === 'SUPPLIER_PAYABLE').reduce((s, i) => s + i.amountRM, 0);
    const totalReceivablesRM = invoices.filter((i) => i.type === 'CUSTOMER_RECEIVABLE').reduce((s, i) => s + i.amountRM, 0);

    const handleSendPaymentReminder = (inv: InvoiceItem, lang: 'EN' | 'BM' | 'ZH' = 'EN') => {
        const amountFormatted = `${sym} ${(inv.amountRM * exchangeRate).toFixed(2)}`;
        const messages = {
            EN: `Hi ${inv.party}, friendly reminder from Vyte Billing that Invoice ${inv.invNo} (${amountFormatted}) dated ${inv.date} is currently due. Pay instantly via DuitNow/FPX here: https://pay.vyte.my/${inv.invNo}`,
            BM: `Salam ${inv.party}, peringatan mesra bahawa Invois ${inv.invNo} (${amountFormatted}) bertarikh ${inv.date} masih belum dijelaskan. Sila buat bayaran melalui DuitNow/FPX: https://pay.vyte.my/${inv.invNo}`,
            ZH: `${inv.party} æ‚¨å¥½ï¼Œæ¸©é¦¨æé†’æ‚¨è´¦å• ${inv.invNo}ï¼ˆé‡‘é¢ï¼š${amountFormatted}ï¼Œæ—¥æœŸï¼š${inv.date}ï¼‰ç›®å‰å·²åˆ°æœŸã€‚æ‚¨å¯é€šè¿‡ DuitNow/FPX ä¸€é”®å®Œæˆä»˜æ¬¾ï¼šhttps://pay.vyte.my/${inv.invNo}`,
        };

        const whatsappUrl = `https://wa.me/60123456789?text=${encodeURIComponent(messages[lang])}`;
        window.open(whatsappUrl, '_blank', 'noopener,noreferrer');
    };

    const getSourceBadge = (inv: InvoiceItem) => {
        if (inv.invNo.startsWith('SUP-')) return { label: 'Auto-Restock PO', color: 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-800' };
        if (inv.invNo.startsWith('SALE-')) return { label: 'Direct Counter Sale', color: 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800' };
        if (inv.invNo.startsWith('CUST-')) return { label: 'NLP Customer Order', color: 'bg-blue-100 text-blue-800' };
        if (inv.attachmentName?.endsWith('.csv') || inv.attachmentName?.endsWith('.xlsx')) return { label: `Excel: ${inv.attachmentName}`, color: 'bg-teal-100 text-teal-800' };
        if (inv.attachmentPreviewUrl) return { label: 'Photo OCR Extracted', color: 'bg-purple-100 text-purple-800' };
        return { label: 'Uploaded Invoice', color: 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-700' };
    };

    const handlePhotoUpload = async (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
        const file = e.target.files?.[0];
        if (!file) return;
        if (!getDebugMockMode()) {
            try {
                const created = await orbyteApi.uploadInvoiceFileOrText({ file, userID: 'M067' });
                if (created) {
                    onAddValidatedInvoice(created);
                    e.target.value = '';
                    return;
                }
            } catch (err) {
                console.error(err);
            }
        }
        const reader = new FileReader();
        reader.onload = () => {
            setPhotoPreviewUrl(String(reader.result));
            setUploadedFileName(file.name);
            setShowUploadForm(true);
            if (!invNo) setInvNo(`SUP-OCR-${Math.floor(1000 + Math.random() * 9000)}`);
            if (!partyName) setPartyName('Selangor Cold-Storage & Packaging Sdn Bhd');
            if (!amountInput) setAmountInput((780 * exchangeRate).toFixed(2));
            if (!rawLineDescription) setRawLineDescription(`AI OCR extracted from photo "${file.name}": 25 boxes insulated packaging & cold-chain delivery`);
            setFormErrors({});
        };
        reader.readAsDataURL(file);
    };

    const handleExcelSpreadsheetUpload = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
        const file = e.target.files?.[0];
        if (!file) return;
        const reader = new FileReader();
        reader.onload = () => {
            const lines = String(reader.result || '').split(/\r?\n/).map((l) => l.trim()).filter(Boolean);
            const imported: InvoiceItem[] = [];
            lines.forEach((line, idx) => {
                if (idx === 0 && line.toLowerCase().includes('invoice')) return;
                const cols = line.split(',').map((c) => c.trim().replace(/^"|"$/g, ''));
                if (cols.length >= 3) {
                    imported.push({
                        id: `INV-XLS-${Date.now()}-${idx}`,
                        invNo: cols[0] || `XLS-2026-${100 + idx}`,
                        type: cols[1]?.toUpperCase() === 'CUSTOMER_RECEIVABLE' ? 'CUSTOMER_RECEIVABLE' : 'SUPPLIER_PAYABLE',
                        party: cols[2] || 'Spreadsheet Imported Partner',
                        date: '22 Oct 2026',
                        amountRM: Number(cols[3]) || 650.0,
                        aiCategory: cols[4] || 'Inventory Procurement (Raw Materials)',
                        aiConfidence: 96, paid: false, status: 'NEEDS_REVIEW', attachmentName: file.name,
                    });
                }
            });
            if (!imported.length) {
                imported.push(
                    { id: `INV-XLS-${Date.now()}-1`, invNo: `XLS-2026-${Math.floor(1000 + Math.random() * 9000)}`, type: 'SUPPLIER_PAYABLE', party: 'Klang Valley Roasters Sdn Bhd', date: '22 Oct 2026', amountRM: 1480.0, aiCategory: 'Inventory Procurement (Raw Materials)', aiConfidence: 97, paid: false, status: 'NEEDS_REVIEW', attachmentName: file.name },
                    { id: `INV-XLS-${Date.now()}-2`, invNo: `XLS-2026-${Math.floor(1000 + Math.random() * 9000)}`, type: 'CUSTOMER_RECEIVABLE', party: 'PJ Specialty Coffee Roasters', date: '22 Oct 2026', amountRM: 920.0, aiCategory: 'Wholesale B2B Product Sales', aiConfidence: 95, paid: true, status: 'NEEDS_REVIEW', attachmentName: file.name }
                );
            }
            if (onBatchImportInvoices) onBatchImportInvoices(imported);
            else imported.forEach(onAddValidatedInvoice);
            e.target.value = '';
        };
        reader.readAsText(file);
    };

    const handleUploadSubmit = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        const errs: Record<string, string> = {};
        if (!invNo.trim() || !/^[A-Za-z0-9-_]{4,20}$/.test(invNo.trim())) errs.invNo = 'Invoice No. must be 4â€“20 alphanumeric chars (e.g., SUP-2026-9901).';
        if (!partyName.trim() || partyName.trim().length < 3) errs.partyName = 'Counterparty business name is required (min 3 chars).';
        const amt = Number(amountInput);
        if (!amountInput || isNaN(amt) || amt <= 0 || amt > 1000000) errs.amountInput = `Enter a valid amount between ${sym} 0.01 and 1,000,000.`;
        if (!rawLineDescription.trim() || rawLineDescription.trim().length < 6) errs.rawLineDescription = 'Describe invoice items (min 6 chars) for AI classification.';
        if (Object.keys(errs).length > 0) return setFormErrors(errs);

        const lower = rawLineDescription.toLowerCase();
        const aiCategory = /box|pack|cup/.test(lower)
            ? 'Packaging & Sustainable Supplies'
            : /delivery|freight|cold/.test(lower)
                ? 'Cold-Chain Logistics & Freight'
                : /electric|chiller|repair/.test(lower)
                    ? 'Store Utilities & Equipment Maintenance'
                    : invType === 'SUPPLIER_PAYABLE' ? 'Inventory Procurement (Raw Materials)' : 'Wholesale B2B Product Sales';

        onAddValidatedInvoice({
            id: `INV-${Date.now()}`, invNo: invNo.trim().toUpperCase(), type: invType, party: partyName.trim(),
            date: '22 Oct 2026', amountRM: Number((amt / exchangeRate).toFixed(2)), aiCategory, aiConfidence: 96,
            paid: false, status: 'NEEDS_REVIEW', attachmentName: uploadedFileName || undefined, attachmentPreviewUrl: photoPreviewUrl || undefined,
        });
        setInvNo(''); setPartyName(''); setAmountInput(''); setRawLineDescription(''); setPhotoPreviewUrl(null); setUploadedFileName(null); setFormErrors({}); setShowUploadForm(false);
    };

    return (
        <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 shadow-xs overflow-hidden space-y-4">
            <input ref={photoInputRef} type="file" accept="image/*" onChange={handlePhotoUpload} className="hidden" />
            <input ref={excelInputRef} type="file" accept=".csv,.xlsx,.xls" onChange={handleExcelSpreadsheetUpload} className="hidden" />

            <div className="px-5 py-4 border-b border-slate-100 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-4">
                <div>
                    <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900 flex items-center gap-2"><FileText className="w-5 h-5 text-emerald-600" /> Supplier &amp; Customer Smart Invoice Repository</h3>
                    <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-0.5">Automatically gathers supplier bills &amp; customer invoices across Auto Restock and Customer Orders, plus supports Photo OCR &amp; Excel/CSV batch import.</p>
                </div>

                <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2.5">
                    <div className="hidden xl:flex items-center gap-3 bg-slate-50 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 text-xs">
                        <span className="flex items-center gap-1 text-amber-700 font-bold"><ArrowUpRight className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Payables: {sym} {(totalPayablesRM * exchangeRate).toLocaleString(undefined, { minimumFractionDigits: 2, maximumFractionDigits: 2 })}</span>
                        <span className="text-slate-300">|</span>
                        <span className="flex items-center gap-1 text-blue-700 font-bold"><ArrowDownLeft className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Receivables: {sym} {(totalReceivablesRM * exchangeRate).toLocaleString(undefined, { minimumFractionDigits: 2, maximumFractionDigits: 2 })}</span>
                    </div>
                    <button type="button" onClick={() => photoInputRef.current?.click()} className="px-3 py-2 bg-purple-50 hover:bg-purple-100 text-purple-800 border border-purple-200 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-1.5 transition cursor-pointer">
                        <Camera className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-purple-600" /> Upload Receipt Photo
                    </button>
                    <button type="button" onClick={() => excelInputRef.current?.click()} className="px-3 py-2 bg-teal-50 hover:bg-teal-100 text-teal-800 border border-teal-200 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-1.5 transition cursor-pointer">
                        <FileSpreadsheet className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-teal-600" /> Import Excel / CSV
                    </button>
                    <button type="button" onClick={() => setShowUploadForm((p) => !p)} className="px-3.5 py-2 bg-[#2BB656] hover:bg-emerald-600 text-white rounded-xl text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-1.5 shadow-xs transition cursor-pointer">
                        {showUploadForm ? <X className="w-4 h-4" /> : <Plus className="w-4 h-4" />}{showUploadForm ? 'Close Form' : 'Manual / AI Parse Invoice'}
                    </button>
                </div>
            </div>

            {showUploadForm && (
                <form onSubmit={handleUploadSubmit} noValidate className="mx-5 p-4 rounded-xl bg-emerald-50/60 border border-emerald-200 grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-4 gap-3 text-xs">
                    {photoPreviewUrl && (
                        <div className="md:col-span-4 flex items-center justify-between bg-white p-3 rounded-xl border border-purple-200">
                            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                                <img src={photoPreviewUrl} alt="Receipt preview" className="w-12 h-12 object-cover rounded-lg border border-slate-200" />
                                <div>
                                    <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-purple-100 text-purple-800 font-bold text-[10px]">AI Vision OCR Active</span>
                                    <p className="font-bold text-slate-800 mt-0.5">{uploadedFileName}</p>
                                    <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-500">Fields below were auto-populated from your receipt image. Review and click Save.</p>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                            <button type="button" onClick={() => { setPhotoPreviewUrl(null); setUploadedFileName(null); }} className="text-slate-400 hover:text-rose-600 p-1 cursor-pointer"><X className="w-4 h-4" /></button>
                        </div>
                    )}

                    <div>
                        <label className="font-bold text-slate-700 block mb-1">Invoice Number *</label>
                        <input type="text" maxLength={20} placeholder="e.g., SUP-2026-9920" value={invNo} onChange={(e) => { setInvNo(e.target.value); if (formErrors.invNo) setFormErrors({ ...formErrors, invNo: '' }); }} className={`w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-lg bg-white border ${formErrors.invNo ? 'border-rose-500' : 'border-slate-300'}`} />
                        {formErrors.invNo && <p className="text-[10px] text-rose-600 font-semibold mt-1 flex items-center gap-1"><AlertCircle className="w-3 h-3" /> {formErrors.invNo}</p>}
                    </div>
                    <div>
                        <label className="font-bold text-slate-700 block mb-1">Invoice Direction *</label>
                        <select value={invType} onChange={(e) => setInvType(e.target.value as 'SUPPLIER_PAYABLE' | 'CUSTOMER_RECEIVABLE')} className="w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-lg bg-white border border-slate-300 font-semibold text-slate-900">
                            <option value="SUPPLIER_PAYABLE">Supplier Bill (Payable)</option>
                            <option value="CUSTOMER_RECEIVABLE">Customer Invoice (Receivable)</option>
                        </select>
                    </div>
                    <div>
                        <label className="font-bold text-slate-700 block mb-1">Counterparty Name *</label>
                        <input type="text" maxLength={60} placeholder="e.g., EcoPack Malaysia Sdn Bhd" value={partyName} onChange={(e) => { setPartyName(e.target.value); if (formErrors.partyName) setFormErrors({ ...formErrors, partyName: '' }); }} className={`w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-lg bg-white border ${formErrors.partyName ? 'border-rose-500' : 'border-slate-300'}`} />
                        {formErrors.partyName && <p className="text-[10px] text-rose-600 font-semibold mt-1 flex items-center gap-1"><AlertCircle className="w-3 h-3" /> {formErrors.partyName}</p>}
                    </div>
                    <div>
                        <label className="font-bold text-slate-700 block mb-1">Total Amount ({currency}) *</label>
                        <input type="text" inputMode="decimal" placeholder="e.g., 1350.00" value={amountInput} onChange={(e) => { setAmountInput(sanitizeDecimal(e.target.value)); if (formErrors.amountInput) setFormErrors({ ...formErrors, amountInput: '' }); }} className={`w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-lg bg-white border ${formErrors.amountInput ? 'border-rose-500' : 'border-slate-300'}`} />
                        {formErrors.amountInput && <p className="text-[10px] text-rose-600 font-semibold mt-1 flex items-center gap-1"><AlertCircle className="w-3 h-3" /> {formErrors.amountInput}</p>}
                    </div>
                    <div className="md:col-span-3">
                        <label className="font-bold text-slate-700 block mb-1">Invoice Line Items / Description (Used by AI to Classify Category) *</label>
                        <input type="text" maxLength={180} placeholder="e.g., 30 boxes of biodegradable takeaway packaging & kraft paper cups" value={rawLineDescription} onChange={(e) => { setRawLineDescription(e.target.value); if (formErrors.rawLineDescription) setFormErrors({ ...formErrors, rawLineDescription: '' }); }} className={`w-full px-3 py-2 rounded-lg bg-white border ${formErrors.rawLineDescription ? 'border-rose-500' : 'border-slate-300'}`} />
                        {formErrors.rawLineDescription && <p className="text-[10px] text-rose-600 font-semibold mt-1 flex items-center gap-1"><AlertCircle className="w-3 h-3" /> {formErrors.rawLineDescription}</p>}
                    </div>
                    <div className="flex items-end gap-2">
                        <button type="button" onClick={() => photoInputRef.current?.click()} className="py-2 px-3 bg-white hover:bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 border border-slate-300 rounded-lg font-bold flex items-center gap-1 cursor-pointer"><Upload className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Photo</button>
                        <button type="submit" className="flex-1 py-2 px-4 bg-[#2BB656] hover:bg-emerald-600 text-white font-bold rounded-lg flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 transition cursor-pointer"><Sparkles className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Save &amp; AI Classify</button>
                    </div>
                </form>
            )}

            <div className="px-5 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
                <div className="flex items-center bg-slate-100 p-1 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 text-xs font-semibold">
                    {[
                        { id: 'ALL' as const, label: `All (${invoices.length})`, active: 'bg-white text-slate-900 shadow-2xs font-bold' },
                        { id: 'SUPPLIER_PAYABLE' as const, label: `Supplier Bills (${invoices.filter((i) => i.type === 'SUPPLIER_PAYABLE').length})`, active: 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-900 font-bold' },
                        { id: 'CUSTOMER_RECEIVABLE' as const, label: `Customer Invoices (${invoices.filter((i) => i.type === 'CUSTOMER_RECEIVABLE').length})`, active: 'bg-blue-100 text-blue-900 font-bold' },
                    ].map((t) => (
                        <button key={t.id} type="button" onClick={() => setDirectionFilter(t.id)} className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg transition cursor-pointer ${directionFilter === t.id ? t.active : 'text-slate-600'}`}>{t.label}</button>
                    ))}
                </div>

                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs">
                    <span className="text-slate-500 font-medium">Filter:</span>
                    <button type="button" onClick={() => setStatusFilter(statusFilter === 'NEEDS_REVIEW' ? 'ALL' : 'NEEDS_REVIEW')} className={`px-3 py-1 rounded-lg border font-semibold transition cursor-pointer ${statusFilter === 'NEEDS_REVIEW' ? 'bg-amber-500 text-white border-amber-500' : 'bg-slate-50 text-slate-600 border-slate-200'}`}>
                        Pending Review ({invoices.filter((i) => i.status === 'NEEDS_REVIEW').length})
                    </button>
                </div>
            </div>

            <div className="overflow-x-auto">
                <table className="w-full text-left border-collapse">
                    <thead>
                    <tr className="border-b border-slate-200 bg-slate-50/70 text-[11px] font-semibold text-slate-600 uppercase">
                        <th className="py-3 px-4">Invoice &amp; Source</th>
                        <th className="py-3 px-3">Direction</th>
                        <th className="py-3 px-3">Counterparty Name</th>
                        <th className="py-3 px-3">AI Categorization &amp; Confidence</th>
                        <th className="py-3 px-3">E-Invoice</th>
                        <th className="py-3 px-3">Total ({currency})</th>
                        <th className="py-3 px-3">Payment (Click to Toggle)</th>
                        <th className="py-3 px-4">Action &amp; Verification</th>
                    </tr>
                    </thead>
                    <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-100 text-xs">
                    {filtered.map((inv) => {
                        const source = getSourceBadge(inv);
                        const activeCategory = selectedCategories[inv.id] ?? inv.aiCategory;
                        return (
                            <tr key={inv.id} className="hover:bg-slate-50/80 transition">
                                <td className="py-3 px-4 whitespace-nowrap">
                                    <p className="font-mono font-bold text-slate-900">{inv.invNo}</p>
                                    <span className={`inline-block mt-0.5 px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-bold ${source.color}`}>{source.label}</span>
                                </td>
                                <td className="py-3 px-3 whitespace-nowrap">
                                    <span className={`px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md text-[11px] font-semibold ${inv.type === 'SUPPLIER_PAYABLE' ? 'bg-amber-50 text-amber-800' : 'bg-blue-50 text-blue-800'}`}>
                                      {inv.type === 'SUPPLIER_PAYABLE' ? 'Supplier Bill' : 'Customer Sales'}
                                    </span>
                                </td>
                                <td className="py-3 px-3 font-semibold text-slate-900 max-w-[200px] truncate">
                                    <p className="truncate">{inv.party}</p><p className="text-[10px] text-slate-400">{inv.date}</p>
                                </td>
                                <td className="py-3 px-3">
                                    <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                                        {inv.status === 'NEEDS_REVIEW' ? (
                                            <select value={activeCategory} onChange={(e) => setSelectedCategories({ ...selectedCategories, [inv.id]: e.target.value })} className="max-w-[230px] px-2 py-1 rounded-lg bg-purple-50/70 border border-purple-200 font-semibold text-purple-800 text-xs focus:outline-none">
                                                <option value={inv.aiCategory}>{inv.aiCategory}</option>
                                                {ACCOUNTING_CATEGORIES.filter((c) => c !== inv.aiCategory).map((cat) => <option key={cat} value={cat}>{cat}</option>)}
                                            </select>
                                        ) : (
                                            <span className="font-semibold text-purple-700">{inv.aiCategory}</span>
                                        )}
                                        <span className="bg-purple-100 text-purple-800 text-[10px] px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded font-bold shrink-0">{inv.aiConfidence}% AI</span>
                                    </div>
                                </td>
                                <td className="py-3 px-3 whitespace-nowrap">
                                    <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-[10px] font-bold text-emerald-700 bg-emerald-50 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md border border-emerald-200"><Check className="w-3 h-3" /> Ready</span>
                                </td>
                                <td className="py-3 px-3 font-bold text-slate-900 whitespace-nowrap">
                                    {sym} {(inv.amountRM * exchangeRate).toLocaleString(undefined, { minimumFractionDigits: 2, maximumFractionDigits: 2 })}
                                </td>
                                <td className="py-3 px-3 whitespace-nowrap">
                                    <button type="button" onClick={() => onToggleInvoicePaid(inv.id)} className={`px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg text-[11px] font-bold cursor-pointer border transition ${inv.paid ? 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800 border-emerald-300 hover:bg-emerald-200' : 'bg-rose-50 text-rose-700 border-rose-200 hover:bg-rose-100'}`}>
                                        {inv.paid ? 'Paid âœ“ (Toggle)' : 'Unpaid (Mark Paid)'}
                                    </button>
                                </td>
                                <td className="py-3 px-4 whitespace-nowrap flex items-center gap-2">
                                    {inv.status === 'NEEDS_REVIEW' ? (
                                        <button type="button" onClick={() => onVerifyInvoice(inv.id, activeCategory)} className="px-3 py-1.5 bg-amber-400 hover:bg-amber-500 text-slate-900 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold transition cursor-pointer">
                                            {canApprove ? 'Confirm Category' : 'Owner/Mgr Only'}
                                        </button>
                                    ) : (
                                        <span className="px-2.5 py-1 bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold inline-flex items-center gap-1"><CheckCircle2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-600" /> Verified</span>
                                    )}

                                    {inv.type === 'CUSTOMER_RECEIVABLE' && !inv.paid && (
                                        <button
                                            type="button"
                                            onClick={() => handleSendPaymentReminder(inv, 'EN')}
                                            className="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg bg-amber-500 hover:bg-amber-600 text-white text-[11px] font-bold inline-flex items-center gap-1 shadow-2xs cursor-pointer"
                                        >
                                            Remind Customer (WhatsApp)
                                        </button>
                                    )}
                                </td>
                            </tr>
                        );
                    })}
                    </tbody>
                </table>
            </div>
        </div>
    );
};

import {CURRENCY_SYMBOLS} from '../../types/vyte.ts';

interface ComplianceRenewalModuleProps {
    profiles: BusinessComplianceProfile[];
    setProfiles: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<BusinessComplianceProfile[]>>;
    vaultDocs: DocumentVaultItem[];

    setRenewalTasks: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<RenewalTask[]>>;
